<div class="col-sm order-1">
      <h2 class="chapter_title">CHAPTER 245C. HUMAN SERVICES BACKGROUND STUDIES</h2>
      
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.01">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.01 TITLE.</h1>
        <p>This chapter may be cited as the "Department of Human Services Background Studies Act."</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.01.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.1.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.02">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.02 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>The definitions in this section apply to this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Access to persons served by a program.</span></h2>
          <p>"Access to persons served by a program" means physical access to persons receiving services, access to the persons' personal property, or access to the persons' personal, financial, or health information, without continuous, direct supervision, as defined in subdivision 8.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Alternative background study.</span></h2>
          <p>"Alternative background study" means:</p>
          <p>(1) the collection and processing of a background study subject's fingerprints, including the process of obtaining a background study subject's classifiable fingerprints and photograph as required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.5">245C.05, subdivision 5</a>, paragraph (c); and</p>
          <p>(2) a review of records conducted by the commissioner pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08">245C.08</a> in order to forward the background study investigating information to the entity that submitted the alternative background study request under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.031#stat.245C.031.2">245C.031, subdivision 2</a>. The commissioner shall not make any eligibility determinations on background studies conducted under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.031">245C.031</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Annual or annually.</span></h2>
          <p>"Annual" or "annually" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02#stat.245A.02.2b">245A.02, subdivision 2b</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Applicant.</span></h2>
          <p>"Applicant" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02#stat.245A.02.3">245A.02, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Authorized fingerprint collection vendor.</span></h2>
          <p>"Authorized fingerprint collection vendor" means a qualified organization under a written contract with the commissioner to provide services in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.5">245C.05, subdivision 5</a>, paragraph (c).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Background study.</span></h2>
          <p>"Background study" means the review of records conducted by the commissioner to determine whether a subject is disqualified from direct contact with persons served by a program and, where specifically provided in statutes, whether a subject is disqualified from having access to persons served by a program and from working in a children's residential facility or foster residence setting.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.5a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5a.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Renumbered subd 12a]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Child.</span></h2>
          <p>"Child" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02#stat.245A.02.4">245A.02, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.6a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6a.<span class="headnote">Child care background study subject.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Child care background study subject" means an individual who is affiliated with a licensed child care center, certified license-exempt child care center, licensed family child care program, or legal nonlicensed child care provider authorized under chapter 142E, and who is:</p>
          <p>(1) employed by a child care provider for compensation;</p>
          <p>(2) assisting in the care of a child for a child care provider;</p>
          <p>(3) a person applying for licensure, certification, or enrollment;</p>
          <p>(4) a controlling individual as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02#stat.245A.02.5a">245A.02, subdivision 5a</a>;</p>
          <p>(5) an individual 13 years of age or older who lives in the household where the licensed program will be provided and who is not receiving licensed services from the program;</p>
          <p>(6) an individual ten to 12 years of age who lives in the household where the licensed services will be provided when the commissioner has reasonable cause as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02#stat.245C.02.15">245C.02, subdivision 15</a>;</p>
          <p>(7) an individual who, without providing direct contact services at a licensed program, certified program, or program authorized under chapter 142E, may have unsupervised access to a child receiving services from a program when the commissioner has reasonable cause as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02#stat.245C.02.15">245C.02, subdivision 15</a>;</p>
          <p>(8) a volunteer, contractor providing services for hire in the program, prospective employee, or other individual who has unsupervised physical access to a child served by a program and who is not under supervision by an individual listed in clause (1) or (5), regardless of whether the individual provides program services; or</p>
          <p>(9) an authorized agent in a license-exempt certified child care center as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142C.01#stat.142C.01.3">142C.01, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), an individual who is providing services that are not part of the child care program is not required to have a background study if:</p>
          <p>(1) the child receiving services is signed out of the child care program for the duration that the services are provided;</p>
          <p>(2) the licensed child care center, certified license-exempt child care center, licensed family child care program, or legal nonlicensed child care provider authorized under chapter 142E has obtained advanced written permission from the parent authorizing the child to receive the services, which is maintained in the child's record;</p>
          <p>(3) the licensed child care center, certified license-exempt child care center, licensed family child care program, or legal nonlicensed child care provider authorized under chapter 119B maintains documentation on site that identifies the individual service provider and the services being provided; and</p>
          <p>(4) the licensed child care center, certified license-exempt child care center, licensed family child care program, or legal nonlicensed child care provider authorized under chapter 119B ensures that the service provider does not have unsupervised access to a child not receiving the provider's services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.6b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6b.<span class="headnote">Children's residential facility.</span></h2>
          <p>"Children's residential facility" means a children's residential facility licensed by the commissioner of corrections or the commissioner of human services under Minnesota Rules, chapter 2960.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>"Commissioner" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02#stat.245A.02.5">245A.02, subdivision 5</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.7a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7a.<span class="headnote">Conservator.</span></h2>
          <p>"Conservator" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.1-201">524.1-201</a>, clause (10), and includes proposed and current conservators.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Continuous, direct supervision.</span></h2>
          <p>"Continuous, direct supervision" means an individual is within sight or hearing of the program's supervising individual to the extent that the program's supervising individual is capable at all times of intervening to protect the health and safety of the persons served by the program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.17.63.0">2023 c 70 art 17 s 63</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.9a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9a.<span class="headnote">Conviction.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Conviction" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.02">609.02</a>, subdivision 5.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">County agency.</span></h2>
          <p>"County agency" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02#stat.245A.02.6">245A.02, subdivision 6</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Direct contact.</span></h2>
          <p>"Direct contact" means providing face-to-face care, training, supervision, counseling, consultation, or medication assistance to persons served by the program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.11a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11a.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Renumbered subd 11d]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.11b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11b.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Renumbered subd 11e]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.11c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11c.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered 245C.02, subd 11g]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.11d">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11d.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered 245C.02, subd 11h]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.11e">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11e.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered 245C.02, subd 11i]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.11f">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11f.<span class="headnote">Employee.</span></h2>
          <p>"Employee" means an individual who provides services or seeks to provide services for or through the entity with which they are required to be affiliated in NETStudy 2.0 and who is subject to oversight by the entity, which includes but is not limited to continuous, direct supervision by the entity and being subject to immediate removal from providing direct contact services by the entity when required. This subdivision does not apply to child care background study subjects under subdivision 6a.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.11g">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11g.<span class="headnote">Entity.</span></h2>
          <p>"Entity" means any program, organization, license holder, or agency required to initiate or submit a background study.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.11g">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11g.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered 245C.02, subd 11j]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.11h">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11h.<span class="headnote">Foster family setting.</span></h2>
          <p>"Foster family setting" has the meaning given in Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/2960.3010">2960.3010</a>, subpart 23.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.11i">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11i.<span class="headnote">Foster residence setting.</span></h2>
          <p>"Foster residence setting" has the meaning given in Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/2960.3010">2960.3010</a>, subpart 26, and includes settings licensed by the commissioner of corrections or the commissioner of human services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.11j">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11j.<span class="headnote">Guardian.</span></h2>
          <p>"Guardian" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.1-201">524.1-201</a>, clause (27), and includes proposed and current guardians.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">License.</span></h2>
          <p>"License" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02#stat.245A.02.8">245A.02, subdivision 8</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.12a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12a.<span class="headnote">License-exempt child care center certification holder.</span></h2>
          <p>"License-exempt child care center certification holder" has the meaning given for "certification holder" in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142C.01#stat.142C.01.5">142C.01, subdivision 5</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">License holder.</span></h2>
          <p>"License holder" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02#stat.245A.02.9">245A.02, subdivision 9</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.13a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13a.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Renumbered subd 13d]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.13b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13b.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Renumbered subd 13e]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.13c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13c.<span class="headnote">National criminal history record check.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "National criminal history record check" means a check of records maintained by the Federal Bureau of Investigation through submission of fingerprints through the Minnesota Bureau of Criminal Apprehension to the Federal Bureau of Investigation, when specifically required by law.</p>
          <p>(b) For the purposes of this chapter, "national crime information database," "national criminal records repository," "criminal history with the Federal Bureau of Investigation," and "national criminal record check" refer to a national criminal history record check as defined in this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.13d">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13d.<span class="headnote">NETStudy.</span></h2>
          <p>"NETStudy" means the commissioner's online system implemented in July 2004 and used by entities for submitting background study requests required under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.13e">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13e.<span class="headnote">NETStudy 2.0.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "NETStudy 2.0" means the commissioner's system that replaces both NETStudy and the department's internal background study processing system. NETStudy 2.0 is designed to enhance protection of children and vulnerable adults by improving the accuracy of background studies through fingerprint-based criminal record checks and expanding the background studies to include a review of information from the Minnesota Court Information System and the national crime information database. NETStudy 2.0 is also designed to increase efficiencies in and the speed of the hiring process by:</p>
          <p>(1) providing access to and updates from public web-based data related to employment eligibility;</p>
          <p>(2) decreasing the need for repeat studies through electronic updates of background study subjects' criminal records;</p>
          <p>(3) supporting identity verification using subjects' Social Security numbers and photographs;</p>
          <p>(4) using electronic employer notifications;</p>
          <p>(5) issuing immediate verification of subjects' eligibility to provide services as more studies are completed under the NETStudy 2.0 system; and</p>
          <p>(6) providing electronic access to certain notices for entities and background study subjects.</p>
          <p>(b) Information obtained by entities from public web-based data through NETStudy 2.0 under paragraph (a), clause (1), or any other source that is not direct correspondence from the commissioner is not a notice of disqualification from the commissioner under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Person.</span></h2>
          <p>"Person" means a child as defined in subdivision 6 or an adult as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02#stat.245A.02.2">245A.02, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.14a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14a.<span class="headnote">Private agency.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"Private agency" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.01#stat.142B.01.22">142B.01, subdivision 22</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.14b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14b.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.47.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 47</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Reasonable cause.</span></h2>
          <p>"Reasonable cause" means information or circumstances exist that provide the commissioner with articulable suspicion that further pertinent information may exist concerning a subject. The commissioner has reasonable cause to require a background study when the commissioner has received a report from the subject, the license holder, or a third party indicating that the subject has a history that would disqualify the individual or that may pose a risk to the health or safety of persons receiving services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.15a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15a.<span class="headnote">Reasonable cause to require a national criminal history record check.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Reasonable cause to require a national criminal history record check" means information or circumstances exist that provide the commissioner with articulable suspicion that further pertinent information may exist concerning a background study subject that merits conducting a national criminal history record check on that subject. The commissioner has reasonable cause to require a national criminal history record check when:</p>
          <p>(1) information from the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension indicates that the subject is a multistate offender;</p>
          <p>(2) information from the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension indicates that multistate offender status is undetermined;</p>
          <p>(3) the commissioner has received a report from the subject or a third party indicating that the subject has a criminal history in a jurisdiction other than Minnesota; or</p>
          <p>(4) information from the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension for a state-based name and date of birth background study in which the subject is a minor that indicates that the subject has a criminal history.</p>
          <p>(b) In addition to the circumstances described in paragraph (a), the commissioner has reasonable cause to require a national criminal history record check if the subject is not currently residing in Minnesota or resided in a jurisdiction other than Minnesota during the previous five years.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Recurring maltreatment.</span></h2>
          <p>"Recurring maltreatment" means more than one incident of maltreatment for which there is a preponderance of evidence that the maltreatment occurred and that the subject was responsible for the maltreatment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.16a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16a.<span class="headnote">Results.</span></h2>
          <p>"Results" means a determination that a study subject is eligible, disqualified, set aside, granted a variance, or that more time is needed to complete the background study.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.83.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 83</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.17a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17a.<span class="headnote">Roster.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Roster" means the electronic method used to identify the entity or entities required to conduct background studies under this chapter with which a background subject is affiliated. There are three types of rosters: active roster, inactive roster, and master roster.</p>
          <p>(b) "Active roster" means the list of individuals specific to an entity who have been determined eligible under this chapter to provide services for the entity and who the entity has identified as affiliated. An individual shall remain on the entity's active roster and is considered affiliated until the commissioner determines the individual is ineligible or the entity removes the individual from the entity's active roster.</p>
          <p>(c) "Inactive roster" means the list maintained by the commissioner of individuals who are eligible under this chapter to provide services and are not on an active roster. Individuals shall remain on the inactive roster for no more than 180 consecutive days, unless the individual submits a written request to the commissioner requesting to remain on the inactive roster for a longer period of time. Upon the commissioner's receipt of information that may cause an individual on the inactive roster to be disqualified under this chapter, the commissioner shall remove the individual from the inactive roster, and if the individual again seeks a position requiring a background study, the individual shall be required to complete a new background study.</p>
          <p>(d) "Master roster" means the list maintained by the commissioner of all individuals who, as a result of a background study under this chapter, and regardless of affiliation with an entity, are determined by the commissioner to be eligible to provide services for one or more entities. The master roster includes all background study subjects on rosters under paragraphs (b) and (c).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Serious maltreatment.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Serious maltreatment" means sexual abuse, maltreatment resulting in death, neglect resulting in serious injury which reasonably requires the care of a physician, advanced practice registered nurse, or physician assistant whether or not the care of a physician, advanced practice registered nurse, or physician assistant was sought, or abuse resulting in serious injury.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of this definition, "care of a physician, advanced practice registered nurse, or physician assistant" is treatment received or ordered by a physician, physician assistant, or advanced practice registered nurse, but does not include:</p>
          <p>(1) diagnostic testing, assessment, or observation;</p>
          <p>(2) the application of, recommendation to use, or prescription solely for a remedy that is available over the counter without a prescription; or</p>
          <p>(3) a prescription solely for a topical antibiotic to treat burns when there is no follow-up appointment.</p>
          <p>(c) For purposes of this definition, "abuse resulting in serious injury" means: bruises, bites, skin laceration, or tissue damage; fractures; dislocations; evidence of internal injuries; head injuries with loss of consciousness; extensive second-degree or third-degree burns and other burns for which complications are present; extensive second-degree or third-degree frostbite and other frostbite for which complications are present; irreversible mobility or avulsion of teeth; injuries to the eyes; ingestion of foreign substances and objects that are harmful; near drowning; and heat exhaustion or sunstroke.</p>
          <p>(d) Serious maltreatment includes neglect when it results in criminal sexual conduct against a child or vulnerable adult.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Subject of a background study.</span></h2>
          <p>"Subject of a background study" means an individual on whom a background study is required or completed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Substance use disorder treatment field.</span></h2>
          <p>"Substance use disorder treatment field" means a program exclusively serving individuals 18 years of age and older and that is required to be:</p>
          <p>(1) licensed under chapter 245G; or</p>
          <p>(2) registered under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/157.17">157.17</a> as a board and lodge establishment that predominantly serves individuals being treated for or recovering from a substance use disorder.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.21">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.<span class="headnote">Title IV-E eligible.</span></h2>
          <p>"Title IV-E eligible" means a children's residential facility or foster residence setting that is designated by the commissioner as eligible to receive Title IV-E payments for a child placed at the children's residential facility or foster residence setting.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.02.22">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 22.<span class="headnote">Volunteer.</span></h2>
          <p>"Volunteer" means an individual who:</p>
          <p>(1) provides or seeks to provide services for or through an entity without direct compensation for services provided or resides in the home where services are being provided;</p>
          <p>(2) is required to be affiliated in NETStudy 2.0 with the entity; and</p>
          <p>(3) is subject to oversight by the entity, including but not limited to continuous, direct supervision and immediate removal from providing direct contact services when required.</p>
          <p class="bl">This subdivision does not apply to child care background study subjects under subdivision 6a.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.02.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.2.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.38.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.26.0">2007 c 112 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.3.5.0">2007 c 147 art 3 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/329/#laws.1.14.0">2010 c 329 art 1 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/250/#laws.0.1.0">2014 c 250 s 1</a>-4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/78/#laws.4.33.0">2015 c 78 art 4 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.16.18.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 16 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/166/#laws.0.1.0">2018 c 166 s 1</a>-5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/9/#laws.2.61.0">1Sp2019 c 9 art 2 s 61</a>-64; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/115/#laws.4.79.0">2020 c 115 art 4 s 79</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.5.5.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 5 s 5</a>-8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.11.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 11</a>-16,85; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/58/#laws.0.100.0">2022 c 58 s 100</a>,170; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.2.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 2</a>-4; art 17 s 20-23,61; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/80/#laws.2.74.0">2024 c 80 art 2 s 74</a>; art 3 s 3; art 5 s 7; art 8 s 10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/115/#laws.16.41.0">2024 c 115 art 16 s 41</a>; art 19 s 10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.62.14.0">2024 c 127 art 62 s 14</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.03">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.03 BACKGROUND STUDY; INDIVIDUALS TO BE STUDIED.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote"> Programs licensed by the commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall conduct a background study on:</p>
          <p>(1) the person or persons applying for a license;</p>
          <p>(2) an individual age 13 and over living in the household where the licensed program will be provided who is not receiving licensed services from the program;</p>
          <p>(3) current or prospective employees of the applicant or license holder who will have direct contact with persons served by the facility, agency, or program;</p>
          <p>(4) volunteers or student volunteers who will have direct contact with persons served by the program to provide program services if the contact is not under the continuous, direct supervision by an individual listed in clause (1) or (3);</p>
          <p>(5) an individual age ten to 12 living in the household where the licensed services will be provided when the commissioner has reasonable cause as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02">245C.02</a>, subdivision 15;</p>
          <p>(6) an individual who, without providing direct contact services at a licensed program, may have unsupervised access to children or vulnerable adults receiving services from a program, when the commissioner has reasonable cause as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02">245C.02</a>, subdivision 15; and</p>
          <p>(7) all controlling individuals as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02#stat.245A.02.5a">245A.02, subdivision 5a</a>;</p>
          <p>(8) notwithstanding clause (3), for children's residential facilities and foster residence settings, any adult working in the facility, whether or not the individual will have direct contact with persons served by the facility.</p>
          <p>(b) For child foster care when the license holder resides in the home where foster care services are provided, a short-term substitute caregiver providing direct contact services for a child for less than 72 hours of continuous care is not required to receive a background study under this chapter.</p>
          <p>(c) This subdivision applies to the following programs that must be licensed under chapter 245A:</p>
          <p>(1) adult foster care;</p>
          <p>(2) children's residential facilities;</p>
          <p>(3) licensed home and community-based services under chapter 245D;</p>
          <p>(4) residential mental health programs for adults;</p>
          <p>(5) substance use disorder treatment programs under chapter 245G;</p>
          <p>(6) withdrawal management programs under chapter 245F;</p>
          <p>(7) adult day care centers;</p>
          <p>(8) family adult day services;</p>
          <p>(9) detoxification programs;</p>
          <p>(10) community residential settings;</p>
          <p>(11) intensive residential treatment services and residential crisis stabilization under chapter 245I; and</p>
          <p>(12) treatment programs for persons with sexual psychopathic personality or sexually dangerous persons, licensed under chapter 245A and according to Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/9515.3000">9515.3000</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/9515.3110">9515.3110</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Procedure.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Individuals and organizations that are required under this section to have or initiate background studies shall comply with the requirements of this chapter.</p>
          <p>(b) All studies conducted under this section shall be conducted according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/299C.60">299C.60</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/299C.64">299C.64</a>, including the consent and self-disclosure required in section 299C.62, subdivision 2. This requirement does not apply to subdivisions 1, paragraph (c), clauses (2) to (5), and 6a.</p>
          <p>(c) All data obtained by the commissioner for a background study completed under this section is classified as private data on individuals, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02">13.02, subdivision </a>12.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Personal care provider organizations.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall conduct background studies on any individual required under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0651">256B.0651 </a>to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0654">256B.0654</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0659">256B.0659</a> to have a background study completed under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Supplemental nursing services agencies.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall conduct all background studies required under this chapter and initiated by supplemental nursing services agencies registered under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144A.71#stat.144A.71.1">144A.71, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Personal care assistance provider agency; background studies.</span></h2>
          <p>Personal care assistance provider agencies enrolled to provide personal care assistance services under the medical assistance program must meet the following requirements:</p>
          <p>(1) owners who have a five percent interest or more and all managing employees are subject to a background study as provided in this chapter. This requirement applies to currently enrolled personal care assistance provider agencies and agencies seeking enrollment as a personal care assistance provider agency. "Managing employee" has the same meaning as in Code of Federal Regulations, title 42, section 455.101. An organization is barred from enrollment if:</p>
          <p>(i) the organization has not initiated background studies of owners and managing employees; or</p>
          <p>(ii) the organization has initiated background studies of owners and managing employees and the commissioner has sent the organization a notice that an owner or managing employee of the organization has been disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a>, and the owner or managing employee has not received a set aside of the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>; and</p>
          <p>(2) a background study must be initiated and completed for all qualified professionals.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.3b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3b.<span class="headnote">Exception to personal care assistant; requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>The personal care assistant for a recipient may be allowed to enroll with a different personal care assistance provider agency upon initiation of a new background study according to this chapter if:</p>
          <p>(1) the commissioner determines that a change in enrollment or affiliation of the personal care assistant is needed in order to ensure continuity of services and protect the health and safety of the recipient;</p>
          <p>(2) the chosen agency has been continuously enrolled as a personal care assistance provider agency for at least two years;</p>
          <p>(3) the recipient chooses to transfer to the personal care assistance provider agency;</p>
          <p>(4) the personal care assistant has been continuously enrolled with the former personal care assistance provider agency since the last background study was completed; and</p>
          <p>(5) the personal care assistant continues to meet requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0659">256B.0659</a>, subdivision 11, notwithstanding paragraph (a), clause (3).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Personnel pool agencies; temporary personnel agencies; educational programs; professional services agencies.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner also may conduct studies on individuals specified in subdivision 1, paragraph (a), clauses (3) and (4), when the studies are initiated by:</p>
          <p>(1) personnel pool agencies;</p>
          <p>(2) temporary personnel agencies;</p>
          <p>(3) educational programs that train individuals by providing direct contact services in licensed programs; and</p>
          <p>(4) professional services agencies that are not licensed and that work with licensed programs to provide direct contact services or individuals who provide direct contact services.</p>
          <p>(b) Personnel pool agencies, temporary personnel agencies, and professional services agencies must employ the individuals providing direct care services for children, people with disabilities, or the elderly. Individuals must be affiliated in NETStudy 2.0 and subject to oversight by the entity, which includes but is not limited to continuous, direct supervision by the entity and being subject to immediate removal from providing direct care services when required.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Other state agencies.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall conduct background studies on applicants and license holders under the jurisdiction of other state agencies who are required in other statutory sections to initiate background studies under this chapter, including the applicant's or license holder's employees and volunteers when required under other statutory sections.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.5a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5a.<span class="headnote">Facilities serving children or adults licensed or regulated by the Department of Health.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as specified in paragraph (b), the commissioner shall conduct background studies of:</p>
          <p>(1) individuals providing services who have direct contact, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02#stat.245C.02.11">245C.02, subdivision 11</a>, with patients and residents in hospitals, boarding care homes, outpatient surgical centers licensed under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.50">144.50</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.58">144.58</a>; nursing homes and home care agencies licensed under chapter 144A; assisted living facilities and assisted living facilities with dementia care licensed under chapter 144G; and board and lodging establishments that are registered to provide supportive or health supervision services under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/157.17">157.17</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) individuals specified in subdivision 2 who provide direct contact services in a nursing home or a home care agency licensed under chapter 144A; an assisted living facility or assisted living facility with dementia care licensed under chapter 144G; or a boarding care home licensed under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.50">144.50</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.58">144.58</a>. If the individual undergoing a study resides outside of Minnesota, the study must include a check for substantiated findings of maltreatment of adults and children in the individual's state of residence when the state makes the information available;</p>
          <p>(3) all other employees in assisted living facilities or assisted living facilities with dementia care licensed under chapter 144G, nursing homes licensed under chapter 144A, and boarding care homes licensed under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.50">144.50</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.58">144.58</a>. A disqualification of an individual in this section shall disqualify the individual from positions allowing direct contact with or access to patients or residents receiving services. "Access" means physical access to a client or the client's personal property without continuous, direct supervision as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02#stat.245C.02.8">245C.02, subdivision 8</a>, when the employee's employment responsibilities do not include providing direct contact services;</p>
          <p>(4) individuals employed by a supplemental nursing services agency, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144A.70">144A.70</a>, who are providing services in health care facilities;</p>
          <p>(5) controlling persons of a supplemental nursing services agency, as defined by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144A.70">144A.70</a>; and</p>
          <p>(6) license applicants, owners, managerial officials, and controlling individuals who are required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144A.476#stat.144A.476.1">144A.476, subdivision 1</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.13#stat.144G.13.1">144G.13, subdivision 1</a>, to undergo a background study under this chapter, regardless of the licensure status of the license applicant, owner, managerial official, or controlling individual.</p>
          <p>(b) An entity shall not initiate a background study on any individual identified in paragraph (a), clauses (1) to (5), if the individual has a valid license issued by a health-related licensing board as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.01#stat.214.01.2">214.01, subdivision 2</a>, and has completed the criminal background check as required in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.075">214.075</a>. An entity that is affiliated with individuals who meet the requirements of this paragraph must separate those individuals from the entity's roster for NETStudy 2.0. The Department of Human Services is not liable for conducting background studies that have been submitted or not removed from the roster in violation of this provision.</p>
          <p>(c) If a facility or program is licensed by the Department of Human Services and the Department of Health and is subject to the background study provisions of this chapter, the Department of Human Services is solely responsible for the background studies of individuals in the jointly licensed program.</p>
          <p>(d) The commissioner of health shall review and make decisions regarding reconsideration requests, including whether to grant variances, according to the procedures and criteria in this chapter. The commissioner of health shall inform the requesting individual and the Department of Human Services of the commissioner of health's decision regarding the reconsideration. The commissioner of health's decision to grant or deny a reconsideration of a disqualification is a final administrative agency action.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.5b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5b.<span class="headnote">Facilities serving children or youth licensed by the Department of Corrections.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall conduct background studies of individuals working in secure and nonsecure children's residential facilities, juvenile detention facilities, and foster residence settings, whether or not the individual will have direct contact, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02#stat.245C.02.11">245C.02, subdivision 11</a>, with persons served in the facilities or settings.</p>
          <p>(b) A clerk or administrator of any court, the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension, a prosecuting attorney, a county sheriff, or a chief of a local police department shall assist in conducting background studies by providing the commissioner of human services or the commissioner's representative all criminal conviction data available from local and state criminal history record repositories related to applicants, operators, all persons living in a household, and all staff of any facility subject to background studies under this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(c) For the purpose of this subdivision, the term "secure and nonsecure residential facility and detention facility" includes programs licensed or certified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/241.021">241.021</a>, subdivision 2.</p>
          <p>(d) If an individual is disqualified, the Department of Human Services shall notify the disqualified individual and the facility in which the disqualified individual provides services of the disqualification and shall inform the disqualified individual of the right to request a reconsideration of the disqualification by submitting the request to the Department of Corrections.</p>
          <p>(e) The commissioner of corrections shall review and make decisions regarding reconsideration requests, including whether to grant variances, according to the procedures and criteria in this chapter. The commissioner of corrections shall inform the requesting individual and the Department of Human Services of the commissioner of corrections' decision regarding the reconsideration. The commissioner of corrections' decision to grant or deny a reconsideration of a disqualification is the final administrative agency action.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.5c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5c.<span class="headnote">Programs licensed or regulated by the Department of Children, Youth, and Families.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall conduct a background study on:</p>
          <p>(1) the person or persons applying for a license;</p>
          <p>(2) an individual age 13 and over, living in the household where the licensed program will be provided, who is not receiving licensed services from the program;</p>
          <p>(3) current or prospective employees of the applicant or license holder who will have direct contact with persons served by the facility, agency, or program;</p>
          <p>(4) volunteers or student volunteers who will have direct contact with persons served by the program to provide program services if the contact is not under the continuous, direct supervision by an individual listed in clause (1) or (3);</p>
          <p>(5) an individual age ten to 12 living in the household where the licensed services will be provided when the commissioner has reasonable cause as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02">245C.02</a>, subdivision 15;</p>
          <p>(6) an individual who, without providing direct contact services at a licensed program, may have unsupervised access to children receiving services from a program, when the commissioner has reasonable cause as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02#stat.245C.02.15">245C.02, subdivision 15</a>;</p>
          <p>(7) all controlling individuals as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142B.01#stat.142B.01.11">142B.01, subdivision 11</a>; and</p>
          <p>(8) notwithstanding the other requirements in this subdivision, child care background study subjects as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02#stat.245C.02.6a">245C.02, subdivision 6a</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) For child foster care when the license holder resides in the home where foster care services are provided, a short-term substitute caregiver providing direct contact services for a child for less than 72 hours of continuous care is not required to receive a background study under this chapter.</p>
          <p>(c) This subdivision applies to the following programs:</p>
          <p>(1) a child foster care program licensed or seeking a license under chapter 142B;</p>
          <p>(2) a child care center licensed or seeking a license under chapter 142B;</p>
          <p>(3) a license-exempt child care center certified under chapter 142C;</p>
          <p>(4) a legal nonlicensed child care provider authorized under chapter 142E; or</p>
          <p>(5) a Head Start program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Unlicensed home and community-based waiver providers of service to seniors and individuals with disabilities.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall conduct background studies of any individual who provides direct contact, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02">245C.02</a>, subdivision 11, for services specified in the federally approved home and community-based waiver plans under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.4912">256B.4912</a>. The individual studied must meet the requirements of this chapter prior to providing waiver services and as part of ongoing enrollment.</p>
          <p>(b) The requirements in paragraph (a) apply to consumer-directed community supports under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.4911">256B.4911</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.6a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6a.<span class="headnote">Legal nonlicensed and certified child care programs.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall conduct background studies for each child care background study subject as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02#stat.245C.02.6a">245C.02, subdivision 6a</a>, as required by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142C.09">142C.09</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142E.16">142E.16</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Children's therapeutic services and supports providers.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall conduct background studies of all direct service providers and volunteers for children's therapeutic services and supports providers under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0943">256B.0943</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.17.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 17</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Community first services and supports and financial management services organizations.</span></h2>
          <p>Individuals affiliated with Community First Services and Supports (CFSS) agency-providers and Financial Management Services (FMS) providers enrolled to provide CFSS services under the medical assistance program must meet the following requirements:</p>
          <p>(1) owners who have a five percent interest or more and all managing employees are subject to a background study under this chapter. This requirement applies to currently enrolled providers and agencies seeking enrollment. "Managing employee" has the meaning given in Code of Federal Regulations, title 42, section 455.101. An organization is barred from enrollment if:</p>
          <p>(i) the organization has not initiated background studies of owners and managing employees; or</p>
          <p>(ii) the organization has initiated background studies of owners and managing employees and the commissioner has sent the organization a notice that an owner or managing employee of the organization has been disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> and the owner or managing employee has not received a set aside of the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) a background study must be initiated and completed for all staff who will have direct contact with the participant to provide worker training and development; and</p>
          <p>(3) a background study must be initiated and completed for all support workers.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.9a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9a.<span class="headnote">Exception to support worker requirements for continuity of services.</span></h2>
          <p>The support worker for a participant may enroll with a different Community First Services and Supports (CFSS) agency-provider or Financial Management Services (FMS) provider upon initiation, rather than completion, of a new background study according to this chapter if:</p>
          <p>(1) the commissioner determines that the support worker's change in enrollment or affiliation is necessary to ensure continuity of services and to protect the health and safety of the participant;</p>
          <p>(2) the chosen agency-provider or FMS provider has been continuously enrolled as a CFSS agency-provider or FMS provider for at least two years or since the inception of the CFSS program, whichever is shorter;</p>
          <p>(3) the participant served by the support worker chooses to transfer to the CFSS agency-provider or the FMS provider to which the support worker is transferring;</p>
          <p>(4) the support worker has been continuously enrolled with the former CFSS agency-provider or FMS provider since the support worker's last background study was completed; and</p>
          <p>(5) the support worker continues to meet the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.85">256B.85, subdivision </a>16, notwithstanding paragraph (a), clause (1).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Providers of housing support or supplementary services.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall conduct background studies of the following individuals who provide services under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256I.04">256I.04</a>:</p>
          <p>(1) controlling individuals as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02">245A.02</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) managerial officials as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02">245A.02</a>; and</p>
          <p>(3) all employees and volunteers of the establishment who have direct contact with recipients or who have unsupervised access to recipients, recipients' personal property, or recipients' private data.</p>
          <p>(b) The provider of housing support must comply with all requirements for entities initiating background studies under this chapter.</p>
          <p>(c) A provider of housing support must demonstrate that all individuals who are required to have a background study according to paragraph (a) have a notice stating that:</p>
          <p>(1) the individual is not disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a>; or</p>
          <p>(2) the individual is disqualified and the individual has been issued a set aside of the disqualification for the setting under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Repealed by amendment, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.17.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 17</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Providers of special transportation service.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall conduct background studies of the following individuals who provide special transportation services under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/174.30">174.30</a>:</p>
          <p>(1) each person with a direct or indirect ownership interest of five percent or higher in a transportation service provider;</p>
          <p>(2) each controlling individual as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02">245A.02</a>;</p>
          <p>(3) a managerial official as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02">245A.02</a>;</p>
          <p>(4) each driver employed by the transportation service provider;</p>
          <p>(5) each individual employed by the transportation service provider to assist a passenger during transport; and</p>
          <p>(6) each employee of the transportation service agency who provides administrative support, including an employee who:</p>
          <p>(i) may have face-to-face contact with or access to passengers, passengers' personal property, or passengers' private data;</p>
          <p>(ii) performs any scheduling or dispatching tasks; or</p>
          <p>(iii) performs any billing activities.</p>
          <p>(b) When a local or contracted agency is authorizing a ride under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0625">256B.0625</a>, subdivision 17, by a volunteer driver, and the agency authorizing the ride has a reason to believe that the volunteer driver has a history that would disqualify the volunteer driver or that may pose a risk to the health or safety of passengers, the agency may initiate a background study that shall be completed according to this chapter using the commissioner of human services' online NETStudy system, or by contacting the Department of Human Services background study division for assistance. The agency that initiates the background study under this paragraph shall be responsible for providing the volunteer driver with the privacy notice required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.2c">245C.05, subdivision 2c</a>, and with the payment for the background study required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.10">245C.10</a> before the background study is completed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Providers of housing stabilization services.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall conduct background studies of any provider of housing stabilization services required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.051">256B.051</a> to have a background study completed under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Tribal nursing facilities.</span></h2>
          <p>For completed background studies to comply with a Tribal organization's licensing requirements for individuals affiliated with a Tribally licensed nursing facility, the commissioner shall obtain state and national criminal history data.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.03.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Early intensive developmental and behavioral intervention providers.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall conduct background studies according to this chapter when initiated by an early intensive developmental and behavioral intervention provider under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0949">256B.0949</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.03.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.3.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.39.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 39</a>,40; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/4/#laws.1.26.0">1Sp2005 c 4 art 1 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/79/#laws.6.3.0">2009 c 79 art 6 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/142/#laws.3.1.0">2009 c 142 art 3 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/173/#laws.1.4.0">2009 c 173 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/9/#laws.3.2.0">1Sp2011 c 9 art 3 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/216/#laws.16.16.0">2012 c 216 art 16 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/250/#laws.0.5.0">2014 c 250 s 5</a>,6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/262/#laws.4.9.0">2014 c 262 art 4 s 9; art 5 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.26.1.0">2014 c 312 art 26 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/21/#laws.1.48.0">2015 c 21 art 1 s 48</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/71/#laws.1.3.0">2015 c 71 art 1 s 3</a>,4; art 11 s 8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/90/#laws.0.2.0">2017 c 90 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.16.19.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 16 s 19</a>,20,68; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/166/#laws.0.6.0">2018 c 166 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/9/#laws.2.65.0">1Sp2019 c 9 art 2 s 65</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.5.9.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 5 s 9</a>,10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/0/30/#laws.13.83.0">2021 c 30 art 13 s 83</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.17.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.1.62.0">2022 c 98 art 1 s 62</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/50/#laws.4.3.0">2023 c 50 art 4 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.5.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 5</a>,6; art 17 s 24-27; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/80/#laws.3.3.0">2024 c 80 art 3 s 3</a>; art 5 s 7; art 8 s 11,12</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.031">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.031 BACKGROUND STUDY; ALTERNATIVE BACKGROUND STUDIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.031.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Alternative background studies.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall conduct an alternative background study of individuals listed in this section.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding other sections of this chapter, all alternative background studies except subdivision 12 shall be conducted according to this section and with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/299C.60">299C.60 </a>to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/299C.64">299C.64</a>, including the consent and self-disclosure required in section 299C.62, subdivision 2.</p>
          <p>(c) All terms in this section shall have the definitions provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02">245C.02</a>.</p>
          <p>(d) The entity that submits an alternative background study request under this section shall submit the request to the commissioner according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05">245C.05</a>.</p>
          <p>(e) The commissioner shall comply with the destruction requirements in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.051">245C.051</a>.</p>
          <p>(f) Background studies conducted under this section are subject to the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.32">245C.32</a>.</p>
          <p>(g) The commissioner shall forward all information that the commissioner receives under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08">245C.08</a> to the entity that submitted the alternative background study request under subdivision 2. The commissioner shall not make any eligibility determinations regarding background studies conducted under this section.</p>
          <p>(h) All data obtained by the commissioner for a background study completed under this section is classified as private data on individuals, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02">13.02, subdivision </a>12.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.031.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Access to information.</span></h2>
          <p>Each entity that submits an alternative background study request shall enter into an agreement with the commissioner before submitting requests for alternative background studies under this section. As a part of the agreement, the entity must agree to comply with state and federal law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.031.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Child protection workers or social services staff having responsibility for child protective duties.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall conduct an alternative background study of any person who has responsibility for child protection duties when the background study is initiated by a county social services agency or by a local welfare agency according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.36#stat.260E.36.3">260E.36, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.031.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Applicants, licensees, and other occupations regulated by the commissioner of health.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall conduct an alternative background study, including a check of state data, and a national criminal history records check of the following individuals. For studies under this section, the following persons shall complete a consent form and criminal history disclosure form:</p>
          <p>(1) An applicant for initial licensure, temporary licensure, or relicensure after a lapse in licensure as an audiologist, speech-language pathologist, or speech-language pathologist assistant, or an applicant for initial certification as a hearing instrument dispenser who must submit to a background study under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.0572">144.0572</a>.</p>
          <p>(2) An applicant for a renewal license or certificate as an audiologist, speech-language pathologist, or hearing instrument dispenser who was licensed or obtained a certificate before January 1, 2018.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.031.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.47.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 47</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.031.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.47.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 47</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.031.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.47.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 47</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.031.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Guardians ad litem.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall conduct an alternative background study of:</p>
          <p>(1) A guardian ad litem appointed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518.165">518.165</a> if a background study of the guardian ad litem has not been completed within the past three years. The background study of the guardian ad litem must be completed before the court appoints the guardian ad litem, unless the court determines that it is in the best interests of the child to appoint the guardian ad litem before a background study is completed by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(2) A guardian ad litem once every three years after the guardian has been appointed, as long as the individual continues to serve as a guardian ad litem.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.031.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Guardians ad litem; required checks.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An alternative background study for a guardian ad litem under subdivision 8 must include:</p>
          <p>(1) criminal history data from the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension and other criminal history data obtained by the commissioner of human services; and</p>
          <p>(2) data regarding whether the person has been a perpetrator of substantiated maltreatment of a minor or a vulnerable adult. If the study subject has been determined by the Department of Human Services or the Department of Health to be the perpetrator of substantiated maltreatment of a minor or a vulnerable adult in a licensed facility, the response must include a copy of the public portion of the investigation memorandum under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.30">260E.30</a> or the public portion of the investigation memorandum under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557#stat.626.557.12b">626.557, subdivision 12b</a>. When the background study shows that the subject has been determined by a county adult protection or child protection agency to have been responsible for maltreatment, the court shall be informed of the county, the date of the finding, and the nature of the maltreatment that was substantiated.</p>
          <p>(b) For checks of records under paragraph (a), clauses (1) and (2), the commissioner shall provide the records within 15 working days of receiving the request. The information obtained under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05">245C.05</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08">245C.08</a> from a national criminal history records check shall be provided within three working days of the commissioner's receipt of the data.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.30">260E.30</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557#stat.626.557.12b">626.557, subdivision 12b</a>, if the commissioner or county lead agency or lead investigative agency has information that a person of whom a background study was previously completed under this section has been determined to be a perpetrator of maltreatment of a minor or vulnerable adult, the commissioner or the county may provide this information to the court that requested the background study.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.031.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">First-time applicants for educator licenses with the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board shall make all eligibility determinations for alternative background studies conducted under this section for the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board. The commissioner may conduct an alternative background study of all first-time applicants for educator licenses pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.18#stat.122A.18.8">122A.18, subdivision 8</a>. The alternative background study for all first-time applicants for educator licenses must include a review of information from the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension, including criminal history data as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.87">13.87</a>, and must also include a review of the national criminal records repository.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.031.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">First-time applicants for administrator licenses with the Board of School Administrators.</span></h2>
          <p>The Board of School Administrators shall make all eligibility determinations for alternative background studies conducted under this section for the Board of School Administrators. The commissioner may conduct an alternative background study of all first-time applicants for administrator licenses pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.18#stat.122A.18.8">122A.18, subdivision 8</a>. The alternative background study for all first-time applicants for administrator licenses must include a review of information from the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension, including criminal history data as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.87">13.87</a>, and must also include a review of the national criminal records repository.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.031.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Occupations regulated by MNsure.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall conduct a background study of any individual required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62V.05">62V.05</a> to have a background study completed under this chapter. Notwithstanding subdivision 1, paragraph (g), the commissioner shall conduct a background study only based on Minnesota criminal records of:</p>
          <p>(1) each navigator;</p>
          <p>(2) each in-person assister; and</p>
          <p>(3) each certified application counselor.</p>
          <p>(b) The MNsure board of directors may initiate background studies required by paragraph (a) using the online NETStudy 2.0 system operated by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner shall review information that the commissioner receives to determine if the study subject has potentially disqualifying offenses. The commissioner shall send a letter to the subject indicating any of the subject's potential disqualifications as well as any relevant records. The commissioner shall send a copy of the letter indicating any of the subject's potential disqualifications to the MNsure board.</p>
          <p>(d) The MNsure board or its delegate shall review a reconsideration request of an individual in paragraph (a), including granting a set aside, according to the procedures and criteria in chapter 245C. The board shall notify the individual and the Department of Human Services of the board's decision.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.031.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.18.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.7.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 7</a>; art 17 s 28,29; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.25.28.0">2024 c 127 art 25 s 28</a></p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>The amendment to subdivision 4 by Laws 2024, chapter 127, article 25, section 28, is effective July 1, 2025. Laws 2024, chapter 127, article 25, section 28, the effective date.</p>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245C.032"><b>245C.032</b> MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.47.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 47</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.033">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.033 GUARDIANS AND CONSERVATORS; MALTREATMENT AND STATE LICENSING AGENCY CHECKS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.033.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Maltreatment data.</span></h2>
