          <p>Requests for maltreatment data submitted pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.5-118">524.5-118</a> must include information regarding whether the guardian or conservator has been a perpetrator of substantiated maltreatment of a vulnerable adult under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a> or a minor under chapter 260E. If the guardian or conservator has been the perpetrator of substantiated maltreatment of a vulnerable adult or a minor, the commissioner must include a copy of any available public portion of the investigation memorandum under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557#stat.626.557.12b">626.557, subdivision 12b</a>, or any available public portion of the investigation memorandum under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.30">260E.30</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.033.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">State licensing agency data.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Requests for state licensing agency data submitted pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.5-118">524.5-118</a> must include information from a check of state licensing agency records.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner shall provide the court with licensing agency data for licenses directly related to the responsibilities of a guardian or conservator if the guardian or conservator has a current or prior affiliation with the:</p>
          <p>(1) Lawyers Responsibility Board;</p>
          <p>(2) State Board of Accountancy;</p>
          <p>(3) Board of Social Work;</p>
          <p>(4) Board of Psychology;</p>
          <p>(5) Board of Nursing;</p>
          <p>(6) Board of Medical Practice;</p>
          <p>(7) Department of Education;</p>
          <p>(8) Department of Commerce;</p>
          <p>(9) Board of Chiropractic Examiners;</p>
          <p>(10) Board of Dentistry;</p>
          <p>(11) Board of Marriage and Family Therapy;</p>
          <p>(12) Department of Human Services;</p>
          <p>(13) Peace Officer Standards and Training (POST) Board; or</p>
          <p>(14) Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner shall provide to the court the electronically available data maintained in the agency's database, including whether the guardian or conservator is or has been licensed by the agency and whether a disciplinary action or a sanction against the individual's license, including a condition, suspension, revocation, or cancellation, is in the licensing agency's database.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.033.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Procedure; maltreatment and state licensing agency data.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For requests paid directly by the guardian or conservator, requests for maltreatment and state licensing agency data checks must be submitted by the guardian or conservator to the commissioner on the form or in the manner prescribed by the commissioner. Upon receipt of a signed informed consent and payment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.10">245C.10</a>, the commissioner shall complete the maltreatment and state licensing agency checks. Upon completion of the checks, the commissioner shall provide the requested information to the courts on the form or in the manner prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(b) For requests paid by the court based on the in forma pauperis status of the guardian or conservator, requests for maltreatment and state licensing agency data checks must be submitted by the court to the commissioner on the form or in the manner prescribed by the commissioner. The form will serve as certification that the individual has been granted in forma pauperis status. Upon receipt of a signed data request consent form from the court, the commissioner shall initiate the maltreatment and state licensing agency checks. Upon completion of the checks, the commissioner shall provide the requested information to the courts on the form or in the manner prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.033.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Classification of maltreatment and state licensing agency data; access to information.</span></h2>
          <p>All data obtained by the commissioner for maltreatment and state licensing agency checks completed under this section is classified as private data on individuals, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02#stat.13.02.12">13.02, subdivision 12</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.033.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.8.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/115/#laws.19.11.0">2024 c 115 art 19 s 11</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.04">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.04 WHEN BACKGROUND STUDY MUST OCCUR.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.04.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Licensed programs; other child care programs.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The commissioner shall conduct a background study of an individual required to be studied under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.1">245C.03, subdivision 1</a>, at least upon application for initial license for all license types.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The commissioner shall conduct a background study of an individual required to be studied under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03</a>, subdivision 1, including a child care background study subject as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02#stat.245C.02.6a">245C.02, subdivision 6a</a>, in a family child care program, licensed child care center, certified license-exempt child care center, or legal nonlicensed child care provider, on a schedule determined by the commissioner. Except as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.5a">245C.05, subdivision 5a</a>, a child care background study must include submission of fingerprints for a national criminal history record check and a review of the information under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08">245C.08</a>. A background study for a child care program must be repeated within five years from the most recent study conducted under this paragraph.</p>
          <p>(c) At reauthorization or when a new background study is needed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142E.16#stat.142E.16.2">142E.16, subdivision 2</a>, for a legal nonlicensed child care provider authorized under chapter 142E:</p>
          <p>(1) for a background study affiliated with a legal nonlicensed child care provider, the individual shall provide information required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.1">245C.05, subdivision 1</a>, paragraphs (a), (b), and (d), to the commissioner and be fingerprinted and photographed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.5">245C.05, subdivision 5</a>; and</p>
          <p>(2) the commissioner shall verify the information received under clause (1) and submit the request in NETStudy 2.0 to complete the background study.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) At reapplication for a family child care license:</p>
          <p>(1) for a background study affiliated with a licensed family child care center, the individual shall provide information required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.1">245C.05, subdivision 1</a>, paragraphs (a), (b), and (d), to the county agency, and be fingerprinted and photographed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.5">245C.05, subdivision 5</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) the county agency shall verify the information received under clause (1) and forward the information to the commissioner and submit the request in NETStudy 2.0 to complete the background study; and</p>
          <p>(3) the background study conducted by the commissioner under this paragraph must include a review of the information required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08">245C.08</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) The commissioner is not required to conduct a study of an individual at the time of reapplication for a license if the individual's background study was completed by the commissioner of human services and the following conditions are met:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) a study of the individual was conducted either at the time of initial licensure or when the individual became affiliated with the license holder;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the individual has been continuously affiliated with the license holder since the last study was conducted; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the last study of the individual was conducted on or after October 1, 1995.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) The commissioner of human services shall conduct a background study of an individual specified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.1">245C.03, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clauses (2) to (6), who is newly affiliated with a child foster family setting license holder:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the county or private agency shall collect and forward to the commissioner the information required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05">245C.05, subdivisions 1</a> and 5, when the child foster family setting applicant or license holder resides in the home where child foster care services are provided; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the background study conducted by the commissioner of human services under this paragraph must include a review of the information required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08">245C.08, subdivisions 1</a>, 3, and 4.</p>
          <p>(g) The commissioner shall conduct a background study of an individual specified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.1">245C.03, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clauses (2) to (6), who is newly affiliated with an adult foster care or family adult day services and with a family child care license holder or a legal nonlicensed child care provider authorized under chapter 142E and:</p>
          <p>(1) except as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.5a">245C.05, subdivision 5a</a>, the county shall collect and forward to the commissioner the information required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.1">245C.05, subdivision 1</a>, paragraphs (a) and (b), and subdivision 5, paragraph (b), for background studies conducted by the commissioner for all family adult day services, for adult foster care when the adult foster care license holder resides in the adult foster care residence, and for family child care and legal nonlicensed child care authorized under chapter 142E;</p>
          <p>(2) the license holder shall collect and forward to the commissioner the information required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05">245C.05, subdivisions 1</a>, paragraphs (a) and (b); and 5, paragraphs (a) and (b), for background studies conducted by the commissioner for adult foster care when the license holder does not reside in the adult foster care residence; and</p>
          <p>(3) the background study conducted by the commissioner under this paragraph must include a review of the information required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08#stat.245C.08.1">245C.08, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), and subdivisions 3 and 4.</p>
          <p>(h) Applicants for licensure, license holders, and other entities as provided in this chapter must submit completed background study requests to the commissioner using the electronic system known as NETStudy before individuals specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.1">245C.03, subdivision 1</a>, begin positions allowing direct contact in any licensed program.</p>
          <p class="in">(i) For an individual who is not on the entity's active roster, the entity must initiate a new background study through NETStudy when:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) an individual returns to a position requiring a background study following an absence of 120 or more consecutive days; or</p>
          <p class="in">(2) a program that discontinued providing licensed direct contact services for 120 or more consecutive days begins to provide direct contact licensed services again.</p>
          <p class="in">The license holder shall maintain a copy of the notification provided to the commissioner under this paragraph in the program's files. If the individual's disqualification was previously set aside for the license holder's program and the new background study results in no new information that indicates the individual may pose a risk of harm to persons receiving services from the license holder, the previous set-aside shall remain in effect.</p>
          <p class="in">(j) For purposes of this section, a physician licensed under chapter 147, advanced practice registered nurse licensed under chapter 148, or physician assistant licensed under chapter 147A is considered to be continuously affiliated upon the license holder's receipt from the commissioner of health or human services of the physician's, advanced practice registered nurse's, or physician assistant's background study results.</p>
          <p>(k) For purposes of family child care, a substitute caregiver must receive repeat background studies at the time of each license renewal.</p>
          <p>(l) A repeat background study at the time of license renewal is not required if the family child care substitute caregiver's background study was completed by the commissioner on or after October 1, 2017, and the substitute caregiver is on the license holder's active roster in NETStudy 2.0.</p>
          <p class="in">(m) Before and after school programs authorized under chapter 142E, are exempt from the background study requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123B.03">123B.03</a>, for an employee for whom a background study under this chapter has been completed.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.04.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Other state agencies.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Applicants and license holders under the jurisdiction of other state agencies who are required in other statutory sections to initiate background studies under this chapter must submit completed background study forms to the commissioner before the background study subject begins in a position allowing direct contact in the licensed program or, where applicable, prior to being employed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.04.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Personal care provider organizations.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The commissioner shall conduct a background study of an individual required to be studied under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.2">245C.03, subdivision 2</a>, at least upon application for initial enrollment under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0651">256B.0651</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0654">256B.0654</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0659">256B.0659</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Organizations required to initiate background studies under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0651">256B.0651 </a>to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0654">256B.0654 </a>and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0659">256B.0659 </a>for individuals described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.2">245C.03, subdivision 2</a>, must submit a completed background study request to the commissioner using the electronic system known as NETStudy before those individuals begin a position allowing direct contact with persons served by the organization.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Organizations required to initiate background studies under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0651">256B.0651 </a>to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0654">256B.0654</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0659">256B.0659</a> for individuals described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.2">245C.03, subdivision 2</a>, must initiate a new background study through NETStudy when an individual returns to a position requiring a background study following an absence of 120 or more consecutive days.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.04.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Supplemental nursing services agencies.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The commissioner shall conduct a background study of an individual required to be studied under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.3">245C.03, subdivision 3</a>, at least upon application for registration under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144A.71#stat.144A.71.1">144A.71, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Each supplemental nursing services agency must initiate background studies using the electronic system known as NETStudy before an individual begins a position allowing direct contact with persons served by the agency and annually thereafter.</p>
          <p>(c) A supplemental nursing services agency that initiates background studies through NETStudy 2.0 is exempt from the requirement to initiate annual background studies under paragraph (b) for individuals who are on the agency's active roster.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.04.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Agency background studies; electronic criminal case information updates; rosters; and criteria for eliminating repeat background studies.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall develop and implement an electronic process as a part of NETStudy 2.0 for the regular transfer of new criminal case information that is added to the Minnesota court information system. The commissioner's system must include for review only information that relates to individuals who are on the master roster.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner shall develop and implement an online system as a part of NETStudy 2.0 for agencies that initiate background studies under this chapter to access and maintain records of background studies initiated by that agency. The system must show all active background study subjects affiliated with that agency and the status of each individual's background study. Each agency that initiates background studies must use this system to notify the commissioner of discontinued affiliation for purposes of the processes required under paragraph (a).</p>
          <p>(c) After an entity initiating a background study has paid the applicable fee for the study and has provided the individual with the privacy notice required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.2c">245C.05, subdivision 2c</a>, NETStudy 2.0 shall immediately inform the entity whether the individual requires a background study or whether the individual is immediately eligible to provide services based on a previous background study. If the individual is immediately eligible, the entity initiating the background study shall be able to view the information previously supplied by the individual who is the subject of a background study as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.1">245C.05, subdivision 1</a>, including the individual's photograph taken at the time the individual's fingerprints were recorded. The commissioner shall not provide any entity initiating a subsequent background study with information regarding the other entities that initiated background studies on the subject.</p>
          <p>(d) Verification that an individual is eligible to provide services based on a previous background study is dependent on the individual voluntarily providing the individual's Social Security number to the commissioner at the time each background study is initiated. When an individual does not provide the individual's Social Security number for the background study, that study is not transferable and a repeat background study on that individual is required if the individual seeks a position requiring a background study under this chapter with another entity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.04.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Personnel agencies; educational programs; professional services agencies.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Agencies, programs, and individuals who initiate background studies under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.4">245C.03, subdivision 4</a>, must initiate the studies annually using the electronic system known as NETStudy.</p>
          <p>(b) Agencies, programs, and individuals who initiate background studies through NETStudy 2.0 are exempt from the requirement to initiate annual background studies under paragraph (a) for individuals who are on the agency's or program's active roster.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.04.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Unlicensed home and community-based waiver providers of service to seniors and individuals with disabilities.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Providers required to initiate background studies under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.4912">256B.4912</a> must initiate a study using the electronic system known as NETStudy before the individual begins in a position allowing direct contact with persons served by the provider.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Except as provided in paragraphs (c) and (d), the providers must initiate a background study annually of an individual required to be studied under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.6">245C.03, subdivision 6</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) After an initial background study under this subdivision is initiated on an individual by a provider of both services licensed by the commissioner and the unlicensed services under this subdivision, a repeat annual background study is not required if:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the provider maintains compliance with the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.07">245C.07</a>, paragraph (a), regarding one individual with one address and telephone number as the person to receive sensitive background study information for the multiple programs that depend on the same background study, and that the individual who is designated to receive the sensitive background information is capable of determining, upon the request of the commissioner, whether a background study subject is providing direct contact services in one or more of the provider's programs or services and, if so, at which location or locations; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the individual who is the subject of the background study provides direct contact services under the provider's licensed program for at least 40 hours per year so the individual will be recognized by a probation officer or corrections agent to prompt a report to the commissioner regarding criminal convictions as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.7">245C.05, subdivision 7</a>.</p>
          <p>(d) A provider who initiates background studies through NETStudy 2.0 is exempt from the requirement to initiate annual background studies under paragraph (b) for individuals who are on the provider's active roster.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.04.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">New study required with legal name change.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) For a background study completed on an individual required to be studied under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03</a>, the license holder or other entity that initiated the background study must initiate a new background study using the electronic system known as NETStudy when an individual who is affiliated with the license holder or other entity undergoes a legal name change.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) For background studies subject to a fee paid through the NETStudy system, the entity that initiated the study may initiate a new study under paragraph (a) or notify the commissioner of the name change through a notice to the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.04.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Current or prospective contractors serving multiple family child care license holders.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Before the implementation of NETStudy 2.0, current or prospective contractors who are required to have a background study under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.1">245C.03, subdivision 1</a>, who provide services for multiple family child care license holders in a single county, and will have direct contact with children served in the family child care setting are required to have only one background study which is transferable to all family child care programs in that county if:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the county agency maintains a record of the contractor's background study results which verify the contractor is approved to have direct contact with children receiving services;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the license holder contacts the county agency and obtains notice that the current or prospective contractor is in compliance with background study requirements and approved to have direct contact; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the contractor's background study is repeated every two years.</p>
          <p>(b) For a family child care license holder operating under NETStudy 2.0, the license holder's active roster shall be the system used to document when a background study subject is affiliated with the license holder.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.04.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Community first services and supports organizations.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall conduct a background study of an individual required to be studied under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.9">245C.03, subdivision 9</a>, at least upon application for initial enrollment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.85">256B.85</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) Before an individual described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.9">245C.03, subdivision 9</a>, begins a position allowing direct contact with a person served by an organization required to initiate a background study under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.85">256B.85</a>, the organization must receive a notice from the commissioner that the support worker is:</p>
          <p>(1) not disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a>; or</p>
          <p>(2) disqualified, but the individual has received a set-aside of the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.04.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Child protection workers or social services staff having responsibility for child protective duties.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall conduct background studies of employees of county social services and local welfare agencies having responsibility for child protection duties when the background study is initiated according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.36#stat.260E.36.3">260E.36, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.04.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Children's residential facilities and foster residence settings.</span></h2>
          <p>Applicants and license holders for children's residential facilities and foster residence settings must submit a background study request to the commissioner using the electronic system known as NETStudy 2.0:</p>
          <p>(1) before the commissioner issues a license to an applicant;</p>
          <p>(2) before an individual age 13 or older, who is not currently receiving services from the licensed facility or setting, may live in the licensed program or setting;</p>
          <p>(3) before a volunteer has unsupervised direct contact with persons that the program serves;</p>
          <p>(4) before an individual becomes a controlling individual as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.02">245A.02</a>, subdivision 5a;</p>
          <p>(5) before an adult, regardless of whether or not the individual will have direct contact with persons served by the facility, begins working in the facility or setting;</p>
          <p>(6) when directed to by the commissioner for an individual who resides in the household as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.1">245C.03, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clause (5); and</p>
          <p>(7) when directed to by the commissioner for an individual who may have unsupervised access to children or vulnerable adults as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.1">245C.03, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clause (6).</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.04.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.4.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.3.6.0">2007 c 147 art 3 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/79/#laws.1.8.0">2009 c 79 art 1 s 8</a>; art 6 s 4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/142/#laws.2.23.0">2009 c 142 art 2 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/173/#laws.1.5.0">2009 c 173 art 1 s 5</a>,6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/216/#laws.16.17.0">2012 c 216 art 16 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/247/#laws.4.11.0">2012 c 247 art 4 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/108/#laws.5.3.0">2013 c 108 art 5 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/228/#laws.5.1.0">2014 c 228 art 5 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/250/#laws.0.7.0">2014 c 250 s 7</a>,8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/262/#laws.4.9.0">2014 c 262 art 4 s 9; </a>art 5 s 3,6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/291/#laws.1.5.0">2014 c 291 art 1 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.26.2.0">2014 c 312 art 26 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/71/#laws.1.5.0">2015 c 71 art 1 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/78/#laws.4.34.0">2015 c 78 art 4 s 34</a>-36; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/90/#laws.0.3.0">2017 c 90 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.16.21.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 16 s 21</a>,22; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/166/#laws.0.7.0">2018 c 166 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/115/#laws.4.80.0">2020 c 115 art 4 s 80</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.5.11.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 5 s 11</a>,12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/55/#laws.1.126.0">2022 c 55 art 1 s 126</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/58/#laws.0.101.0">2022 c 58 s 101</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/25/#laws.0.126.0">2023 c 25 s 126</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.9.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/80/#laws.5.7.0">2024 c 80 art 5 s 7</a></p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>The amendment to subdivision 1 by Laws 2023, chapter 70, article 7, section 9, is effective April 28, 2025. Laws 2023, chapter 70, article 7, section 9, the effective date.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.041">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.041 EMERGENCY WAIVER TO TEMPORARILY MODIFY BACKGROUND STUDY REQUIREMENTS.</h1>
        <p>(a) In the event of an emergency identified by the commissioner, the commissioner may temporarily waive or modify provisions in this chapter, except that the commissioner shall not waive or modify:</p>
        <p>(1) disqualification standards in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15</a>; or</p>
        <p>(2) any provision regarding the scope of individuals required to be subject to a background study conducted under this chapter.</p>
        <p>(b) For the purposes of this section, an emergency may include but is not limited to a public health emergency, environmental emergency, natural disaster, or other unplanned event that the commissioner has determined prevents the requirements in this chapter from being met. This authority shall not exceed the amount of time needed to respond to the emergency and reinstate the requirements of this chapter. The commissioner has the authority to establish the process and time frame for returning to full compliance with this chapter. The commissioner shall determine the length of time an emergency study is valid.</p>
        <p>(c) At the conclusion of the emergency, entities must submit a new, compliant background study application and fee for each individual who was the subject of background study affected by the powers created in this section, referred to as an "emergency study" to have a new study that fully complies with this chapter within a time frame and notice period established by the commissioner.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.041.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.62.15.0">2024 c 127 art 62 s 15</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.05">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.05 BACKGROUND STUDY; INFORMATION AND DATA PROVIDED TO COMMISSIONER.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.05.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Individual studied.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The individual who is the subject of the background study must provide the applicant, license holder, or other entity under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.04">245C.04</a> with sufficient information to ensure an accurate study, including:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the individual's first, middle, and last name and all other names by which the individual has been known;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) current home address, city, and state of residence;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) current zip code;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) sex;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) date of birth;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) driver's license number or state identification number or, for those without a driver's license or state identification card, an acceptable form of identification as determined by the commissioner; and</p>
          <p class="in">(7) upon implementation of NETStudy 2.0, the home address, city, county, and state of residence for the past five years.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Every subject of a background study conducted or initiated by counties or private agencies under this chapter must also provide the home address, city, county, and state of residence for the past five years.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Every subject of a background study related to private agency adoptions or related to child foster care licensed through a private agency, who is 18 years of age or older, shall also provide the commissioner a signed consent for the release of any information received from national crime information databases to the private agency that initiated the background study.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The subject of a background study shall provide fingerprints and a photograph as required in subdivision 5.</p>
          <p>(e) The subject of a background study shall submit a completed criminal and maltreatment history records check consent form and criminal history disclosure form for applicable national and state level record checks.</p>
          <p>(f) A background study subject who has access to the NETStudy 2.0 applicant portal must provide updated contact information to the commissioner via NETStudy 2.0 any time the subject's personal information changes for as long as they remain affiliated on any roster.</p>
          <p>(g) An entity must update contact information in NETStudy 2.0 for a background study subject on the entity's roster any time the entity receives new contact information from the study subject.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.05.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Applicant, license holder, or other entity.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The applicant, license holder, or other entity initiating the background study as provided in this chapter shall verify that the information collected under subdivision 1 about an individual who is the subject of the background study is correct and must provide the information on forms or in a format prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(b) The information collected under subdivision 1 about an individual who is the subject of a completed background study may only be viewable by an entity that initiates a subsequent background study on that individual under NETStudy 2.0 after the entity has paid the applicable fee for the study and has provided the individual with the privacy notice in subdivision 2c.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.05.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">County or private agency.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">For background studies related to child foster care when the applicant or license holder resides in the home where child foster care services are provided, county and private agencies initiating the background study must collect the information under subdivision 1 and forward it to the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.05.2b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.<span class="headnote">County agency to collect and forward information to commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For background studies related to all family adult day services and to adult foster care when the adult foster care license holder resides in the adult foster care residence, the county agency or private agency initiating the background study must collect the information required under subdivision 1 and forward it to the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(b) Upon implementation of NETStudy 2.0, for background studies related to family child care and legal nonlicensed child care authorized under chapter 142E, the county agency initiating the background study must collect the information required under subdivision 1 and provide the information to the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.05.2c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2c.<span class="headnote">Privacy notice to background study subject.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Prior to initiating each background study, the entity initiating the study must provide the commissioner's privacy notice to the background study subject required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.04#stat.13.04.2">13.04, subdivision 2</a>. The notice must be available through the commissioner's electronic NETStudy and NETStudy 2.0 systems and shall include the information in paragraphs (b) and (c).</p>
          <p>(b) The background study subject shall be informed that any previous background studies that received a set-aside will be reviewed, and without further contact with the background study subject, the commissioner may notify the agency that initiated the subsequent background study that the individual has a disqualification that has been set aside for the program or agency that initiated the study.</p>
          <p>(c) The background study subject must also be informed that:</p>
          <p>(1) the subject's fingerprints collected for purposes of completing the background study under this chapter must not be retained by the Department of Public Safety, Bureau of Criminal Apprehension, or by the commissioner. The Federal Bureau of Investigation will not retain background study subjects' fingerprints;</p>
          <p>(2) effective upon implementation of NETStudy 2.0, the subject's photographic image will be retained by the commissioner, and if the subject has provided the subject's Social Security number for purposes of the background study, the photographic image will be available to prospective employers and agencies initiating background studies under this chapter to verify the identity of the subject of the background study;</p>
          <p>(3) the authorized fingerprint collection vendor or vendors shall, for purposes of verifying the identity of the background study subject, be able to view the identifying information entered into NETStudy 2.0 by the entity that initiated the background study, but shall not retain the subject's fingerprints, photograph, or information from NETStudy 2.0. The authorized fingerprint collection vendor or vendors shall retain no more than the subject's name and the date and time the subject's fingerprints were recorded and sent, only as necessary for auditing and billing activities;</p>
          <p>(4) the commissioner shall provide the subject notice, as required in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17">245C.17</a>, subdivision 1, paragraph (a), when an entity initiates a background study on the individual;</p>
          <p>(5) the subject may request in writing a report listing the entities that initiated a background study on the individual as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17#stat.245C.17.1">245C.17, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b);</p>
          <p>(6) the subject may request in writing that information used to complete the individual's background study in NETStudy 2.0 be destroyed if the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.051">245C.051</a>, paragraph (a), are met; and</p>
          <p>(7) notwithstanding clause (6), the commissioner shall destroy:</p>
          <p>(i) the subject's photograph after a period of two years when the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.051">245C.051</a>, paragraph (c), are met; and</p>
          <p>(ii) any data collected on a subject under this chapter after a period of two years following the individual's death as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.051">245C.051</a>, paragraph (d).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.05.2d">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2d.<span class="headnote">Fingerprint data notification.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of human services shall notify all background study subjects under this chapter that the Department of Human Services, Department of Public Safety, and the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension do not retain fingerprint data after a background study is completed, and that the Federal Bureau of Investigation does not retain background study subjects' fingerprints.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.05.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Additional information from individual studied.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) For purposes of completing the background study, the commissioner may request the individual's Social Security number or race. The individual is not required to provide this information to the commissioner.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The commissioner may also require additional information if the commissioner determines the information is necessary to complete the background study. Failure to provide the required information may result in a disqualification pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.09">245C.09</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.05.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Electronic transmission.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) For background studies conducted by the Department of Human Services, the commissioner shall implement a secure system for the electronic transmission of:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) background study information to the commissioner;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) background study results to the license holder;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) background study information obtained under this section and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08">245C.08 </a>to counties and private agencies for background studies conducted by the commissioner for child foster care, including a summary of nondisqualifying results, except as prohibited by law; and</p>
          <p>(4) background study results to county agencies for background studies conducted by the commissioner for adult foster care and family adult day services and, upon implementation of NETStudy 2.0, family child care.</p>
          <p>(b) Unless the commissioner has granted a hardship variance under paragraph (c), a license holder or an applicant must use the electronic transmission system known as NETStudy or NETStudy 2.0 to submit all requests for background studies to the commissioner as required by this chapter.</p>
          <p>(c) A license holder or applicant whose program is located in an area in which high-speed Internet is inaccessible may request the commissioner to grant a variance to the electronic transmission requirement.</p>
          <p>(d) Section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08#stat.245C.08.3">245C.08, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (c), applies to results transmitted under this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(e) The background study subject shall access background study-related documents electronically in the applicant portal. A background study subject may request for the commissioner to grant a variance to the requirement to access documents electronically in the NETStudy 2.0 applicant portal and may also request paper documentation of their background studies.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.05.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Fingerprints and photograph.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Notwithstanding paragraph (c), for background studies conducted by the commissioner for child foster care, children's residential facilities, adoptions, or a transfer of permanent legal and physical custody of a child, the subject of the background study, who is 18 years of age or older, shall provide the commissioner with a set of classifiable fingerprints obtained from an authorized agency for a national criminal history record check.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Notwithstanding paragraph (c), for background studies conducted by the commissioner for Head Start programs, the subject of the background study shall provide the commissioner with a set of classifiable fingerprints obtained from an authorized agency for a national criminal history record check.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) For background studies initiated on or after the implementation of NETStudy 2.0, except as provided under subdivision 5a, every subject of a background study must provide the commissioner with a set of the background study subject's classifiable fingerprints and photograph. The photograph and fingerprints must be recorded at the same time by the authorized fingerprint collection vendor or vendors and sent to the commissioner through the commissioner's secure data system described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.32#stat.245C.32.1a">245C.32, subdivision 1a</a>, paragraph (b).</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The fingerprints shall be submitted by the commissioner to the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension and, when specifically required by law, submitted to the Federal Bureau of Investigation for a national criminal history record check.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) The fingerprints must not be retained by the Department of Public Safety, Bureau of Criminal Apprehension, or the commissioner. The Federal Bureau of Investigation will not retain background study subjects' fingerprints.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) The authorized fingerprint collection vendor or vendors shall, for purposes of verifying the identity of the background study subject, be able to view the identifying information entered into NETStudy 2.0 by the entity that initiated the background study, but shall not retain the subject's fingerprints, photograph, or information from NETStudy 2.0. The authorized fingerprint collection vendor or vendors shall retain no more than the name and date and time the subject's fingerprints were recorded and sent, only as necessary for auditing and billing activities.</p>
          <p>(g) For any background study conducted under this chapter, the subject shall provide the commissioner with a set of classifiable fingerprints when the commissioner has reasonable cause to require a national criminal history record check as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02">245C.02</a>, subdivision 15a.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.05.5a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5a.<span class="headnote">Background study requirements for minors.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A background study completed under this chapter on a subject who is required to be studied under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.1">245C.03, subdivision 1</a>, and is 17 years of age or younger shall be completed by the commissioner for:</p>
          <p>(1) a legal nonlicensed child care provider authorized under chapter 142E;</p>
          <p>(2) a licensed family child care program; or</p>
          <p>(3) a licensed foster care home.</p>
          <p>(b) The subject shall submit to the commissioner only the information under subdivision 1, paragraph (a).</p>
          <p>(c) A subject who is 17 years of age or younger is required to submit fingerprints and a photograph, and the commissioner shall conduct a national criminal history record check, if:</p>
          <p>(1) the commissioner has reasonable cause to require a national criminal history record check defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02#stat.245C.02.15a">245C.02, subdivision 15a</a>; or</p>
          <p>(2) under paragraph (a), clauses (1) and (2), the subject is employed by the provider or supervises children served by the program.</p>
          <p>(d) A subject who is 17 years of age or younger is required to submit non-fingerprint-based data according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08#stat.245C.08.1">245C.08, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clause (6), item (iii), and the commissioner shall conduct the check if:</p>
          <p>(1) the commissioner has reasonable cause to require a national criminal history record check defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02#stat.245C.02.15a">245C.02, subdivision 15a</a>; or</p>
          <p>(2) the subject is employed by the provider or supervises children served by the program under paragraph (a), clauses (1) and (2).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.05.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Applicant, license holder, other entities, and agencies.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The applicant, license holder, other entities as provided in this chapter, Bureau of Criminal Apprehension, law enforcement agencies, commissioner of health, and county agencies shall help with the study by giving the commissioner criminal conviction data and reports about the maltreatment of adults substantiated under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a> and the maltreatment of minors substantiated under chapter 260E.</p>
          <p>(b) If a background study is initiated by an applicant, license holder, or other entities as provided in this chapter, and the applicant, license holder, or other entity receives information about the possible criminal or maltreatment history of an individual who is the subject of the background study, the applicant, license holder, or other entity must immediately provide the information to the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(c) The program or county or other agency must provide written notice to the individual who is the subject of the background study of the requirements under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.05.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Probation officer and corrections agent.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) A probation officer or corrections agent shall notify the commissioner of an individual's conviction if the individual:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) has been affiliated with a program or facility regulated by the Department of Human Services or Department of Health, a facility serving children or youth licensed by the Department of Corrections, or any type of home care agency or provider of personal care assistance services within the preceding year; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) has been convicted of a crime constituting a disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) For the purpose of this subdivision, "conviction" has the meaning given it in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.02#stat.609.02.5">609.02, subdivision 5</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The commissioner, in consultation with the commissioner of corrections, shall develop forms and information necessary to implement this subdivision and shall provide the forms and information to the commissioner of corrections for distribution to local probation officers and corrections agents.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The commissioner shall inform individuals subject to a background study that criminal convictions for disqualifying crimes shall be reported to the commissioner by the corrections system.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) A probation officer, corrections agent, or corrections agency is not civilly or criminally liable for disclosing or failing to disclose the information required by this subdivision.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) Upon receipt of disqualifying information, the commissioner shall provide the notice required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17">245C.17</a>, as appropriate, to agencies on record as having initiated a background study or making a request for documentation of the background study status of the individual.</p>
          <p class="in">(g) This subdivision does not apply to family child care programs or legal nonlicensed child care programs for individuals whose background study was completed in NETStudy 2.0.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.05.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Study submitted.</span></h2>
          <p>The entity with which the background study subject is seeking affiliation shall initiate the background study in the NETStudy 2.0 system.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.05.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.5.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/14/#laws.6.5.0">1Sp2003 c 14 art 6 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.41.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 41</a>-44; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.27.0">2007 c 112 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.3.7.0">2007 c 147 art 3 s 7</a>-11; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/79/#laws.1.9.0">2009 c 79 art 1 s 9</a>,10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/173/#laws.1.7.0">2009 c 173 art 1 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/216/#laws.16.18.0">2012 c 216 art 16 s 18</a>-21; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/247/#laws.4.12.0">2012 c 247 art 4 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/228/#laws.5.2.0">2014 c 228 art 5 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/250/#laws.0.9.0">2014 c 250 s 9</a>-13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/262/#laws.5.4.0">2014 c 262 art 5 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.25.3.0">2014 c 312 art 25 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/78/#laws.4.37.0">2015 c 78 art 4 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/90/#laws.0.4.0">2017 c 90 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.16.23.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 16 s 23</a>-26; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/166/#laws.0.8.0">2018 c 166 s 8</a>-10,15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/9/#laws.2.66.0">1Sp2019 c 9 art 2 s 66</a>-68; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.5.13.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 5 s 13</a>; art 8 s 50; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.20.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 20</a>-27; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.10.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 10</a>-12; art 17 s 30; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/80/#laws.5.7.0">2024 c 80 art 5 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.62.16.0">2024 c 127 art 62 s 16</a></p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>The amendment to subdivision 4, paragraph (a), clause (4), by Laws 2023, chapter 70, article 7, section 12, is effective April 28, 2025. Laws 2023, chapter 70, article 7, section 12, the effective date.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.051">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.051 DESTRUCTION OF BACKGROUND STUDY SUBJECT INFORMATION.</h1>
        <p>(a) A background study subject may request in writing to the commissioner that information used to complete the individual's study in NETStudy 2.0 be destroyed if the individual:</p>
        <p>(1) has not been affiliated with any entity for the previous two years; and</p>
        <p>(2) has no current disqualifying characteristic.</p>
        <p>(b) After receiving the request and verifying the information in paragraph (a), the commissioner shall destroy the information used to complete the subject's background study and shall keep a record of the subject's name and a notation of the date that the information was destroyed.</p>
        <p>(c) When a previously studied individual has not been on the master roster for two years, the commissioner shall destroy the photographic image of the individual obtained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.5">245C.05, subdivision 5</a>, paragraph (c).</p>
        <p>(d) Any data collected on an individual under this chapter that is maintained by the commissioner that has not been destroyed according to paragraph (b) or (c) shall be destroyed when two years have elapsed from the individual's actual death that is reported to the commissioner or when 90 years have elapsed since the individual's birth except when readily available data indicate that the individual is still living.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.051.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/250/#laws.0.14.0">2014 c 250 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/166/#laws.0.11.0">2018 c 166 s 11</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245C.06"><b>245C.06</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.59.0">2007 c 112 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.07">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.07 STUDY SUBJECT AFFILIATED WITH MULTIPLE FACILITIES.</h1>
        <p class="in">(a) Subject to the conditions in paragraph (d), when a license holder, applicant, or other entity owns multiple programs or services that are licensed by the Department of Human Services, Department of Health, or Department of Corrections, only one background study is required for an individual who provides direct contact services in one or more of the licensed programs or services if:</p>
        <p class="in">(1) the license holder designates one individual with one address and telephone number as the person to receive sensitive background study information for the multiple licensed programs or services that depend on the same background study; and</p>
        <p class="in">(2) the individual designated to receive the sensitive background study information is capable of determining, upon request of the department, whether a background study subject is providing direct contact services in one or more of the license holder's programs or services and, if so, at which location or locations.</p>
        <p class="in">(b) When a license holder maintains background study compliance for multiple licensed programs according to paragraph (a), and one or more of the licensed programs closes, the license holder shall immediately notify the commissioner which staff must be transferred to an active license so that the background studies can be electronically paired with the license holder's active program.</p>
        <p class="in">(c) When a background study is being initiated by a licensed program or service or a foster care provider that is also licensed under chapter 144G, a study subject affiliated with multiple licensed programs or services may attach to the background study form a cover letter indicating the additional names of the programs or services, addresses, and background study identification numbers.</p>
        <p class="in">When the commissioner receives a notice, the commissioner shall notify each program or service identified by the background study subject of the study results.</p>
        <p class="in">The background study notice the commissioner sends to the subsequent agencies shall satisfy those programs' or services' responsibilities for initiating a background study on that individual.</p>
        <p>(d) If a background study was conducted on an individual related to child foster care and the requirements under paragraph (a) are met, the background study is transferable across all licensed programs. If a background study was conducted on an individual under a license other than child foster care and the requirements under paragraph (a) are met, the background study is transferable to all licensed programs except child foster care.</p>
        <p>(e) The provisions of this section that allow a single background study in one or more licensed programs or services do not apply to background studies submitted by adoption agencies, supplemental nursing services agencies, personnel pool agencies, educational programs, professional services agencies, temporary personnel agencies, and unlicensed personal care provider organizations.</p>
        <p>(f) For an entity operating under NETStudy 2.0, the entity's active roster must be the system used to document when a background study subject is affiliated with multiple entities. For a background study to be transferable:</p>
        <p>(1) the background study subject must be on and moving to a roster for which the person designated to receive sensitive background study information is the same; and</p>
        <p>(2) the same entity must own or legally control both the roster from which the transfer is occurring and the roster to which the transfer is occurring. For an entity that holds or controls multiple licenses, or unlicensed personal care provider organizations, there must be a common highest level entity that has a legally identifiable structure that can be verified through records available from the secretary of state.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.07.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.7.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/4/#laws.1.27.0">1Sp2005 c 4 art 1 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.28.0">2007 c 112 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/142/#laws.2.24.0">2009 c 142 art 2 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/216/#laws.16.22.0">2012 c 216 art 16 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/250/#laws.0.15.0">2014 c 250 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/78/#laws.4.38.0">2015 c 78 art 4 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/7/1/#laws.6.25.0">7Sp2020 c 1 art 6 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.17.31.0">2023 c 70 art 17 s 31</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.08">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.08 BACKGROUND STUDY; COMMISSIONER REVIEWS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.08.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Background studies conducted by Department of Human Services.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) For a background study conducted by the Department of Human Services, the commissioner shall review:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) information related to names of substantiated perpetrators of maltreatment of vulnerable adults that has been received by the commissioner as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557#stat.626.557.9c">626.557, subdivision 9c</a>, paragraph (j);</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the commissioner's records relating to the maltreatment of minors in licensed programs, and from findings of maltreatment of minors as indicated through the social service information system;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) information from juvenile courts as required for studies under this chapter when there is reasonable cause;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) information from the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension, including information regarding a background study subject's registration in Minnesota as a predatory offender under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/243.166">243.166</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) except as provided in clause (6), information received as a result of submission of fingerprints for a national criminal history record check, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02">245C.02</a>, subdivision 13c, when the commissioner has reasonable cause for a national criminal history record check as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.02#stat.245C.02.15a">245C.02, subdivision 15a</a>, or as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.057#stat.144.057.1">144.057, subdivision 1</a>, clause (2);</p>
          <p class="in">(6) for a background study related to a child foster family setting application for licensure, foster residence settings, children's residential facilities, a transfer of permanent legal and physical custody of a child under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.503">260C.503</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.515">260C.515</a>, or adoptions, and for a background study required for family child care, certified license-exempt child care, child care centers, and legal nonlicensed child care authorized under chapter 142E, the commissioner shall also review:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) information from the child abuse and neglect registry for any state in which the background study subject has resided for the past five years;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) when the background study subject is 18 years of age or older, or a minor under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.5a">245C.05, subdivision 5a</a>, paragraph (c), information received following submission of fingerprints for a national criminal history record check; and</p>
          <p>(iii) when the background study subject is 18 years of age or older or a minor under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05#stat.245C.05.5a">245C.05, subdivision 5a</a>, paragraph (d), for licensed family child care, certified license-exempt child care, licensed child care centers, and legal nonlicensed child care authorized under chapter 142E, information obtained using non-fingerprint-based data including information from the criminal and sex offender registries for any state in which the background study subject resided for the past five years and information from the national crime information database and the national sex offender registry;</p>
          <p>(7) for a background study required for family child care, certified license-exempt child care centers, licensed child care centers, and legal nonlicensed child care authorized under chapter 142E, the background study shall also include, to the extent practicable, a name and date-of-birth search of the National Sex Offender Public website; and</p>
          <p>(8) for a background study required for treatment programs for sexual psychopathic personalities or sexually dangerous persons, the background study shall only include a review of the information required under paragraph (a), clauses (1) to (4).</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Except as otherwise provided in this paragraph, notwithstanding expungement by a court, the commissioner may consider information obtained under paragraph (a), clauses (3) and (4), unless:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the commissioner received notice of the petition for expungement and the court order for expungement is directed specifically to the commissioner; or</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the commissioner received notice of the expungement order issued pursuant to section 609A.017, 609A.025, or 609A.035, and the order for expungement is directed specifically to the commissioner.</p>
          <p class="bl">The commissioner may not consider information obtained under paragraph (a), clauses (3) and (4), or from any other source that identifies a violation of chapter 152 without determining if the offense involved the possession of marijuana or tetrahydrocannabinol and, if so, whether the person received a grant of expungement or order of expungement, or the person was resentenced to a lesser offense. If the person received a grant of expungement or order of expungement, the commissioner may not consider information related to that violation but may consider any other relevant information arising out of the same incident.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The commissioner shall also review criminal case information received according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.04#stat.245C.04.4a">245C.04, subdivision 4a</a>, from the Minnesota court information system that relates to individuals who have already been studied under this chapter and who remain affiliated with the agency that initiated the background study.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) When the commissioner has reasonable cause to believe that the identity of a background study subject is uncertain, the commissioner may require the subject to provide a set of classifiable fingerprints for purposes of completing a fingerprint-based record check with the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension. Fingerprints collected under this paragraph shall not be saved by the commissioner after they have been used to verify the identity of the background study subject against the particular criminal record in question.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) The commissioner may inform the entity that initiated a background study under NETStudy 2.0 of the status of processing of the subject's fingerprints.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.08.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>MS 2023 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.62.53.0">2024 c 127 art 62 s 53</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.08.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Arrest and investigative information.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For any background study completed under this section, if the commissioner has reasonable cause to believe the information is pertinent to the disqualification of an individual, the commissioner also may review arrest and investigative information from:</p>
          <p>(1) the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension;</p>
          <p>(2) the commissioners of children, youth, and families; health; and human services;</p>
          <p>(3) a county attorney;</p>
          <p>(4) a county sheriff;</p>
          <p>(5) a county agency;</p>
          <p>(6) a local chief of police;</p>
          <p>(7) other states;</p>
          <p>(8) the courts;</p>
          <p>(9) the Federal Bureau of Investigation;</p>
          <p>(10) the National Criminal Records Repository; and</p>
          <p>(11) criminal records from other states.</p>
          <p>(b) Except when specifically required by law, the commissioner is not required to conduct more than one review of a subject's records from the Federal Bureau of Investigation if a review of the subject's criminal history with the Federal Bureau of Investigation has already been completed by the commissioner and there has been no break in the subject's affiliation with the entity that initiated the background study.</p>
          <p>(c) If the commissioner conducts a national criminal history record check when required by law and uses the information from the national criminal history record check to make a disqualification determination, the data obtained is private data and cannot be shared with private agencies or prospective employers of the background study subject.</p>
          <p>(d) If the commissioner conducts a national criminal history record check when required by law and uses the information from the national criminal history record check to make a disqualification determination, the license holder or entity that submitted the study is not required to obtain a copy of the background study subject's disqualification letter under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17#stat.245C.17.3">245C.17, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.08.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Juvenile court records.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) For a background study conducted by the Department of Human Services, the commissioner shall review records from the juvenile courts for an individual studied under this chapter when the commissioner has reasonable cause.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The juvenile courts shall help with the study by giving the commissioner existing juvenile court records relating to delinquency proceedings held on individuals who are subjects of studies under this chapter when requested pursuant to this subdivision.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) For purposes of this chapter, a finding that a delinquency petition is proven in juvenile court shall be considered a conviction in state district court.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) Juvenile courts shall provide orders of involuntary and voluntary termination of parental rights under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.301">260C.301</a> to the commissioner upon request for purposes of conducting a background study under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.08.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Authorization.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of human services shall be authorized to receive information under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.08.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.8.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/14/#laws.6.5.0">1Sp2003 c 14 art 6 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.45.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 45</a>-47; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/4/#laws.1.28.0">1Sp2005 c 4 art 1 s 28</a>,29; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.29.0">2007 c 112 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.3.12.0">2007 c 147 art 3 s 12</a>,13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/361/#laws.5.1.0">2008 c 361 art 5 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/79/#laws.1.11.0">2009 c 79 art 1 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/142/#laws.2.25.0">2009 c 142 art 2 s 25</a>; art 3 s 2-4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/108/#laws.5.4.0">2013 c 108 art 5 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/228/#laws.5.3.0">2014 c 228 art 5 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.25.4.0">2014 c 312 art 25 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/71/#laws.7.8.0">2015 c 71 art 7 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.16.27.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 16 s 27</a>-29;
