<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/166/#laws.0.12.0">2018 c 166 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/9/#laws.2.69.0">1Sp2019 c 9 art 2 s 69</a>,70; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.5.14.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 5 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.28.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 28</a>,29; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/52/#laws.7.4.0">2023 c 52 art 7 s 4</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/63/#laws.6.43.0">2023 c 63 art 6 s 43</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.13.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/80/#laws.5.7.0">2024 c 80 art 5 s 7</a>; art 8 s 13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/115/#laws.19.12.0">2024 c 115 art 19 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.62.17.0">2024 c 127 art 62 s 17</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.09">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.09 FAILURE OR REFUSAL TO COOPERATE WITH BACKGROUND STUDY.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.09.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Disqualification; licensing action.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">An applicant's, license holder's, or other entity's failure or refusal to cooperate with the commissioner, including failure to provide additional information required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05">245C.05</a>, is reasonable cause to disqualify a subject, deny a license application, or immediately suspend or revoke a license or registration.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.09.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Employment action.</span></h2>
          <p>An individual's failure or refusal to cooperate with the background study is just cause for denying or terminating employment of the individual if the individual's failure or refusal to cooperate could cause the applicant's application to be denied or the license holder's license to be immediately suspended or revoked.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.09.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">False statement in connection with a background study.</span></h2>
          <p>A child care background study subject shall be disqualified for knowingly making a materially false statement in connection with a background study.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.09.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.9.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.48.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 48</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.30.0">2007 c 112 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.16.30.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 16 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/166/#laws.0.16.0">2018 c 166 s 16</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.10">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.10 BACKGROUND STUDY; FEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/142/#laws.2.49.0">2009 c 142 art 2 s 49</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Expenses.</span></h2>
          <p>Section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/181.645">181.645</a> does not apply to background studies completed under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.1b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.<span class="headnote">Background study fees.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall recover the cost of background studies. Except as otherwise provided in subdivisions 1c and 1d, the fees collected under this section shall be appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies under this chapter. Fees under this section are charges under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.1283">16A.1283</a>, paragraph (b), clause (3).</p>
          <p>(b) Background study fees may include:</p>
          <p>(1) a fee to compensate the commissioner's authorized fingerprint collection vendor or vendors for obtaining and processing a background study subject's classifiable fingerprints and photograph pursuant to subdivision 1c; and</p>
          <p>(2) a separate fee under subdivision 1c to complete a review of background-study-related records as authorized under this chapter.</p>
          <p>(c) Fees charged under paragraph (b) may be paid in whole or in part when authorized by law by a state agency or board; by state court administration; by a service provider, employer, license holder, or other organization that initiates the background study; by the commissioner or other organization with duly appropriated funds; by a background study subject; or by some combination of these sources.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.1c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1c.<span class="headnote">Fingerprint and photograph processing fees.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall enter into a contract with a qualified vendor or vendors to obtain and process a background study subject's classifiable fingerprints and photograph as required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05">245C.05</a>. The commissioner may, at their discretion, directly collect fees and reimburse the commissioner's authorized fingerprint collection vendor for the vendor's services or require the vendor to collect the fees. The authorized vendor is responsible for reimbursing the vendor's subcontractors at a rate specified in the contract with the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.1d">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1d.<span class="headnote">State; national criminal history record check fees.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may increase background study fees as necessary, commensurate with an increase in state Bureau of Criminal Apprehension or the national criminal history record check fees.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Supplemental nursing services agencies.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of the background studies initiated by supplemental nursing services agencies registered under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144A.71#stat.144A.71.1">144A.71, subdivision 1</a>, through a fee of no more than $44 per study charged to the agency. The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Occupations regulated by commissioner of health.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall set fees to recover the cost of combined background studies and criminal background checks initiated by applicants, licensees, and certified practitioners regulated under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.511">148.511 </a>to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.5198">148.5198</a> and chapter 153A through a fee of no more than $44 per study charged to the entity. The fees collected under this subdivision shall be deposited in the special revenue fund and are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies and criminal background checks.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Personal care provider organizations.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of background studies initiated by a personal care provider organization under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0651">256B.0651</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0654">256B.0654 </a>and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0659">256B.0659 </a>through a fee of no more than $44 per study charged to the organization responsible for submitting the background study form. The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Temporary personnel agencies, personnel pool agencies, educational programs, and professional services agencies.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The commissioner shall recover the cost of the background studies initiated by temporary personnel agencies, personnel pool agencies, educational programs, and professional services agencies that initiate background studies under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.4">245C.03, subdivision 4</a>, through a fee of no more than $44 per study charged to the agency. The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Adult foster care and family adult day services.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of background studies required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.1">245C.03, subdivision 1</a>, for the purposes of adult foster care and family adult day services licensing, through a fee of no more than $44 per study charged to the license holder. The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Unlicensed home and community-based waiver providers of service to seniors and individuals with disabilities.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of background studies initiated by unlicensed home and community-based waiver providers of service to seniors and individuals with disabilities under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.4912">256B.4912</a> through a fee of no more than $44 per study.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Private agencies.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of conducting background studies under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.33">245C.33</a> for studies initiated by private agencies for the purpose of adoption through a fee of no more than $70 per study charged to the private agency. The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Children's therapeutic services and supports providers.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of background studies required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.7">245C.03, subdivision 7</a>, for the purposes of children's therapeutic services and supports under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0943">256B.0943</a>, through a fee of no more than $44 per study charged to the license holder. The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Human services licensed programs.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of background studies required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.1">245C.03, subdivision 1</a>, for all programs that are licensed by the commissioner, except child foster care when the applicant or license holder resides in the home where child foster care services are provided, family child care, child care centers, certified license-exempt child care centers, and legal nonlicensed child care authorized under chapter 142E, through a fee of no more than $44 per study charged to the license holder. The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.9a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9a.<span class="headnote">Child care programs.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of a background study required for family child care, certified license-exempt child care centers, licensed child care centers, and legal nonlicensed child care providers authorized under chapter 142E through a fee of no more than $44 per study charged to the license holder. A fee of no more than $44 per study shall be charged for studies conducted under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05">245C.05, subdivision </a>5a, paragraph (a). The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner to conduct background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Community first services and supports organizations.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of background studies initiated by an agency-provider delivering services under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.85#stat.256B.85.11">256B.85, subdivision 11</a>, or a financial management services provider providing service functions under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.85#stat.256B.85.13">256B.85, subdivision 13</a>, through a fee of no more than $44 per study, charged to the organization responsible for submitting the background study form. The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Providers of housing support.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of background studies initiated by providers of housing support under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256I.04">256I.04</a> through a fee of no more than $44 per study. The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Child protection workers or social services staff having responsibility for child protective duties.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of background studies initiated by county social services agencies and local welfare agencies for individuals who are required to have a background study under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.36">260E.36</a>, subdivision 3, through a fee of no more than $44 per study. The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Providers of special transportation service.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of background studies initiated by providers of special transportation service under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/174.30">174.30</a> through a fee of no more than $44 per study. The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Children's residential facilities.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of background studies initiated by a licensed children's residential facility through a fee of no more than $53 per study. Fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for purposes of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Guardians and conservators.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall recover the cost of conducting maltreatment and state licensing agency checks for guardians and conservators under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.033">245C.033 </a>through a fee of no more than $50. The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting maltreatment and state licensing agency checks.</p>
          <p>(b) The fee must be paid directly to and in the manner prescribed by the commissioner before any maltreatment and state licensing agency checks under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.033">245C.033</a> may be conducted.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding paragraph (b), the court shall pay the fee for an applicant who has been granted in forma pauperis status upon receipt of the invoice from the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Providers of housing stabilization services.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of background studies initiated by providers of housing stabilization services under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.051">256B.051</a> through a fee of no more than $44 per study. The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Early intensive developmental and behavioral intervention providers.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of background studies required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03</a>, subdivision 15, for the purposes of early intensive developmental and behavioral intervention under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0949">256B.0949</a>, through a fee of no more than $44 per study charged to the enrolled agency. The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Applicants, licensees, and other occupations regulated by commissioner of health.</span></h2>
          <p>The applicant or license holder is responsible for paying to the Department of Human Services all fees associated with the preparation of the fingerprints, the criminal records check consent form, and, through a fee of no more than $44 per study, the criminal background check.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Occupations regulated by MNsure.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall set fees to recover the cost of background studies and criminal background checks initiated by MNsure under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/62V.05">62V.05</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.031">245C.031</a>. The fee amount shall be established through interagency agreement between the commissioner and the board of MNsure or its designee. The fees collected under this subdivision shall be deposited in the special revenue fund and are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies and criminal background checks.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Professional Educators Licensing Standards Board.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of background studies initiated by the Professional Educators Licensing Standards Board through a fee of no more than $53 per study. Fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for purposes of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.10.21">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.<span class="headnote">Board of School Administrators.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall recover the cost of background studies initiated by the Board of School Administrators through a fee of no more than $53 per study. Fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for purposes of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.10.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.10.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/4/#laws.5.7.0">1Sp2005 c 4 art 5 s 7</a>,8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.3.14.0">2007 c 147 art 3 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/79/#laws.1.12.0">2009 c 79 art 1 s 12</a>,13; art 6 s 5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/173/#laws.1.8.0">2009 c 173 art 1 s 8</a>,9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/9/#laws.3.3.0">1Sp2011 c 9 art 3 s 3</a>; art 4 s 8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/262/#laws.4.9.0">2014 c 262 art 4 s 9; art 5 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.26.3.0">2014 c 312 art 26 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/71/#laws.1.6.0">2015 c 71 art 1 s 6</a>,7; art 11 s 9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/78/#laws.4.39.0">2015 c 78 art 4 s 39</a>; art 6 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/90/#laws.0.5.0">2017 c 90 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.2.39.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 2 s 39</a>; art 10 s 134; art 16 s 31,32; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/166/#laws.0.13.0">2018 c 166 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/9/#laws.2.71.0">1Sp2019 c 9 art 2 s 71</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.1.12.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 1 s 12</a>; art 5 s 15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.30.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 30</a>-51; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.14.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 14</a>-33; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/80/#laws.5.7.0">2024 c 80 art 5 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/115/#laws.19.13.0">2024 c 115 art 19 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.62.18.0">2024 c 127 art 62 s 18</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.11">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.11 BACKGROUND STUDY; COUNTY AGENCIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.11.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/79/#laws.1.21.0">2009 c 79 art 1 s 21</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.11.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/79/#laws.1.21.0">2009 c 79 art 1 s 21</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.11.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Criminal history data.</span></h2>
          <p>County agencies shall have access to the criminal history data in the same manner as county licensing agencies under this chapter for purposes of background studies completed before the implementation of NETStudy 2.0 by county agencies on legal nonlicensed child care providers to determine eligibility for child care funds under chapter 142E.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.11.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Background study.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A county agency may accept a background study completed by the commissioner under this chapter in place of the background study required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.16#stat.245A.16.3">245A.16, subdivision 3</a>, for educational programs that train individuals by providing direct contact services in licensed programs.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.11.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.11.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.31.0">2007 c 112 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.3.15.0">2007 c 147 art 3 s 15</a>,16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.16.33.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 16 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/80/#laws.5.7.0">2024 c 80 art 5 s 7</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.12">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.12 BACKGROUND STUDY; TRIBAL ORGANIZATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.12.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Access to data.</span></h2>
          <p class="in"> For the purposes of background studies completed by tribal organizations performing licensing activities otherwise required of the commissioner under this chapter, after obtaining consent from the background study subject, tribal licensing agencies shall have access to criminal history data in the same manner as county licensing agencies and private licensing agencies under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.12.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Adoptions; child foster care.</span></h2>
          <p class="in"> Tribal organizations may contract with the commissioner to obtain background study data on individuals under tribal jurisdiction related to adoptions according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.34">245C.34</a>. Tribal organizations may also contract with the commissioner to obtain background study data on individuals under tribal jurisdiction related to child foster care according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.34">245C.34</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.12.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Nursing facility.</span></h2>
          <p class="in"> For the purposes of background studies completed to comply with a tribal organization's licensing requirements for individuals affiliated with a tribally licensed nursing facility, the commissioner shall obtain criminal history data from the National Criminal Records Repository in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.32">245C.32</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.12.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Child care.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Tribal organizations may contract with the commissioner to:</p>
          <p>(1) conduct background studies on individuals affiliated with a child care program sponsored, managed, or licensed by a tribal organization; and</p>
          <p>(2) obtain background study data on individuals affiliated with a child care program sponsored, managed, or licensed by a tribal organization.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner must include a national criminal history record check in a background study conducted under paragraph (a).</p>
          <p>(c) A tribally affiliated child care program that does not contract with the commissioner to conduct background studies is exempt from the relevant requirements in this chapter. For a background study conducted under this subdivision to be transferable to other child care entities, the study must include all components of studies for a certified license-exempt child care center under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.12.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.12.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.3.17.0">2007 c 147 art 3 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/71/#laws.7.9.0">2015 c 71 art 7 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/11/#laws.8.9.0">1Sp2019 c 11 art 8 s 9</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245C.125"><b>245C.125</b> MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.62.53.0">2024 c 127 art 62 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.13">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.13 BACKGROUND STUDY PROCESSING.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.13.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Completion of background study.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon receipt of the background study forms from an applicant, license holder, or other entity as provided in this chapter required to initiate a background study under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.04">245C.04</a>, the commissioner shall complete the background study and provide the notice required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17#stat.245C.17.1">245C.17, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.13.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Activities pending completion of background study.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The subject of a background study may not perform any activity requiring a background study under paragraph (c) until the commissioner has issued one of the notices under paragraph (a).</p>
          <p class="in">(a) Notices from the commissioner required prior to activity under paragraph (c) include:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) a notice of the study results under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17">245C.17</a> stating that:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) the individual is not disqualified; or</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) more time is needed to complete the study but the individual is not required to be removed from direct contact or access to people receiving services prior to completion of the study as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17#stat.245C.17.1">245C.17, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b) or (c). The notice that more time is needed to complete the study must also indicate whether the individual is required to be under continuous direct supervision prior to completion of the background study. When more time is necessary to complete a background study of an individual affiliated with a Title IV-E eligible children's residential facility or foster residence setting, the individual may not work in the facility or setting regardless of whether or not the individual is supervised;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) a notice that a disqualification has been set aside under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.23">245C.23</a>; or</p>
          <p class="in">(3) a notice that a variance has been granted related to the individual under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.30">245C.30</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) For a background study affiliated with a licensed child care center or certified license-exempt child care center, the notice sent under paragraph (a), clause (1), item (ii), must require the individual to be under continuous direct supervision prior to completion of the background study except as permitted in subdivision 3.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Activities prohibited prior to receipt of notice under paragraph (a) include:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) being issued a license;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) living in the household where the licensed program will be provided;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) providing direct contact services to persons served by a program unless the subject is under continuous direct supervision;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) having access to persons receiving services if the background study was completed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.057#stat.144.057.1">144.057, subdivision 1</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.1">245C.03, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clause (2), (5), or (6), unless the subject is under continuous direct supervision;</p>
          <p>(5) for licensed child care centers and certified license-exempt child care centers, providing direct contact services to persons served by the program;</p>
          <p>(6) for children's residential facilities or foster residence settings, working in the facility or setting; or</p>
          <p>(7) for background studies affiliated with a personal care provider organization, except as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.3b">245C.03, subdivision 3b</a>, before a personal care assistant provides services, the personal care assistance provider agency must initiate a background study of the personal care assistant under this chapter and the personal care assistance provider agency must have received a notice from the commissioner that the personal care assistant is:</p>
          <p>(i) not disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a>; or</p>
          <p>(ii) disqualified, but the personal care assistant has received a set aside of the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.13.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Other state information.</span></h2>
          <p>If the commissioner has not received criminal, sex offender, or maltreatment information from another state that is required to be reviewed under this chapter within ten days of requesting the information, and the lack of the information is the only reason that a notice is issued under subdivision 2, paragraph (a), clause (1), item (ii), the commissioner may issue a notice under subdivision 2, paragraph (a), clause (1), item (i). The commissioner may take action on information received from other states after issuing a notice under subdivision 2, paragraph (a), clause (1), item (ii).</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.13.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.13.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.49.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 49</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/136/#laws.6.1.0">2005 c 136 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.32.0">2007 c 112 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/142/#laws.2.26.0">2009 c 142 art 2 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/250/#laws.0.16.0">2014 c 250 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/9/#laws.2.72.0">1Sp2019 c 9 art 2 s 72</a>,73; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.5.16.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 5 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.52.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 52</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.14">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.14 DISQUALIFICATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.14.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Disqualification from direct contact.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The commissioner shall disqualify an individual who is the subject of a background study from any position allowing direct contact with persons receiving services from the license holder or entity identified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03</a>, upon receipt of information showing, or when a background study completed under this chapter shows any of the following:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) a conviction of, admission to, or Alford plea to one or more crimes listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15</a>, regardless of whether the conviction or admission is a felony, gross misdemeanor, or misdemeanor level crime;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) a preponderance of the evidence indicates the individual has committed an act or acts that meet the definition of any of the crimes listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15</a>, regardless of whether the preponderance of the evidence is for a felony, gross misdemeanor, or misdemeanor level crime;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) an investigation results in an administrative determination listed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15#stat.245C.15.4">245C.15, subdivision 4</a>, paragraph (b); or</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the individual's parental rights have been terminated under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.301">260C.301</a>, subdivision 1, paragraph (b), or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.301#stat.260C.301.3">260C.301, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) No individual who is disqualified following a background study under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03</a>, subdivisions 1 and 2, may be retained in a position involving direct contact with persons served by a program or entity identified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03</a>, unless the commissioner has provided written notice under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17">245C.17</a> stating that:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the individual may remain in direct contact during the period in which the individual may request reconsideration as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.21#stat.245C.21.2">245C.21, subdivision 2</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the commissioner has set aside the individual's disqualification for that program or entity identified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03</a>, as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22#stat.245C.22.4">245C.22, subdivision 4</a>; or</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the license holder has been granted a variance for the disqualified individual under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.30">245C.30</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), for the purposes of a background study affiliated with a licensed family foster setting, the commissioner shall disqualify an individual who is the subject of a background study from any position allowing direct contact with persons receiving services from the license holder or entity identified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03</a>, upon receipt of information showing or when a background study completed under this chapter shows reason for disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15#stat.245C.15.4a">245C.15, subdivision 4a</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.14.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Disqualification from access.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If an individual who is studied under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.1">245C.03, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clauses (2), (5), and (6), is disqualified from direct contact under subdivision 1, the commissioner shall also disqualify the individual from access to a person receiving services from the license holder.</p>
          <p>(b) No individual who is disqualified following a background study under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.1">245C.03, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clauses (2), (5), and (6), or as provided elsewhere in statute who is disqualified as a result of this section, may be allowed access to persons served by the program unless the commissioner has provided written notice under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17">245C.17</a> stating that:</p>
          <p>(1) the individual may remain in direct contact during the period in which the individual may request reconsideration as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.21#stat.245C.21.2">245C.21, subdivision 2</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) the commissioner has set aside the individual's disqualification for that licensed program or entity identified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03</a> as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22#stat.245C.22.4">245C.22, subdivision 4</a>; or</p>
          <p>(3) the license holder has been granted a variance for the disqualified individual under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.30">245C.30</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.14.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Disqualification from working in children's residential facilities and foster residence settings.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For a background study affiliated with a children's residential facility or foster residence setting, if an individual is disqualified from direct contact under subdivision 1, the commissioner must also disqualify the individual from working in the children's residential facility or foster residence setting and from having access to a person receiving services from the facility or setting.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding any other requirement of this chapter, for a background study affiliated with a Title IV-E eligible children's residential facility or foster residence setting, if an individual is disqualified, the individual may not work in the facility or setting until the commissioner has issued a notice stating that:</p>
          <p>(1) the individual is not disqualified;</p>
          <p>(2) a disqualification has been set aside under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.23">245C.23</a>; or</p>
          <p>(3) a variance has been granted related to the individual under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.30">245C.30</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.14.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Disqualification from working in licensed child care centers or certified license-exempt child care centers.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For a background study affiliated with a licensed child care center or certified license-exempt child care center, if an individual is disqualified from direct contact under subdivision 1, the commissioner must also disqualify the individual from working in any position regardless of whether the individual would have direct contact with or access to children served in the licensed child care center or certified license-exempt child care center and from having access to a person receiving services from the center.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding any other requirement of this chapter, for a background study affiliated with a licensed child care center or a certified license-exempt child care center, if an individual is disqualified, the individual may not work in the child care center until the commissioner has issued a notice stating that:</p>
          <p>(1) the individual is not disqualified;</p>
          <p>(2) a disqualification has been set aside under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.23">245C.23</a>; or</p>
          <p>(3) a variance has been granted related to the individual under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.30">245C.30</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.14.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Basis for disqualification.</span></h2>
          <p>Information obtained by entities from public web-based data through NETStudy 2.0 or any other source that is not direct correspondence from the commissioner is not a notice of disqualification from the commissioner under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.14.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.14.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.50.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 50</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.33.0">2007 c 112 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/142/#laws.3.5.0">2009 c 142 art 3 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.5.17.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 5 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.53.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 53</a>,54; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.62.19.0">2024 c 127 art 62 s 19</a>,20</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.15">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.15 DISQUALIFYING CRIMES OR CONDUCT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.15.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Permanent disqualification.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if: (1) regardless of how much time has passed since the discharge of the sentence imposed, if any, for the offense; and (2) unless otherwise specified, regardless of the level of the offense, the individual has committed any of the following offenses: sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/243.166">243.166</a> (violation of predatory offender registration law); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.185">609.185</a> (murder in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.19">609.19</a> (murder in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.195">609.195</a> (murder in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.20">609.20 </a>(manslaughter in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.205">609.205</a> (manslaughter in the second degree); a felony offense under <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.221">609.221</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.222">609.222</a> (assault in the first or second degree); a felony offense under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2242">609.2242</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2243">609.2243</a> (domestic assault), spousal abuse, child abuse or neglect, or a crime against children; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2247">609.2247</a> (domestic assault by strangulation); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.228">609.228 </a>(great bodily harm caused by distribution of drugs); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.245">609.245</a> (aggravated robbery); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.247">609.247</a>, subdivision 2 or 3 (carjacking in the first or second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.25">609.25 </a>(kidnapping); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2661">609.2661 </a>(murder of an unborn child in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2662">609.2662</a> (murder of an unborn child in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2663">609.2663</a> (murder of an unborn child in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.322">609.322 </a>(solicitation, inducement, and promotion of prostitution); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.324#stat.609.324.1">609.324, subdivision 1</a> (other prohibited acts); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.342">609.342</a> (criminal sexual conduct in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.343">609.343</a> (criminal sexual conduct in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.344">609.344</a> (criminal sexual conduct in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.345">609.345</a> (criminal sexual conduct in the fourth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.3451">609.3451 </a>(criminal sexual conduct in the fifth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.3453">609.3453 </a>(criminal sexual predatory conduct); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.3458">609.3458</a> (sexual extortion); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.352">609.352</a> (solicitation of children to engage in sexual conduct); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.365">609.365</a> (incest); a felony offense under <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.377">609.377</a> (malicious punishment of a child); a felony offense under <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.378">609.378 </a>(neglect or endangerment of a child); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.561">609.561 </a>(arson in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.66#stat.609.66.1e">609.66, subdivision 1e</a> (drive-by shooting); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.749#stat.609.749.3">609.749, subdivision 3</a>, 4, or 5 (felony-level harassment or stalking); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.855#stat.609.855.5">609.855, subdivision 5</a> (shooting at or in a public transit vehicle or facility); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.23">617.23</a>, subdivision 2, clause (1), or subdivision 3, clause (1) (indecent exposure involving a minor); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.246">617.246</a> (use of minors in sexual performance prohibited); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.247">617.247</a> (possession of pictorial representations of minors); or, for a child care background study subject, conviction of a crime that would make the individual ineligible for employment under United States Code, title 42, section 9858f, except for a felony drug conviction, regardless of whether a period of disqualification under subdivisions 2 to 4, would apply if the individual were not a child care background study subject.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) An individual's aiding and abetting, attempt, or conspiracy to commit any of the offenses listed in paragraph (a), as each of these offenses is defined in Minnesota Statutes, permanently disqualifies the individual under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) An individual's offense in any other state or country, where the elements of the offense are substantially similar to any of the offenses listed in paragraph (a), permanently disqualifies the individual under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) When a disqualification is based on a judicial determination other than a conviction, the disqualification period begins from the date of the court order. When a disqualification is based on an admission, the disqualification period begins from the date of an admission in court. When a disqualification is based on an Alford Plea, the disqualification period begins from the date the Alford Plea is entered in court. When a disqualification is based on a preponderance of evidence of a disqualifying act, the disqualification date begins from the date of the dismissal, the date of discharge of the sentence imposed for a conviction for a disqualifying crime of similar elements, or the date of the incident, whichever occurs last.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) If the individual studied commits one of the offenses listed in paragraph (a) that is specified as a felony-level only offense, but the sentence or level of offense is a gross misdemeanor or misdemeanor, the individual is disqualified, but the disqualification look-back period for the offense is the period applicable to gross misdemeanor or misdemeanor offenses.</p>
          <p>(f) A child care background study subject shall be disqualified if the individual is registered, or required to be registered, on a state sex offender registry or repository or the National Sex Offender Registry.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.15.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">15-year disqualification.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14 </a>if: (1) less than 15 years have passed since the discharge of the sentence imposed, if any, for the offense; and (2) the individual has committed a felony-level violation of any of the following offenses: sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.021#stat.152.021.1">152.021, subdivision 1</a> or 2b, (aggravated controlled substance crime in the first degree; sale crimes); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.022#stat.152.022.1">152.022, subdivision 1</a> (controlled substance crime in the second degree; sale crimes); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.023#stat.152.023.1">152.023, subdivision 1</a> (controlled substance crime in the third degree; sale crimes); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.024#stat.152.024.1">152.024, subdivision 1</a> (controlled substance crime in the fourth degree; sale crimes); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.98">256.98</a> (wrongfully obtaining assistance); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/268.182">268.182</a> (fraud); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/393.07#stat.393.07.10">393.07, subdivision 10</a>, paragraph (c) (federal SNAP fraud); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518B.01#stat.518B.01.14">518B.01, subdivision 14 </a>(violation of an order for protection); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.165">609.165</a> (felon ineligible to possess firearm); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2112">609.2112</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2113">609.2113</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2114">609.2114</a> (criminal vehicular homicide or injury); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.215">609.215</a> (suicide); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.223">609.223 </a>or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2231">609.2231 </a>(assault in the third or fourth degree); repeat offenses under <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.224">609.224</a> (assault in the fifth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.229">609.229</a> (crimes committed for benefit of a gang); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2325">609.2325</a> (criminal abuse of a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2335">609.2335</a> (financial exploitation of a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.235">609.235 </a>(use of drugs to injure or facilitate crime); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.24">609.24</a> (simple robbery); 609.247, subdivision 4 (carjacking in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.255">609.255</a> (false imprisonment); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2664">609.2664</a> (manslaughter of an unborn child in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2665">609.2665</a> (manslaughter of an unborn child in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.267">609.267 </a>(assault of an unborn child in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2671">609.2671</a> (assault of an unborn child in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.268">609.268</a> (injury or death of an unborn child in the commission of a crime); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.27">609.27</a> (coercion); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.275">609.275 </a>(attempt to coerce); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.466">609.466</a> (medical assistance fraud); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.495">609.495 </a>(aiding an offender); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.498#stat.609.498.1">609.498, subdivision 1</a> or 1b (aggravated first-degree or first-degree tampering with a witness); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.52">609.52</a> (theft); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.521">609.521</a> (possession of shoplifting gear); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.522">609.522</a> (organized retail theft); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.525">609.525 </a>(bringing stolen goods into Minnesota); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.527">609.527</a> (identity theft); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.53">609.53</a> (receiving stolen property); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.535">609.535</a> (issuance of dishonored checks); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.562">609.562</a> (arson in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.563">609.563</a> (arson in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.582">609.582</a> (burglary); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.59">609.59</a> (possession of burglary tools); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.611">609.611</a> (insurance fraud); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.625">609.625</a> (aggravated forgery); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.63">609.63</a> (forgery); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.631">609.631 </a>(check forgery; offering a forged check); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.635">609.635</a> (obtaining signature by false pretense); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.66">609.66</a> (dangerous weapons); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.67">609.67</a> (machine guns and short-barreled shotguns); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.687">609.687 </a>(adulteration); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.71">609.71</a> (riot); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.713">609.713</a> (terroristic threats); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.746">609.746</a> (interference with privacy); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.82">609.82 </a>(fraud in obtaining credit); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.821">609.821</a> (financial transaction card fraud); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.23">617.23</a> (indecent exposure), not involving a minor; repeat offenses under <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.241">617.241</a> (obscene materials and performances; distribution and exhibition prohibited; penalty); or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/624.713">624.713</a> (certain persons not to possess firearms).</p>
          <p class="in">(b) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if less than 15 years has passed since the individual's aiding and abetting, attempt, or conspiracy to commit any of the offenses listed in paragraph (a), as each of these offenses is defined in Minnesota Statutes.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if less than 15 years has passed since the termination of the individual's parental rights under section 260C.301, subdivision 1, paragraph (b), or subdivision 3.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if less than 15 years has passed since the discharge of the sentence imposed for an offense in any other state or country, the elements of which are substantially similar to the elements of the offenses listed in paragraph (a) or since the termination of parental rights in any other state or country, the elements of which are substantially similar to the elements listed in paragraph (c).</p>
          <p class="in">(e) If the individual studied commits one of the offenses listed in paragraph (a), but the sentence or level of offense is a gross misdemeanor or misdemeanor, the individual is disqualified but the disqualification look-back period for the offense is the period applicable to the gross misdemeanor or misdemeanor disposition.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) When a disqualification is based on a judicial determination other than a conviction, the disqualification period begins from the date of the court order. When a disqualification is based on an admission, the disqualification period begins from the date of an admission in court. When a disqualification is based on an Alford Plea, the disqualification period begins from the date the Alford Plea is entered in court. When a disqualification is based on a preponderance of evidence of a disqualifying act, the disqualification date begins from the date of the dismissal, the date of discharge of the sentence imposed for a conviction for a disqualifying crime of similar elements, or the date of the incident, whichever occurs last.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.15.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Ten-year disqualification.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if: (1) less than ten years have passed since the discharge of the sentence imposed, if any, for the offense; and (2) the individual has committed a gross misdemeanor-level violation of any of the following offenses: sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.98">256.98</a> (wrongfully obtaining assistance); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260B.425">260B.425</a> (criminal jurisdiction for contributing to status as a juvenile petty offender or delinquency); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.425">260C.425</a> (criminal jurisdiction for contributing to need for protection or services); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/268.182">268.182</a> (fraud); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/393.07#stat.393.07.10">393.07, subdivision 10</a>, paragraph (c) (federal SNAP fraud); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2112">609.2112</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2113">609.2113</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2114">609.2114</a> (criminal vehicular homicide or injury); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.221">609.221</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.222">609.222 </a>(assault in the first or second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.223">609.223</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2231">609.2231</a> (assault in the third or fourth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.224">609.224</a> (assault in the fifth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.224#stat.609.224.2">609.224, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (c) (assault in the fifth degree by a caregiver against a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2242">609.2242</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2243">609.2243 </a>(domestic assault); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.23">609.23</a> (mistreatment of persons confined); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.231">609.231</a> (mistreatment of residents or patients); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2325">609.2325</a> (criminal abuse of a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.233">609.233</a> (criminal neglect of a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2335">609.2335 </a>(financial exploitation of a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.234">609.234</a> (failure to report maltreatment of a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.265">609.265</a> (abduction); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.275">609.275 </a>(attempt to coerce); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.324#stat.609.324.1a">609.324, subdivision 1a</a> (other prohibited acts; minor engaged in prostitution); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.33">609.33</a> (disorderly house); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.377">609.377</a> (malicious punishment of a child); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.378">609.378 </a>(neglect or endangerment of a child); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.466">609.466</a> (medical assistance fraud); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.52">609.52</a> (theft); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.522">609.522</a> (organized retail theft); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.525">609.525</a> (bringing stolen goods into Minnesota); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.527">609.527 </a>(identity theft); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.53">609.53</a> (receiving stolen property); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.535">609.535</a> (issuance of dishonored checks); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.582">609.582</a> (burglary); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.59">609.59</a> (possession of burglary tools); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.611">609.611 </a>(insurance fraud); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.631">609.631 </a>(check forgery; offering a forged check); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.66">609.66</a> (dangerous weapons); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.71">609.71</a> (riot); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.72#stat.609.72.3">609.72, subdivision 3</a> (disorderly conduct against a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.749#stat.609.749.2">609.749, subdivision 2 </a>(harassment); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.82">609.82</a> (fraud in obtaining credit); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.821">609.821</a> (financial transaction card fraud); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.23">617.23</a> (indecent exposure), not involving a minor; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.241">617.241</a> (obscene materials and performances); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.243">617.243</a> (indecent literature, distribution); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.293">617.293</a> (harmful materials; dissemination and display to minors prohibited); or Minnesota Statutes 2012, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.21">609.21</a>; or violation of an order for protection under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518B.01#stat.518B.01.14">518B.01, subdivision 14</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if less than ten years has passed since the individual's aiding and abetting, attempt, or conspiracy to commit any of the offenses listed in paragraph (a), as each of these offenses is defined in Minnesota Statutes.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if less than ten years has passed since the discharge of the sentence imposed for an offense in any other state or country, the elements of which are substantially similar to the elements of any of the offenses listed in paragraph (a).</p>
          <p class="in">(d) If the individual studied commits one of the offenses listed in paragraph (a), but the sentence or level of offense is a misdemeanor disposition, the individual is disqualified but the disqualification lookback period for the offense is the period applicable to misdemeanors.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) When a disqualification is based on a judicial determination other than a conviction, the disqualification period begins from the date of the court order. When a disqualification is based on an admission, the disqualification period begins from the date of an admission in court. When a disqualification is based on an Alford Plea, the disqualification period begins from the date the Alford Plea is entered in court. When a disqualification is based on a preponderance of evidence of a disqualifying act, the disqualification date begins from the date of the dismissal, the date of discharge of the sentence imposed for a conviction for a disqualifying crime of similar elements, or the date of the incident, whichever occurs last.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.15.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Seven-year disqualification.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if: (1) less than seven years has passed since the discharge of the sentence imposed, if any, for the offense; and (2) the individual has committed a misdemeanor-level violation of any of the following offenses: sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.98">256.98</a> (wrongfully obtaining assistance); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260B.425">260B.425 </a>(criminal jurisdiction for contributing to status as a juvenile petty offender or delinquency); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.425">260C.425</a> (criminal jurisdiction for contributing to need for protection or services); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/268.182">268.182 </a>(fraud); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/393.07#stat.393.07.10">393.07, subdivision 10</a>, paragraph (c) (federal SNAP fraud); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2112">609.2112</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2113">609.2113</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2114">609.2114</a> (criminal vehicular homicide or injury); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.221">609.221</a> (assault in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.222">609.222</a> (assault in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.223">609.223</a> (assault in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2231">609.2231 </a>(assault in the fourth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.224">609.224 </a>(assault in the fifth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2242">609.2242 </a>(domestic assault); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2335">609.2335</a> (financial exploitation of a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.234">609.234</a> (failure to report maltreatment of a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2672">609.2672</a> (assault of an unborn child in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.27">609.27</a> (coercion); violation of an order for protection under <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.3232">609.3232 </a>(protective order authorized; procedures; penalties); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.466">609.466</a> (medical assistance fraud); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.52">609.52</a> (theft); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.522">609.522</a> (organized retail theft); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.525">609.525</a> (bringing stolen goods into Minnesota); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.527">609.527 </a>(identity theft); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.53">609.53</a> (receiving stolen property); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.535">609.535</a> (issuance of dishonored checks); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.611">609.611 </a>(insurance fraud); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.66">609.66</a> (dangerous weapons); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.665">609.665</a> (spring guns); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.746">609.746 </a>(interference with privacy); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.79">609.79</a> (obscene or harassing telephone calls); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.795">609.795</a> (letter, telegram, or package; opening; harassment); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.82">609.82</a> (fraud in obtaining credit); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.821">609.821 </a>(financial transaction card fraud); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.23">617.23 </a>(indecent exposure), not involving a minor; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.293">617.293 </a>(harmful materials; dissemination and display to minors prohibited); or Minnesota Statutes 2012, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.21">609.21</a>; or violation of an order for protection under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518B.01">518B.01</a> (Domestic Abuse Act).</p>
          <p class="in">(b) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if less than seven years has passed since a determination or disposition of the individual's:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) failure to make required reports under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.06">260E.06</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557#stat.626.557.3">626.557, subdivision 3</a>, for incidents in which: (i) the final disposition under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557 </a>or chapter 260E was substantiated maltreatment, and (ii) the maltreatment was recurring or serious; or</p>
          <p class="in">(2) substantiated serious or recurring maltreatment of a minor under chapter 260E, a vulnerable adult under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a>, or serious or recurring maltreatment in any other state, the elements of which are substantially similar to the elements of maltreatment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557 </a>or chapter 260E for which: (i) there is a preponderance of evidence that the maltreatment occurred, and (ii) the subject was responsible for the maltreatment.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if less than seven years has passed since the individual's aiding and abetting, attempt, or conspiracy to commit any of the offenses listed in paragraphs (a) and (b), as each of these offenses is defined in Minnesota Statutes.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if less than seven years has passed since the discharge of the sentence imposed for an offense in any other state or country, the elements of which are substantially similar to the elements of any of the offenses listed in paragraphs (a) and (b).</p>
          <p class="in">(e) When a disqualification is based on a judicial determination other than a conviction, the disqualification period begins from the date of the court order. When a disqualification is based on an admission, the disqualification period begins from the date of an admission in court. When a disqualification is based on an Alford Plea, the disqualification period begins from the date the Alford Plea is entered in court. When a disqualification is based on a preponderance of evidence of a disqualifying act, the disqualification date begins from the date of the dismissal, the date of discharge of the sentence imposed for a conviction for a disqualifying crime of similar elements, or the date of the incident, whichever occurs last.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if less than seven years has passed since the individual was disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.98#stat.256.98.8">256.98, subdivision 8</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.15.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Licensed family foster setting disqualifications.</span></h2>
