          <p>(a) Notwithstanding subdivisions 1 to 4, for a background study affiliated with a licensed family foster setting, regardless of how much time has passed, an individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14 </a>if the individual committed an act that resulted in a felony-level conviction for sections: <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.185">609.185</a> (murder in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.19">609.19</a> (murder in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.195">609.195</a> (murder in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.20">609.20</a> (manslaughter in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.205">609.205</a> (manslaughter in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2112">609.2112</a> (criminal vehicular homicide); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.221">609.221</a> (assault in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.223#stat.609.223.2">609.223, subdivision 2</a> (assault in the third degree, past pattern of child abuse); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.223#stat.609.223.3">609.223, subdivision 3</a> (assault in the third degree, victim under four); a felony offense under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2242">609.2242</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2243">609.2243</a> (domestic assault, spousal abuse, child abuse or neglect, or a crime against children); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2247">609.2247</a> (domestic assault by strangulation); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2325">609.2325 </a>(criminal abuse of a vulnerable adult resulting in the death of a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.245">609.245 </a>(aggravated robbery); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.247#stat.609.247.2">609.247, subdivision 2</a> or 3 (carjacking in the first or second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.25">609.25</a> (kidnapping); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.255">609.255</a> (false imprisonment); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2661">609.2661</a> (murder of an unborn child in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2662">609.2662</a> (murder of an unborn child in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2663">609.2663 </a>(murder of an unborn child in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2664">609.2664</a> (manslaughter of an unborn child in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2665">609.2665</a> (manslaughter of an unborn child in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.267">609.267</a> (assault of an unborn child in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2671">609.2671</a> (assault of an unborn child in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.268">609.268</a> (injury or death of an unborn child in the commission of a crime); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.322#stat.609.322.1">609.322, subdivision 1</a> (solicitation, inducement, and promotion of prostitution; sex trafficking in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.324#stat.609.324.1">609.324, subdivision 1</a> (other prohibited acts; engaging in, hiring, or agreeing to hire minor to engage in prostitution); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.342">609.342 </a>(criminal sexual conduct in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.343">609.343</a> (criminal sexual conduct in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.344">609.344</a> (criminal sexual conduct in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.345">609.345</a> (criminal sexual conduct in the fourth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.3451">609.3451</a> (criminal sexual conduct in the fifth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.3453">609.3453</a> (criminal sexual predatory conduct); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.3458">609.3458</a> (sexual extortion); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.352">609.352</a> (solicitation of children to engage in sexual conduct); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.377">609.377</a> (malicious punishment of a child); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.378">609.378</a> (neglect or endangerment of a child); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.561">609.561</a> (arson in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.582#stat.609.582.1">609.582, subdivision 1</a> (burglary in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.746">609.746</a> (interference with privacy); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.23">617.23</a> (indecent exposure); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.246">617.246 </a>(use of minors in sexual performance prohibited); or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.247">617.247</a> (possession of pictorial representations of minors).</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding subdivisions 1 to 4, for the purposes of a background study affiliated with a licensed family foster setting, an individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a>, regardless of how much time has passed, if the individual:</p>
          <p>(1) committed an action under paragraph (e) that resulted in death or involved sexual abuse, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.03#stat.260E.03.20">260E.03, subdivision 20</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) committed an act that resulted in a gross misdemeanor-level conviction for section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.3451">609.3451</a> (criminal sexual conduct in the fifth degree);</p>
          <p>(3) committed an act against or involving a minor that resulted in a felony-level conviction for: section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.222">609.222</a> (assault in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.223#stat.609.223.1">609.223, subdivision 1</a> (assault in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2231">609.2231</a> (assault in the fourth degree); or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.224">609.224</a> (assault in the fifth degree); or</p>
          <p>(4) committed an act that resulted in a misdemeanor or gross misdemeanor-level conviction for section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.293">617.293</a> (dissemination and display of harmful materials to minors).</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding subdivisions 1 to 4, for a background study affiliated with a licensed family foster setting, an individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if fewer than 20 years have passed since the termination of the individual's parental rights under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.301#stat.260C.301.1">260C.301, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), or if the individual consented to a termination of parental rights under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.301#stat.260C.301.1">260C.301, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), to settle a petition to involuntarily terminate parental rights. An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14 </a>if fewer than 20 years have passed since the termination of the individual's parental rights in any other state or country, where the conditions for the individual's termination of parental rights are substantially similar to the conditions in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.301#stat.260C.301.1">260C.301, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b).</p>
          <p>(d) Notwithstanding subdivisions 1 to 4, for a background study affiliated with a licensed family foster setting, an individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if fewer than five years have passed since a felony-level violation for sections: <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.021">152.021</a> (controlled substance crime in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.022">152.022</a> (controlled substance crime in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.023">152.023 </a>(controlled substance crime in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.024">152.024</a> (controlled substance crime in the fourth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.025">152.025</a> (controlled substance crime in the fifth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.0261">152.0261</a> (importing controlled substances across state borders); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.0262#stat.152.0262.1">152.0262, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b) (possession of substance with intent to manufacture methamphetamine); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.027">152.027, subdivision </a>6, paragraph (c) (sale or possession of synthetic cannabinoids); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.096">152.096</a> (conspiracies prohibited); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.097">152.097</a> (simulated controlled substances); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.136">152.136</a> (anhydrous ammonia; prohibited conduct; criminal penalties; civil liabilities); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.137">152.137</a> (methamphetamine-related crimes involving children or vulnerable adults); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/169A.24">169A.24</a> (felony first-degree driving while impaired); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/243.166">243.166</a> (violation of predatory offender registration requirements); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2113">609.2113 </a>(criminal vehicular operation; bodily harm); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2114">609.2114</a> (criminal vehicular operation; unborn child); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.228">609.228</a> (great bodily harm caused by distribution of drugs); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2325">609.2325</a> (criminal abuse of a vulnerable adult not resulting in the death of a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.233">609.233</a> (criminal neglect); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.235">609.235</a> (use of drugs to injure or facilitate a crime); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.24">609.24</a> (simple robbery); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.247#stat.609.247.4">609.247, subdivision 4</a> (carjacking in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.322#stat.609.322.1a">609.322, subdivision 1a</a> (solicitation, inducement, and promotion of prostitution; sex trafficking in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.498#stat.609.498.1">609.498, subdivision 1</a> (tampering with a witness in the first degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.498#stat.609.498.1b">609.498, subdivision 1b </a>(aggravated first-degree witness tampering); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.562">609.562 </a>(arson in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.563">609.563 </a>(arson in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.582#stat.609.582.2">609.582, subdivision 2 </a>(burglary in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.66">609.66 </a>(felony dangerous weapons); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.687">609.687</a> (adulteration); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.713">609.713</a> (terroristic threats); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.749#stat.609.749.3">609.749, subdivision 3</a>, 4, or 5 (felony-level harassment or stalking); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.855#stat.609.855.5">609.855, subdivision 5</a> (shooting at or in a public transit vehicle or facility); or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/624.713">624.713</a> (certain people not to possess firearms).</p>
          <p>(e) Notwithstanding subdivisions 1 to 4, except as provided in paragraph (a), for a background study affiliated with a licensed family child foster care license, an individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if fewer than five years have passed since:</p>
          <p>(1) a felony-level violation for an act not against or involving a minor that constitutes: section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.222">609.222</a> (assault in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.223#stat.609.223.1">609.223, subdivision 1</a> (assault in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2231">609.2231</a> (assault in the fourth degree); or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.224#stat.609.224.4">609.224, subdivision 4</a> (assault in the fifth degree);</p>
          <p>(2) a violation of an order for protection under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518B.01#stat.518B.01.14">518B.01, subdivision 14</a>;</p>
          <p>(3) a determination or disposition of the individual's failure to make required reports under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.06">260E.06</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557#stat.626.557.3">626.557, subdivision 3</a>, for incidents in which the final disposition under chapter 260E or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a> was substantiated maltreatment and the maltreatment was recurring or serious;</p>
          <p>(4) a determination or disposition of the individual's substantiated serious or recurring maltreatment of a minor under chapter 260E, a vulnerable adult under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a>, or serious or recurring maltreatment in any other state, the elements of which are substantially similar to the elements of maltreatment under chapter 260E or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a> and meet the definition of serious maltreatment or recurring maltreatment;</p>
          <p>(5) a gross misdemeanor-level violation for sections: <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.224#stat.609.224.2">609.224, subdivision 2</a> (assault in the fifth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2242">609.2242</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2243">609.2243</a> (domestic assault); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.233">609.233</a> (criminal neglect); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.377">609.377</a> (malicious punishment of a child); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.378">609.378</a> (neglect or endangerment of a child); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.746">609.746</a> (interference with privacy); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.749">609.749</a> (stalking); or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.23">617.23</a> (indecent exposure); or</p>
          <p>(6) committing an act against or involving a minor that resulted in a misdemeanor-level violation of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.224#stat.609.224.1">609.224, subdivision 1</a> (assault in the fifth degree).</p>
          <p>(f) For purposes of this subdivision, the disqualification begins from:</p>
          <p>(1) the date of the alleged violation, if the individual was not convicted;</p>
          <p>(2) the date of conviction, if the individual was convicted of the violation but not committed to the custody of the commissioner of corrections; or</p>
          <p>(3) the date of release from prison, if the individual was convicted of the violation and committed to the custody of the commissioner of corrections.</p>
          <p class="bl">Notwithstanding clause (3), if the individual is subsequently reincarcerated for a violation of the individual's supervised release, the disqualification begins from the date of release from the subsequent incarceration.</p>
          <p>(g) An individual's aiding and abetting, attempt, or conspiracy to commit any of the offenses listed in paragraphs (a) and (b), as each of these offenses is defined in Minnesota Statutes, permanently disqualifies the individual under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a>. An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if fewer than five years have passed since the individual's aiding and abetting, attempt, or conspiracy to commit any of the offenses listed in paragraphs (d) and (e).</p>
          <p>(h) An individual's offense in any other state or country, where the elements of the offense are substantially similar to any of the offenses listed in paragraphs (a) and (b), permanently disqualifies the individual under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a>. An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if fewer than five years have passed since an offense in any other state or country, the elements of which are substantially similar to the elements of any offense listed in paragraphs (d) and (e).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.15.4b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4b.<span class="headnote">Five-year disqualification.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if: (1) less than five years have passed since the discharge of the sentence imposed, if any, for the offense; and (2) the individual has committed a felony, gross misdemeanor, or misdemeanor-level violation of any of the following offenses: section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.021">152.021, subdivision </a>2 or 2a (controlled substance possession crime in the first degree; methamphetamine manufacture crime); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.022#stat.152.022.2">152.022, subdivision 2</a> (controlled substance possession crime in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.023#stat.152.023.2">152.023, subdivision 2</a> (controlled substance possession crime in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.024#stat.152.024.2">152.024, subdivision 2</a> (controlled substance possession crime in the fourth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.025">152.025</a> (controlled substance crime in the fifth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.0261">152.0261</a> (importing controlled substances across state borders); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.0262">152.0262</a> (possession of substances with intent to manufacture methamphetamine); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.027#stat.152.027.6">152.027, subdivision 6</a>, paragraph (c) (sale of synthetic cannabinoids); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.096">152.096</a> (conspiracy to commit controlled substance crime); or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.097">152.097 </a>(simulated controlled substances).</p>
          <p class="in">(b) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if less than five years have passed since the individual's aiding and abetting, attempt, or conspiracy to commit any of the offenses listed in paragraph (a), as each of these offenses is defined in Minnesota Statutes.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) An individual is disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14">245C.14</a> if less than five years have passed since the discharge of the sentence imposed for an offense in any other state or country, the elements of which are substantially similar to the elements of any of the offenses listed in paragraph (a).</p>
          <p class="in">(d) When a disqualification is based on a judicial determination other than a conviction, the disqualification period begins from the date of the court order. When a disqualification is based on an admission, the disqualification period begins from the date of an admission in court. When a disqualification is based on an Alford plea, the disqualification period begins from the date the Alford plea is entered in court. When a disqualification is based on a preponderance of evidence of a disqualifying act, the disqualification date begins from the date of the dismissal, the date of discharge of the sentence imposed for a conviction for a disqualifying crime of similar elements, or the date of the incident, whichever occurs last.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.15.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Mental illness.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may not disqualify an individual subject to a background study under this chapter because that individual has, or has had, a mental illness as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.462#stat.245.462.20">245.462, subdivision 20</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.15.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Reproductive health care services.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may not disqualify an individual subject to a background study under this chapter for accessing or providing reproductive health care services, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/147.091#stat.147.091.1c">147.091, subdivision 1c</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.15.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.15.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/14/#laws.6.7.0">1Sp2003 c 14 art 6 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.51.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 51</a>-53; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/136/#laws.6.2.0">2005 c 136 art 6 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/4/#laws.1.30.0">1Sp2005 c 4 art 1 s 30</a>-33; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/212/#laws.3.17.0">2006 c 212 art 3 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.34.0">2007 c 112 s 34</a>-37; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/142/#laws.2.27.0">2009 c 142 art 2 s 27</a>-30; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/299/#laws.0.14.0">2010 c 299 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/329/#laws.1.15.0">2010 c 329 art 1 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.16.34.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 16 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/166/#laws.0.14.0">2018 c 166 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/182/#laws.1.108.0">2018 c 182 art 1 s 108</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/5/#laws.2.29.0">1Sp2019 c 5 art 2 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/9/#laws.1.42.0">1Sp2019 c 9 art 1 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.8.51.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 8 s 51</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.55.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/11/#laws.4.31.0">1Sp2021 c 11 art 4 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/31/#laws.0.6.0">2023 c 31 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/52/#laws.20.5.0">2023 c 52 art 20 s 5</a>-7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.34.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 34</a>,35; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.62.21.0">2024 c 127 art 62 s 21</a>-24</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.16">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.16 DISQUALIFIED INDIVIDUAL'S RISK OF HARM.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.16.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Determining immediate risk of harm.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) If the commissioner determines that the individual studied has a disqualifying characteristic, the commissioner shall review the information immediately available and make a determination as to the subject's immediate risk of harm to persons served by the program where the individual studied will have direct contact with, or access to, people receiving services.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The commissioner shall consider all relevant information available, including the following factors in determining the immediate risk of harm:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the recency of the disqualifying characteristic;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the recency of discharge from probation for the crimes;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the number of disqualifying characteristics;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the intrusiveness or violence of the disqualifying characteristic;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) the vulnerability of the victim involved in the disqualifying characteristic;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) the similarity of the victim to the persons served by the program where the individual studied will have direct contact;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) whether the individual has a disqualification from a previous background study that has not been set aside;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) if the individual has a disqualification which may not be set aside because it is a permanent bar under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.24#stat.245C.24.1">245C.24, subdivision 1</a>, or the individual is a child care background study subject who has a felony-level conviction for a drug-related offense in the last five years, the commissioner may order the immediate removal of the individual from any position allowing direct contact with, or access to, persons receiving services from the program and from working in a children's residential facility or foster residence setting; and</p>
          <p>(9) if the individual has a disqualification which may not be set aside because it is a permanent bar under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.24#stat.245C.24.2">245C.24, subdivision 2</a>, or the individual is a child care background study subject who has a felony-level conviction for a drug-related offense during the last five years, the commissioner may order the immediate removal of the individual from any position allowing direct contact with or access to persons receiving services from the center and from working in a licensed child care center or certified license-exempt child care center.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) This section does not apply when the subject of a background study is regulated by a health-related licensing board as defined in chapter 214, and the subject is determined to be responsible for substantiated maltreatment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a> or chapter 260E.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) This section does not apply to a background study related to an initial application for a child foster family setting license.</p>
          <p>(e) Except for paragraph (f), this section does not apply to a background study that is also subject to the requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0659">256B.0659, subdivisions 11</a> and 13, for a personal care assistant or a qualified professional as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256B.0659#stat.256B.0659.1">256B.0659, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) If the commissioner has reason to believe, based on arrest information or an active maltreatment investigation, that an individual poses an imminent risk of harm to persons receiving services, the commissioner may order that the person be continuously supervised or immediately removed pending the conclusion of the maltreatment investigation or criminal proceedings.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.16.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Findings.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) After evaluating the information immediately available under subdivision 1, the commissioner may have reason to believe one of the following:</p>
          <p>(1) the individual poses an imminent risk of harm to persons served by the program where the individual studied will have direct contact or access to persons served by the program or where the individual studied will work;</p>
          <p>(2) the individual poses a risk of harm requiring continuous, direct supervision while providing direct contact services during the period in which the subject may request a reconsideration; or</p>
          <p>(3) the individual does not pose an imminent risk of harm or a risk of harm requiring continuous, direct supervision while providing direct contact services during the period in which the subject may request a reconsideration.</p>
          <p>(b) After determining an individual's risk of harm under this section, the commissioner must notify the subject of the background study and the applicant or license holder as required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17">245C.17</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) For Title IV-E eligible children's residential facilities and foster residence settings, the commissioner is prohibited from making the findings in paragraph (a), clause (2) or (3).</p>
          <p>(d) For licensed child care centers or certified license-exempt child care centers, the commissioner is prohibited from making the findings in paragraph (a), clause (2) or (3).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.16.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">County agency.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) County licensing agencies performing duties under this section may develop an alternative system for determining the subject's immediate risk of harm to persons served by the program, providing the notices under subdivision 2, paragraph (b), and documenting the action taken by the county licensing agency.</p>
          <p>(b) Each county licensing agency's implementation of the alternative system is subject to approval by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding this alternative system, county licensing agencies shall complete the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17">245C.17</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.16.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.16.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.54.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 54</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.38.0">2007 c 112 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.3.18.0">2007 c 147 art 3 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/216/#laws.16.23.0">2012 c 216 art 16 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/228/#laws.5.4.0">2014 c 228 art 5 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.16.35.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 16 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/166/#laws.0.16.0">2018 c 166 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.5.18.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 5 s 18</a>,19; art 8 s 52; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.56.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 56</a>,57</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.17">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.17 NOTICE OF BACKGROUND STUDY RESULTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.17.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Time frame for notice of study results and auditing system access.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Within three working days after the commissioner's receipt of a request for a background study submitted through the commissioner's NETStudy or NETStudy 2.0 system, the commissioner shall notify the background study subject and the license holder or other entity as provided in this chapter in writing or by electronic transmission of the results of the study or that more time is needed to complete the study. The notice to the individual shall include the identity of the entity that initiated the background study.</p>
          <p>(b) Before being provided access to NETStudy 2.0, the license holder or other entity under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.04">245C.04</a> shall sign an acknowledgment of responsibilities form developed by the commissioner that includes identifying the sensitive background study information person, who must be an employee of the license holder or entity. All queries to NETStudy 2.0 are electronically recorded and subject to audit by the commissioner. The electronic record shall identify the specific user. A background study subject may request in writing to the commissioner a report listing the entities that initiated a background study on the individual.</p>
          <p>(c) When the commissioner has completed a prior background study on an individual that resulted in an order for immediate removal and more time is necessary to complete a subsequent study, the notice that more time is needed that is issued under paragraph (a) shall include an order for immediate removal of the individual from any position allowing direct contact with or access to people receiving services and from working in a children's residential facility, foster residence setting, child care center, or certified license-exempt child care center pending completion of the background study.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.17.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Disqualification notice sent to subject.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) If the information in the study indicates the individual is disqualified from direct contact with, or from access to, persons served by the program, the commissioner shall disclose to the individual studied:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the information causing disqualification;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) instructions on how to request a reconsideration of the disqualification;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) an explanation of any restrictions on the commissioner's discretion to set aside the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.24">245C.24</a>, when applicable to the individual;</p>
          <p>(4) a statement that when a subsequent background study is initiated on the individual following a set-aside of the individual's disqualification, and the commissioner makes a determination under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22#stat.245C.22.5">245C.22, subdivision 5</a>, paragraph (b), that the previous set-aside applies to the subsequent background study, the applicant, license holder, or other entity that initiated the background study will be informed in the notice under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22#stat.245C.22.5">245C.22, subdivision 5</a>, paragraph (c), that the individual's disqualification is set aside for that program or agency; and</p>
          <p class="in">(5) the commissioner's determination of the individual's immediate risk of harm under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.16">245C.16</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) If the commissioner determines under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.16">245C.16</a> that an individual poses an imminent risk of harm to persons served by the program where the individual will have direct contact with, or access to, people receiving services, the commissioner's notice must include an explanation of the basis of this determination.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) If the commissioner determines under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.16">245C.16</a> that an individual studied does not pose a risk of harm that requires immediate removal, the individual shall be informed of the conditions under which the agency that initiated the background study may allow the individual to have direct contact with, or access to, people receiving services, as provided under subdivision 3.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.17.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Disqualification notification.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The commissioner shall notify an applicant, license holder, or other entity as provided in this chapter who is not the subject of the study:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) that the commissioner has found information that disqualifies the individual studied from being in a position allowing direct contact with, or access to, people served by the program; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the commissioner's determination of the individual's risk of harm under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.16">245C.16</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) If the commissioner determines under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.16">245C.16</a> that an individual studied poses an imminent risk of harm to persons served by the program where the individual studied will have direct contact with, or access to, people served by the program, the commissioner shall order the license holder to immediately remove the individual studied from any position allowing direct contact with, or access to, people served by the program.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) If the commissioner determines under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.16">245C.16</a> that an individual studied poses a risk of harm that requires continuous, direct supervision, the commissioner shall order the applicant, license holder, or other entities as provided in this chapter to:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) immediately remove the individual studied from any position allowing direct contact with, or access to, people receiving services; or</p>
          <p class="in">(2) before allowing the disqualified individual to be in a position allowing direct contact with, or access to, people receiving services, the applicant, license holder, or other entity, as provided in this chapter, must:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) ensure that the individual studied is under continuous, direct supervision when in a position allowing direct contact with, or access to, people receiving services during the period in which the individual may request a reconsideration of the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.21">245C.21</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) ensure that the disqualified individual requests reconsideration within 30 days of receipt of the notice of disqualification.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) If the commissioner determines under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.16">245C.16</a> that an individual studied does not pose a risk of harm that requires continuous, direct supervision, the commissioner shall order the applicant, license holder, or other entities as provided in this chapter to:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) immediately remove the individual studied from any position allowing direct contact with, or access to, people receiving services; or</p>
          <p class="in">(2) before allowing the disqualified individual to be in any position allowing direct contact with, or access to, people receiving services, the applicant, license holder, or other entity as provided in this chapter must ensure that the disqualified individual requests reconsideration within 15 days of receipt of the notice of disqualification.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) The commissioner shall not notify the applicant, license holder, or other entity as provided in this chapter of the information contained in the subject's background study unless:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the basis for the disqualification is failure to cooperate with the background study;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the Data Practices Act under chapter 13 provides for release of the information; or</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the individual studied authorizes the release of the information.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.17.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Disqualification notice to family child care or foster care provider.</span></h2>
          <p>For studies on individuals pertaining to a license to provide family child care or group family child care, foster care for children in the provider's own home, or foster care or day care services for adults in the provider's own home, the commissioner is not required to provide a separate notice of the background study results to the individual who is the subject of the study unless the study results in a disqualification of the individual.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.17.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Notice to county or private agency.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">For studies on individuals related to a license to provide child foster care when the applicant or license holder resides in the home where child foster care services are provided, the commissioner shall also provide a notice of the background study results to the county or private agency that initiated the background study.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.17.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Notice to county agency.</span></h2>
          <p>For studies on individuals related to a license to provide adult foster care when the applicant or license holder resides in the adult foster care residence and family adult day services and, effective upon implementation of NETStudy 2.0, family child care, the commissioner shall also provide a notice of the background study results to the county agency that initiated the background study.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.17.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Disqualification notice to children's residential facilities and foster residence settings.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For children's residential facilities and foster residence settings, all notices under this section that order the license holder to immediately remove the individual studied from any position allowing direct contact with, or access to a person served by the program, must also order the license holder to immediately remove the individual studied from working in the program, facility, or setting.</p>
          <p>(b) For Title IV-E eligible children's residential facilities and foster residence settings, notices under this section must not allow an individual to work in the program, facility, or setting under supervision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.17.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Disqualification notice to child care centers and certified license-exempt child care centers.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For child care centers and certified license-exempt child care centers, all notices under this section that order the license holder to immediately remove the individual studied from any position allowing direct contact with, or access to a person served by the center, must also order the license holder to immediately remove the individual studied from working in any position regardless of whether the individual would have direct contact with or access to children served in the center.</p>
          <p>(b) For child care centers and certified license-exempt child care centers, notices under this section must not allow an individual to work in the center.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.17.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.17.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.55.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 55</a>,56; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/136/#laws.6.3.0">2005 c 136 art 6 s 3</a>-5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.39.0">2007 c 112 s 39</a>,40; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.3.19.0">2007 c 147 art 3 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/79/#laws.1.14.0">2009 c 79 art 1 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/216/#laws.16.24.0">2012 c 216 art 16 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/250/#laws.0.17.0">2014 c 250 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/90/#laws.0.6.0">2017 c 90 s 6</a>,7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.16.36.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 16 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.5.20.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 5 s 20</a>,21; art 8 s 53; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.58.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 58</a>,59; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.36.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 36</a>-38</p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>The amendment to subdivision 6 by Laws 2023, chapter 70, article 7, section 38, is effective April 28, 2025. Laws 2023, chapter 70, article 7, section 38, the effective date.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.18">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.18 OBLIGATION TO REMOVE DISQUALIFIED INDIVIDUAL FROM DIRECT CONTACT AND FROM WORKING IN A PROGRAM, FACILITY, SETTING, OR CENTER.</h1>
        <p>(a) Upon receipt of notice from the commissioner, the license holder must remove a disqualified individual from direct contact with persons served by the licensed program if:</p>
        <p>(1) the individual does not request reconsideration under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.21">245C.21</a> within the prescribed time;</p>
        <p>(2) the individual submits a timely request for reconsideration, the commissioner does not set aside the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22#stat.245C.22.4">245C.22, subdivision 4</a>, and the individual does not submit a timely request for a hearing under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.27">245C.27</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.28">245C.28</a> and chapter 14; or</p>
        <p>(3) the individual submits a timely request for a hearing under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.27">245C.27</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.28">245C.28</a> and chapter 14, and the commissioner does not set aside or rescind the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.08#stat.245A.08.5">245A.08, subdivision 5</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>.</p>
        <p>(b) For children's residential facility and foster residence setting license holders, upon receipt of notice from the commissioner under paragraph (a), the license holder must also remove the disqualified individual from working in the program, facility, or setting and from access to persons served by the licensed program.</p>
        <p>(c) For Title IV-E eligible children's residential facility and foster residence setting license holders, upon receipt of notice from the commissioner under paragraph (a), the license holder must also remove the disqualified individual from working in the program and from access to persons served by the program and must not allow the individual to work in the facility or setting until the commissioner has issued a notice stating that:</p>
        <p>(1) the individual is not disqualified;</p>
        <p>(2) a disqualification has been set aside under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.23">245C.23</a>; or</p>
        <p>(3) a variance has been granted related to the individual under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.30">245C.30</a>.</p>
        <p>(d) For licensed child care center and certified license-exempt child care center license holders, upon receipt of notice from the commissioner under paragraph (a), the license holder must remove the disqualified individual from working in any position regardless of whether the individual would have direct contact with or access to children served in the center and from having access to persons served by the center and must not allow the individual to work in the center until the commissioner has issued a notice stating that:</p>
        <p>(1) the individual is not disqualified;</p>
        <p>(2) a disqualification has been set aside under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.23">245C.23</a>; or</p>
        <p>(3) a variance has been granted related to the individual under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.30">245C.30</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.18.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.18.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.57.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 57</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.5.22.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 5 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.60.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 60</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.19">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.19 TERMINATION OF AFFILIATION BASED ON DISQUALIFICATION NOTICE.</h1>
        <p>An applicant or license holder that terminates affiliation with persons studied under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03</a>, when the termination is made in good faith reliance on a notice of disqualification provided by the commissioner, shall not be subject to civil liability.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.19.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.19.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 19</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.20">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.20 LICENSE HOLDER RECORD KEEPING.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.20.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Background studies initiated by program.</span></h2>
          <p>A licensed program shall document the date the program initiates a background study under this chapter and the date the subject of the study first has direct contact with persons served by the program in the program's personnel files. When a background study is completed under this chapter, a licensed program shall maintain a notice that the study was undertaken and completed in the program's personnel files. Except when background studies are initiated through the commissioner's online system, if a licensed program has not received a response from the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17">245C.17</a> within 45 days of initiation of the background study request, the licensed program must contact the human services licensing division to inquire about the status of the study. If a license holder initiates a background study under the commissioner's online system, but the background study subject's name does not appear in the list of active or recent studies initiated by that license holder, the license holder must either contact the human services licensing division or resubmit the background study information online for that individual.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.20.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Background studies initiated by others; personnel pool agencies, temporary personnel agencies, supplemental nursing services agencies, or professional services agencies.</span></h2>
          <p>When a license holder relies on a background study initiated by a personnel pool agency, a temporary personnel agency, a supplemental nursing services agency, or a professional services agency for a person required to have a background study completed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03</a>, the license holder must maintain a copy of the background study results in the license holder's files.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.20.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Background studies initiated by others; educational programs.</span></h2>
          <p>When a license holder relies on a background study initiated by an educational program for a person required to have a background study completed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03</a> and the person is on the educational program's active roster, the license holder is responsible for ensuring that the background study has been completed. The license holder may satisfy the documentation requirements through a written agreement with the educational program verifying that documentation of the background study may be provided upon request and that the educational program will inform the license holder if there is a change in the person's background study status. The license holder remains responsible for ensuring that all background study requirements are met.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.20.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Background studies identified on active rosters.</span></h2>
          <p>The requirements in subdivisions 1 and 2 are met for entities for which active rosters are implemented and for whom all individuals affiliated with the entity are recorded on the active roster.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.20.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.20.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.58.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 58</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/79/#laws.1.15.0">2009 c 79 art 1 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/329/#laws.1.16.0">2010 c 329 art 1 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/228/#laws.5.5.0">2014 c 228 art 5 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/250/#laws.0.18.0">2014 c 250 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/78/#laws.4.40.0">2015 c 78 art 4 s 40</a>,41</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.21">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.21 REQUESTING RECONSIDERATION OF DISQUALIFICATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.21.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Who may request reconsideration.</span></h2>
          <p>An individual who is the subject of a disqualification may request a reconsideration of the disqualification pursuant to this section. The individual must submit the request for reconsideration to the commissioner in writing.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.21.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Submission of reconsideration request.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) For disqualifications related to studies conducted by county agencies for family child care, and for disqualifications related to studies conducted by the commissioner for child foster care, adult foster care, and family adult day services when the applicant or license holder resides in the home where services are provided, the individual shall submit the request for reconsideration to the county agency that initiated the background study.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) For disqualifications related to studies conducted by the commissioner for child foster care providers monitored by private licensing agencies under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.16">245A.16</a>, the individual shall submit the request for reconsideration to the private agency that initiated the background study.</p>
          <p>(c) A reconsideration request shall be submitted within 30 days of the individual's receipt of the disqualification notice.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The county or private agency shall forward the individual's request for reconsideration and provide the commissioner with a recommendation whether to set aside the individual's disqualification.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.21.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Time frame for requesting reconsideration.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) When the commissioner sends an individual a notice of disqualification based on a finding under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.16#stat.245C.16.2">245C.16, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (a), clause (1) or (2), the disqualified individual must submit the request for a reconsideration within 30 calendar days of the individual's receipt of the notice of disqualification. If mailed, the request for reconsideration must be postmarked and sent to the commissioner within 30 calendar days of the individual's receipt of the notice of disqualification. If a request for reconsideration is made by personal service, it must be received by the commissioner within 30 calendar days after the individual's receipt of the notice of disqualification. Upon showing that the information under subdivision 3 cannot be obtained within 30 days, the disqualified individual may request additional time, not to exceed 30 days, to obtain the information.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) When the commissioner sends an individual a notice of disqualification based on a finding under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.16#stat.245C.16.2">245C.16, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (a), clause (3), the disqualified individual must submit the request for reconsideration within 30 calendar days of the individual's receipt of the notice of disqualification. If mailed, the request for reconsideration must be postmarked and sent to the commissioner within 30 calendar days of the individual's receipt of the notice of disqualification. If a request for reconsideration is made by personal service, it must be received by the commissioner within 30 calendar days after the individual's receipt of the notice of disqualification.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) An individual who was determined to have maltreated a child under chapter 260E or a vulnerable adult under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a>, and who is disqualified on the basis of serious or recurring maltreatment, may request a reconsideration of both the maltreatment and the disqualification determinations. The request must be submitted within 30 calendar days of the individual's receipt of the notice of disqualification. If mailed, the request for reconsideration must be postmarked and sent to the commissioner within 30 calendar days of the individual's receipt of the notice of disqualification. If a request for reconsideration is made by personal service, it must be received by the commissioner within 30 calendar days after the individual's receipt of the notice of disqualification.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) Except for family child care and child foster care, reconsideration of a maltreatment determination under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.33">260E.33</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a>, subdivision 9d, and reconsideration of a disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>, shall not be conducted when:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) a denial of a license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.05">245A.05</a>, or a licensing sanction under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.07">245A.07</a>, is based on a determination that the license holder is responsible for maltreatment or the disqualification of a license holder based on serious or recurring maltreatment;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the denial of a license or licensing sanction is issued at the same time as the maltreatment determination or disqualification; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the license holder appeals the maltreatment determination, disqualification, and denial of a license or licensing sanction. In such cases, a fair hearing under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045 </a>must not be conducted under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.27">245C.27</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.33">260E.33</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a>, subdivision 9d. Under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.08#stat.245A.08.2a">245A.08, subdivision 2a</a>, the scope of the consolidated contested case hearing must include the maltreatment determination, disqualification, and denial of a license or licensing sanction.</p>
          <p class="in">Notwithstanding clauses (1) to (3), if the license holder appeals the maltreatment determination or disqualification, but does not appeal the denial of a license or a licensing sanction, reconsideration of the maltreatment determination shall be conducted under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.33">260E.33</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557#stat.626.557.9d">626.557, subdivision 9d</a>, and reconsideration of the disqualification shall be conducted under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>. In such cases, a fair hearing shall also be conducted as provided under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.27">245C.27</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.33">260E.33</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557#stat.626.557.9d">626.557, subdivision 9d</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.21.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Disqualified individuals; information for reconsideration.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The disqualified individual requesting reconsideration must submit information showing that:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the information the commissioner relied upon in determining the underlying conduct that gave rise to the disqualification is incorrect;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) for maltreatment, the information the commissioner relied upon in determining that maltreatment was serious or recurring is incorrect; or</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the subject of the study does not pose a risk of harm to any person served by the applicant, license holder, or other entities as provided in this chapter, by addressing the information required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22#stat.245C.22.4">245C.22, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) In order to determine the individual's risk of harm, the commissioner may require additional information from the disqualified individual as part of the reconsideration process. If the individual fails to provide the required information, the commissioner may deny the individual's request.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.21.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Notice of request for reconsideration.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon request, the commissioner may inform the applicant, license holder, or other entities as provided in this chapter who received a notice of the individual's disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17#stat.245C.17.3">245C.17, subdivision 3</a>, or has the consent of the disqualified individual, whether the disqualified individual has requested reconsideration.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.21.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.21.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.59.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 59</a>,60; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/4/#laws.1.34.0">1Sp2005 c 4 art 1 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.41.0">2007 c 112 s 41</a>,42; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.3.20.0">2007 c 147 art 3 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/79/#laws.1.16.0">2009 c 79 art 1 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/173/#laws.1.10.0">2009 c 173 art 1 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/90/#laws.0.8.0">2017 c 90 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.16.37.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 16 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.8.54.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 8 s 54</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.39.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 39</a>,40</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.22">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.22 REVIEW AND ACTION ON A RECONSIDERATION REQUEST.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.22.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Time frame; response to disqualification reconsideration requests.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall respond in writing or by electronic transmission to all reconsideration requests for which the basis for the request is that the information the commissioner relied upon to disqualify is incorrect or inaccurate within 30 working days of receipt of a complete request and all required relevant information.</p>
          <p>(b) If the basis for a disqualified individual's reconsideration request is that the individual does not pose a risk of harm, the commissioner shall respond to the request within 15 working days after receiving a complete request for reconsideration and all required relevant information.</p>
          <p>(c) If the disqualified individual's reconsideration request is based on both the correctness or accuracy of the information the commissioner relied upon to disqualify the individual and the individual's risk of harm, the commissioner shall respond to the request within 45 working days after receiving a complete request for reconsideration and all required relevant information.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.22.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Incorrect information; rescission.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall rescind the disqualification if the commissioner finds that the information relied upon to disqualify the subject is incorrect.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.22.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Preeminent weight given to safety of persons being served.</span></h2>
          <p>In reviewing a request for reconsideration of a disqualification, the commissioner shall give preeminent weight to the safety of each person served by the license holder, applicant, or other entities as provided in this chapter over the interests of the disqualified individual, license holder, applicant, or other entity as provided in this chapter, and any single factor under subdivision 4, paragraph (b), may be determinative of the commissioner's decision whether to set aside the individual's disqualification.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.22.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Risk of harm; set aside.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The commissioner may set aside the disqualification if the commissioner finds that the individual has submitted sufficient information to demonstrate that the individual does not pose a risk of harm to any person served by the applicant, license holder, or other entities as provided in this chapter.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) In determining whether the individual has met the burden of proof by demonstrating the individual does not pose a risk of harm, the commissioner shall consider:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the nature, severity, and consequences of the event or events that led to the disqualification;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) whether there is more than one disqualifying event;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the age and vulnerability of the victim at the time of the event;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the harm suffered by the victim;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) vulnerability of persons served by the program;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) the similarity between the victim and persons served by the program;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) the time elapsed without a repeat of the same or similar event;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) documentation of successful completion by the individual studied of training or rehabilitation pertinent to the event; and</p>
          <p class="in">(9) any other information relevant to reconsideration.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) For an individual seeking a child foster care license who is a relative of the child, the commissioner shall consider the importance of maintaining the child's relationship with relatives as an additional significant factor in determining whether a background study disqualification should be set aside.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) If the individual requested reconsideration on the basis that the information relied upon to disqualify the individual was incorrect or inaccurate and the commissioner determines that the information relied upon to disqualify the individual is correct, the commissioner must also determine if the individual poses a risk of harm to persons receiving services in accordance with paragraph (b).</p>
          <p>(e) For an individual seeking employment in the substance use disorder treatment field, the commissioner shall set aside the disqualification if the following criteria are met:</p>
          <p>(1) the individual is not disqualified for a crime of violence as listed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/624.712#stat.624.712.5">624.712, subdivision 5</a>, except for the following crimes: crimes listed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.021">152.021</a>, subdivision 2 or 2a; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.022#stat.152.022.2">152.022, subdivision 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.023#stat.152.023.2">152.023, subdivision 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.024">152.024</a>; or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.025">152.025</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) the individual is not disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15#stat.245C.15.1">245C.15, subdivision 1</a>;</p>
          <p>(3) the individual is not disqualified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15#stat.245C.15.4">245C.15, subdivision 4</a>, paragraph (b);</p>
          <p>(4) the individual provided documentation of successful completion of treatment, at least one year prior to the date of the request for reconsideration, at a program licensed under chapter 245G, and has had no disqualifying crimes or conduct under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15</a> after the successful completion of treatment;</p>
          <p>(5) the individual provided documentation demonstrating abstinence from controlled substances, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.01#stat.152.01.4">152.01, subdivision 4</a>, for the period of one year prior to the date of the request for reconsideration; and</p>
          <p>(6) the individual is seeking employment in the substance use disorder treatment field.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.22.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Scope of set-aside.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) If the commissioner sets aside a disqualification under this section, the disqualified individual remains disqualified, but may hold a license and have direct contact with or access to persons receiving services. Except as provided in paragraph (b), the commissioner's set-aside of a disqualification is limited solely to the licensed program, applicant, or agency specified in the set aside notice under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.23">245C.23</a>. For personal care provider organizations, the commissioner's set-aside may further be limited to a specific individual who is receiving services. For new background studies required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.04">245C.04</a>, subdivision 1, paragraph (h), if an individual's disqualification was previously set aside for the license holder's program and the new background study results in no new information that indicates the individual may pose a risk of harm to persons receiving services from the license holder, the previous set-aside shall remain in effect.</p>
          <p>(b) If the commissioner has previously set aside an individual's disqualification for one or more programs or agencies, and the individual is the subject of a subsequent background study for a different program or agency, the commissioner shall determine whether the disqualification is set aside for the program or agency that initiated the subsequent background study. A notice of a set-aside under paragraph (c) shall be issued within 15 working days if all of the following criteria are met:</p>
          <p>(1) the subsequent background study was initiated in connection with a program licensed or regulated under the same provisions of law and rule for at least one program for which the individual's disqualification was previously set aside by the commissioner;</p>
          <p>(2) the individual is not disqualified for an offense specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15</a>, subdivision 1 or 2;</p>
          <p>(3) the commissioner has received no new information to indicate that the individual may pose a risk of harm to any person served by the program; and</p>
          <p>(4) the previous set-aside was not limited to a specific person receiving services.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding paragraph (b), clause (2), for an individual who is employed in the substance use disorder field, if the commissioner has previously set aside an individual's disqualification for one or more programs or agencies in the substance use disorder treatment field, and the individual is the subject of a subsequent background study for a different program or agency in the substance use disorder treatment field, the commissioner shall set aside the disqualification for the program or agency in the substance use disorder treatment field that initiated the subsequent background study when the criteria under paragraph (b), clauses (1), (3), and (4), are met and the individual is not disqualified for an offense specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15</a>, subdivision 1. A notice of a set-aside under paragraph (d) shall be issued within 15 working days.</p>
          <p>(d) When a disqualification is set aside under paragraph (b), the notice of background study results issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17">245C.17</a>, in addition to the requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17">245C.17</a>, shall state that the disqualification is set aside for the program or agency that initiated the subsequent background study. The notice must inform the individual that the individual may request reconsideration of the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.21">245C.21</a> on the basis that the information used to disqualify the individual is incorrect.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.22.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Recision of set-aside.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may rescind a previous set aside of a disqualification under this section based on new information that indicates the individual may pose a risk of harm to persons served by the applicant, license holder, or other entities as provided in this chapter. If the commissioner rescinds a set-aside of a disqualification under this subdivision, the appeal rights under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.21">245C.21</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.27#stat.245C.27.1">245C.27, subdivision 1</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.28#stat.245C.28.3">245C.28, subdivision 3</a>, shall apply.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.22.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Classification of certain data.</span></h2>
