          <p>(a) Notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.46">13.46</a>, except as provided in paragraph (e), upon setting aside a disqualification under this section, the identity of the disqualified individual who received the set-aside and the individual's disqualifying characteristics are private data on individuals, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02#stat.13.02.12">13.02, subdivision 12</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.46">13.46</a>, upon granting a variance to a license holder under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.30">245C.30</a>, the identity of the disqualified individual who is the subject of the variance, the individual's disqualifying characteristics under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15</a>, and the terms of the variance are private data on individuals, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02#stat.13.02.12">13.02, subdivision 12</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) The identity of a disqualified individual and the reason for disqualification remain private data when:</p>
          <p>(1) a disqualification is not set aside and no variance is granted, except as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.46#stat.13.46.4">13.46, subdivision 4</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) the data are not public under paragraph (a) or (b);</p>
          <p>(3) the disqualification is rescinded because the information relied upon to disqualify the individual is incorrect;</p>
          <p>(4) the disqualification relates to a license to provide relative child foster care. As used in this clause, "relative" has the meaning given it under section 260C.007, subdivision 26b or 27;</p>
          <p>(5) the disqualified individual is a household member of a licensed foster care provider and:</p>
          <p>(i) the disqualified individual previously received foster care services from this licensed foster care provider;</p>
          <p>(ii) the disqualified individual was subsequently adopted by this licensed foster care provider; and</p>
          <p>(iii) the disqualifying act occurred before the adoption; or</p>
          <p>(6) a variance is granted to a child care center or family child care license holder for an individual's disqualification that is based on a felony-level conviction for a drug-related offense that occurred within the past five years.</p>
          <p>(d) Notwithstanding paragraphs (a) and (b), the identity of household members who are the subject of a disqualification related set-aside or variance is not public data if:</p>
          <p>(1) the household member resides in the residence where the family child care is provided;</p>
          <p>(2) the subject of the set-aside or variance is under the age of 18 years; and</p>
          <p>(3) the set-aside or variance only relates to a disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15</a>, subdivision 4, for a misdemeanor-level theft crime as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.52">609.52</a>.</p>
          <p>(e) When the commissioner has reason to know that a disqualified individual has received an order for expungement for the disqualifying record that does not limit the commissioner's access to the record, and the record was opened or exchanged with the commissioner for purposes of a background study under this chapter, the data that would otherwise become public under paragraph (a) or (b) remain private data.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.22.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Sharing of certain data for reconsiderations and appeals.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The following commissioners shall be responsible for conducting reconsiderations and appeals of background studies for programs under their jurisdictions:</p>
          <p>(1) the commissioner of human services for programs under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03, subdivision </a>1;</p>
          <p>(2) the commissioner of health for programs under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.5a">245C.03, subdivision 5a</a>;</p>
          <p>(3) the commissioner of corrections for programs under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03, subdivision </a>5b; and</p>
          <p>(4) the commissioner of the children, youth, and families for programs under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.5c">245C.03, subdivision 5c</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner of human services shall share all relevant background study data to allow the commissioners specified in paragraph (a) to complete reconsiderations and appeals for programs licensed or regulated by their agencies.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.22.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.22.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/14/#laws.6.6.0">1Sp2003 c 14 art 6 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.61.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 61</a>-64; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/136/#laws.6.6.0">2005 c 136 art 6 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/4/#laws.1.35.0">1Sp2005 c 4 art 1 s 35</a>-37; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/264/#laws.0.9.0">2006 c 264 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.43.0">2007 c 112 s 43</a>,44; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/142/#laws.2.31.0">2009 c 142 art 2 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/329/#laws.1.17.0">2010 c 329 art 1 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/216/#laws.16.25.0">2012 c 216 art 16 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/228/#laws.5.6.0">2014 c 228 art 5 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/246/#laws.0.1.0">2014 c 246 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/21/#laws.1.49.0">2015 c 21 art 1 s 49</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/78/#laws.1.6.0">2015 c 78 art 1 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.16.38.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 16 s 38</a>,39; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/9/#laws.2.74.0">1Sp2019 c 9 art 2 s 74</a>,75; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/83/#laws.1.69.0">2020 c 83 art 1 s 69</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.41.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/80/#laws.8.14.0">2024 c 80 art 8 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.62.25.0">2024 c 127 art 62 s 25</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.23">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.23 COMMISSIONER'S RECONSIDERATION NOTICE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.23.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Disqualification that is rescinded or set aside.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If the commissioner rescinds or sets aside a disqualification, the commissioner shall notify the applicant, license holder, or other entity in writing or by electronic transmission of the decision.</p>
          <p>(b) In the notice from the commissioner that a disqualification has been rescinded, the commissioner must inform the applicant, license holder, or other entity that the information relied upon to disqualify the individual was incorrect.</p>
          <p>(c) In response to a reconsideration request, the commissioner must inform the applicant, license holder, or other entity that the reason for the individual's disqualification and the information about which factors under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22#stat.245C.22.4">245C.22, subdivision 4</a>, were the basis of the reconsideration decision are not public data.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.23.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Commissioner's notice of disqualification that is not set aside.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The commissioner shall notify the license holder of the disqualification and order the license holder to immediately remove the individual from any position allowing direct contact with persons receiving services from the license holder if:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the individual studied does not submit a timely request for reconsideration under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.21">245C.21</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the individual submits a timely request for reconsideration, but the commissioner does not set aside the disqualification for that license holder under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>, unless the individual has a right to request a hearing under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.27">245C.27</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.28">245C.28</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) an individual who has a right to request a hearing under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.27">245C.27</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.28">245C.28</a> and chapter 14 for a disqualification that has not been set aside, does not request a hearing within the specified time; or</p>
          <p class="in">(4) an individual submitted a timely request for a hearing under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.27">245C.27 </a>and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.28">245C.28</a> and chapter 14, but the commissioner does not set aside the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.08#stat.245A.08.5">245A.08, subdivision 5</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) If the commissioner does not set aside the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>, and the license holder was previously ordered under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17">245C.17</a> to immediately remove the disqualified individual from direct contact with persons receiving services or to ensure that the individual is under continuous, direct supervision when providing direct contact services, the order remains in effect pending the outcome of a hearing under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.27">245C.27 </a>and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.28">245C.28</a> and chapter 14.</p>
          <p>(c) If the commissioner does not set aside the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>, and the license holder was not previously ordered under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.17">245C.17</a> to immediately remove the disqualified individual from direct contact with persons receiving services or to ensure that the individual is under continuous direct supervision when providing direct contact services, the commissioner shall order the individual to remain under continuous direct supervision pending the outcome of a hearing under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.27">245C.27</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.28">245C.28</a> and chapter 14.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) For background studies related to child foster care when the applicant or license holder resides in the home where services are provided, the commissioner shall also notify the county or private agency that initiated the study of the results of the reconsideration.</p>
          <p>(e) For background studies related to family child care, adult foster care programs when the applicant or license holder resides in the home where services are provided, and family adult day services, the commissioner shall also notify the county that initiated the study of the results of the reconsideration.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.23.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.23.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.65.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 65</a>,66; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/4/#laws.1.38.0">1Sp2005 c 4 art 1 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.3.21.0">2007 c 147 art 3 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/79/#laws.1.17.0">2009 c 79 art 1 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/228/#laws.5.7.0">2014 c 228 art 5 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/246/#laws.0.2.0">2014 c 246 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/90/#laws.0.9.0">2017 c 90 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.16.40.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 16 s 40</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.42.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 42</a>,43</p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>The amendment to subdivision 2 by Laws 2023, chapter 70, article 7, section 43, is effective April 28, 2025. Laws 2023, chapter 70, article 7, section 43, the effective date.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.24">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.24 DISQUALIFICATION; BAR TO SET ASIDE A DISQUALIFICATION; REQUEST FOR VARIANCE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.24.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Minimum disqualification periods.</span></h2>
          <p>The disqualification periods under subdivisions 3 to 5 are the minimum applicable disqualification periods. The commissioner may determine that an individual should continue to be disqualified from licensure because the individual continues to pose a risk of harm to persons served by that individual, even after the minimum disqualification period has passed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.24.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Permanent bar to set aside a disqualification.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Except as provided in paragraphs (b) to (g), the commissioner may not set aside the disqualification of any individual disqualified pursuant to this chapter, regardless of how much time has passed, if the individual was disqualified for a crime or conduct listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15#stat.245C.15.1">245C.15, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) For an individual in the substance use disorder or corrections field who was disqualified for a crime or conduct listed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15</a>, subdivision 1, and whose disqualification was set aside prior to July 1, 2005, the commissioner must consider granting a variance pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.30">245C.30</a> for the license holder for a program dealing primarily with adults. A request for reconsideration evaluated under this paragraph must include a letter of recommendation from the license holder that was subject to the prior set-aside decision addressing the individual's quality of care to children or vulnerable adults and the circumstances of the individual's departure from that service.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) If an individual who requires a background study for nonemergency medical transportation services under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.12">245C.03, subdivision 12</a>, was disqualified for a crime or conduct listed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15#stat.245C.15.1">245C.15, subdivision 1</a>, and if more than 40 years have passed since the discharge of the sentence imposed, the commissioner may consider granting a set-aside pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>. A request for reconsideration evaluated under this paragraph must include a letter of recommendation from the employer. This paragraph does not apply to a person disqualified based on a violation of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/243.166">243.166</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.185">609.185</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.205">609.205</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.25">609.25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.342">609.342</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.3453">609.3453</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.352">609.352</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.23#stat.617.23.2">617.23, subdivision 2</a>, clause (1), or 3, clause (1); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.246">617.246</a>; or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.247">617.247</a>.</p>
          <p>(d) When a licensed foster care provider adopts an individual who had received foster care services from the provider for over six months, and the adopted individual is required to receive a background study under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03#stat.245C.03.1">245C.03, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clause (2) or (6), the commissioner may grant a variance to the license holder under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.30">245C.30 </a>to permit the adopted individual with a permanent disqualification to remain affiliated with the license holder under the conditions of the variance when the variance is recommended by the county of responsibility for each of the remaining individuals in placement in the home and the licensing agency for the home.</p>
          <p>(e) For an individual 18 years of age or older affiliated with a licensed family foster setting, the commissioner must not set aside or grant a variance for the disqualification of any individual disqualified pursuant to this chapter, regardless of how much time has passed, if the individual was disqualified for a crime or conduct listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15, subdivision </a>4a, paragraphs (a) and (b).</p>
          <p>(f) In connection with a family foster setting license, the commissioner may grant a variance to the disqualification for an individual who is under 18 years of age at the time the background study is submitted.</p>
          <p>(g) In connection with foster residence settings and children's residential facilities, the commissioner must not set aside or grant a variance for the disqualification of any individual disqualified pursuant to this chapter, regardless of how much time has passed, if the individual was disqualified for a crime or conduct listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15#stat.245C.15.4a">245C.15, subdivision 4a</a>, paragraph (a) or (b).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.24.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Ten-year bar to set aside disqualification.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The commissioner may not set aside the disqualification of an individual in connection with a license to provide family child care for children or foster care or day care services for adults in the provider's home if: (1) less than ten years has passed since the discharge of the sentence imposed, if any, for the offense; or (2) when disqualified based on a preponderance of evidence determination under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14#stat.245C.14.1">245C.14, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clause (2), or an admission under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.14#stat.245C.14.1">245C.14, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a), clause (1), and less than ten years has passed since the individual committed the act or admitted to committing the act, whichever is later; and (3) the individual has committed a violation of any of the following offenses: sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.165">609.165</a> (felon ineligible to possess firearm); criminal vehicular homicide or criminal vehicular operation causing death under <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2112">609.2112</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2113">609.2113</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2114">609.2114</a> (criminal vehicular homicide or injury); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.215">609.215</a> (aiding suicide or aiding attempted suicide); felony violations under <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.223">609.223</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2231">609.2231</a> (assault in the third or fourth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.229">609.229</a> (crimes committed for benefit of a gang); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.713">609.713 </a>(terroristic threats); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.235">609.235</a> (use of drugs to injure or to facilitate crime); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.24">609.24</a> (simple robbery); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.247#stat.609.247.4">609.247, subdivision 4</a> (carjacking in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.255">609.255</a> (false imprisonment); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.562">609.562</a> (arson in the second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.71">609.71</a> (riot); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.498#stat.609.498.1">609.498, subdivision 1</a> or 1b (aggravated first-degree or first-degree tampering with a witness); burglary in the first or second degree under <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.582">609.582 </a>(burglary); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.66">609.66</a> (dangerous weapon); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.665">609.665</a> (spring guns); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.67">609.67</a> (machine guns and short-barreled shotguns); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.749#stat.609.749.2">609.749, subdivision 2</a> (gross misdemeanor harassment); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.021">152.021</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.022">152.022</a> (controlled substance crime in the first or second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.023#stat.152.023.1">152.023, subdivision 1</a>, clause (3) or (4) or subdivision 2, clause (4) (controlled substance crime in the third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.024">152.024</a>, subdivision 1, clause (2), (3), or (4) (controlled substance crime in the fourth degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.224#stat.609.224.2">609.224, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (c) (fifth-degree assault by a caregiver against a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.23">609.23</a> (mistreatment of persons confined); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.231">609.231</a> (mistreatment of residents or patients); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2325">609.2325</a> (criminal abuse of a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.233">609.233</a> (criminal neglect of a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2335">609.2335</a> (financial exploitation of a vulnerable adult); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.234">609.234</a> (failure to report); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.265">609.265</a> (abduction); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2664">609.2664</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2665">609.2665</a> (manslaughter of an unborn child in the first or second degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.267">609.267</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.2672">609.2672</a> (assault of an unborn child in the first, second, or third degree); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.268">609.268</a> (injury or death of an unborn child in the commission of a crime); repeat offenses under <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.23">617.23</a> (indecent exposure); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.293">617.293</a> (disseminating or displaying harmful material to minors); a felony-level conviction involving alcohol or drug use, a gross misdemeanor offense under <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.324#stat.609.324.1">609.324, subdivision 1</a> (other prohibited acts); a gross misdemeanor offense under <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.378">609.378</a> (neglect or endangerment of a child); a gross misdemeanor offense under <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.377">609.377</a> (malicious punishment of a child); <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.72#stat.609.72.3">609.72, subdivision 3</a> (disorderly conduct against a vulnerable adult); or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/624.713">624.713</a> (certain persons not to possess firearms); or Minnesota Statutes 2012, section 609.21.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The commissioner may not set aside the disqualification of an individual if less than ten years have passed since the individual's aiding and abetting, attempt, or conspiracy to commit any of the offenses listed in paragraph (a) as each of these offenses is defined in Minnesota Statutes.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The commissioner may not set aside the disqualification of an individual if less than ten years have passed since the discharge of the sentence imposed for an offense in any other state or country, the elements of which are substantially similar to the elements of any of the offenses listed in paragraph (a).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.24.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Seven-year bar to set aside disqualification.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may not set aside the disqualification of an individual in connection with a license to provide family child care for children or foster care or day care services for adults in the provider's home if within seven years preceding the study:</p>
          <p>(1) the individual committed an act that constitutes maltreatment of a child under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.24">260E.24, subdivisions 1</a>, 2, and 3, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.30">260E.30</a>, subdivisions 1, 2, and 4, and the maltreatment resulted in substantial bodily harm as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.02#stat.609.02.7a">609.02, subdivision 7a</a>, or substantial mental or emotional harm as supported by competent psychological or psychiatric evidence; or</p>
          <p>(2) the individual was determined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a> to be the perpetrator of a substantiated incident of maltreatment of a vulnerable adult that resulted in substantial bodily harm as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.02#stat.609.02.7a">609.02, subdivision 7a</a>, or substantial mental or emotional harm as supported by competent psychological or psychiatric evidence.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.24.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Five-year bar to set aside or variance for disqualification; children's residential facilities, foster residence settings.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall not set aside or grant a variance for the disqualification of an individual in connection with a license for a children's residential facility or foster residence setting who was convicted of a felony within the past five years for: (1) physical assault or battery; or (2) a drug-related offense.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.24.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Five-year bar to set aside disqualification; family foster setting.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall not set aside or grant a variance for the disqualification of an individual 18 years of age or older in connection with a foster family setting license if within five years preceding the study the individual is convicted of a felony in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15, subdivision </a>4a, paragraph (d).</p>
          <p>(b) In connection with a foster family setting license, the commissioner may set aside or grant a variance to the disqualification for an individual who is under 18 years of age at the time the background study is submitted.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.24.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.24.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/136/#laws.6.7.0">2005 c 136 art 6 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/4/#laws.1.39.0">1Sp2005 c 4 art 1 s 39</a>,40; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/264/#laws.0.10.0">2006 c 264 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.45.0">2007 c 112 s 45</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/361/#laws.1.1.0">2008 c 361 art 1 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/142/#laws.2.32.0">2009 c 142 art 2 s 32</a>,33; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/299/#laws.0.14.0">2010 c 299 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/5/#laws.2.29.0">1Sp2019 c 5 art 2 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/9/#laws.2.76.0">1Sp2019 c 9 art 2 s 76</a>-78; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.8.55.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 8 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.61.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 61</a>-64; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.4.51.0">2022 c 98 art 4 s 51</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/52/#laws.20.8.0">2023 c 52 art 20 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.62.26.0">2024 c 127 art 62 s 26</a>,27</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.25">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.25 CONSOLIDATED RECONSIDERATION OF MALTREATMENT DETERMINATION AND DISQUALIFICATION.</h1>
        <p>(a) If an individual is disqualified on the basis of a determination of maltreatment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a> or chapter 260E, which was serious or recurring, and the individual requests reconsideration of the maltreatment determination under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.33">260E.33</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557#stat.626.557.9d">626.557, subdivision 9d</a>, and also requests reconsideration of the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.21">245C.21</a>, the commissioner shall consolidate the reconsideration of the maltreatment determination and the disqualification into a single reconsideration.</p>
        <p>(b) In cases where an individual seeks reconsideration of license denial or sanction by the commissioners of children, youth, and families and human services based on the same disqualification under chapter 245C or maltreatment determination under chapter 260E or 626, the individual shall send their reconsideration request to the commissioner of human services. The commissioner of human services shall consolidate the requests pursuant to this subdivision and review the decision of the commissioner of children, youth, and families pursuant to chapter 142B.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.25.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.25.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.67.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 67</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/142/#laws.2.34.0">2009 c 142 art 2 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.16.41.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 16 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.8.56.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 8 s 56</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/80/#laws.8.15.0">2024 c 80 art 8 s 15</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.26">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.26 RECONSIDERATION OF A DISQUALIFICATION FOR AN INDIVIDUAL LIVING IN A LICENSED HOME.</h1>
        <p>In the case of any ground for disqualification under this chapter, if the act was committed by an individual other than the applicant or license holder residing in the applicant's or license holder's home, the applicant or license holder may seek reconsideration when the individual who committed the act no longer resides in the home.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.26.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.26.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.68.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 68</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.27">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.27 FAIR HEARING RIGHTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.27.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Fair hearing following a reconsideration decision.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) An individual who is disqualified on the basis of a preponderance of evidence that the individual committed an act or acts that meet the definition of any of the crimes listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15</a>; for a determination under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557 </a>or chapter 260E of substantiated maltreatment that was serious or recurring under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15</a>; or for failure to make required reports under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.06#stat.260E.06.1">260E.06, subdivision 1</a> or 2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.11">260E.11</a>, subdivision 1; or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557#stat.626.557.3">626.557, subdivision 3</a>, pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15#stat.245C.15.4">245C.15, subdivision 4</a>, paragraph (b), clause (1), may request a fair hearing under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>, following a reconsideration decision issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.23">245C.23</a>, unless the disqualification is deemed conclusive under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.29">245C.29</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The fair hearing is the only administrative appeal of the final agency determination for purposes of appeal by the disqualified individual. The disqualified individual does not have the right to challenge the accuracy and completeness of data under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.04">13.04</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Except as provided under paragraph (e), if the individual was disqualified based on a conviction of, admission to, or Alford Plea to any crimes listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15</a>, subdivisions 1 to 4, or for a disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.98#stat.256.98.8">256.98, subdivision 8</a>, the reconsideration decision under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a> is the final agency determination for purposes of appeal by the disqualified individual and is not subject to a hearing under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>. If the individual was disqualified based on a judicial determination, that determination is treated the same as a conviction for purposes of appeal.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) This subdivision does not apply to a public employee's appeal of a disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.28#stat.245C.28.3">245C.28, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) Notwithstanding paragraph (c), if the commissioner does not set aside a disqualification of an individual who was disqualified based on both a preponderance of evidence and a conviction or admission, the individual may request a fair hearing under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>, unless the disqualifications are deemed conclusive under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.29">245C.29</a>. The scope of the hearing conducted under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a> with regard to the disqualification based on a conviction or admission shall be limited solely to whether the individual poses a risk of harm, according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045#stat.256.045.3b">256.045, subdivision 3b</a>. In this case, the reconsideration decision under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a> is not the final agency decision for purposes of appeal by the disqualified individual.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.27.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Consolidated fair hearing following a reconsideration decision.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If an individual who is disqualified on the bases of serious or recurring maltreatment requests a fair hearing on the maltreatment determination under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.33">260E.33</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557#stat.626.557.9d">626.557, subdivision 9d</a>, and requests a fair hearing under this section on the disqualification following a reconsideration decision under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.23">245C.23</a>, the scope of the fair hearing under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a> shall include the maltreatment determination and the disqualification.</p>
          <p>(b) A fair hearing is the only administrative appeal of the final agency determination. The disqualified individual does not have the right to challenge the accuracy and completeness of data under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.04">13.04</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) This subdivision does not apply to a public employee's appeal of a disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.28#stat.245C.28.3">245C.28, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.27.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.27.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/14/#laws.6.6.0">1Sp2003 c 14 art 6 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.69.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 69</a>,70; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/4/#laws.1.41.0">1Sp2005 c 4 art 1 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.46.0">2007 c 112 s 46</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/142/#laws.2.35.0">2009 c 142 art 2 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/329/#laws.2.1.0">2010 c 329 art 2 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.8.57.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 8 s 57</a>,58</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.28">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.28 CONTESTED CASE HEARING RIGHTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.28.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">License holder.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) If a maltreatment determination or a disqualification for which reconsideration was timely requested and which was not set aside is the basis for a denial of a license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.05">245A.05</a> or a licensing sanction under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.07">245A.07</a>, the license holder has the right to a contested case hearing under chapter 14 and Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/1400.8505">1400.8505</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/1400.8612">1400.8612</a>. The license holder must submit the appeal under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.05">245A.05</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.07#stat.245A.07.3">245A.07, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) As provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.08#stat.245A.08.2a">245A.08, subdivision 2a</a>, if the denial of a license or licensing sanction is based on a disqualification for which reconsideration was timely requested and was not set aside, the scope of the consolidated contested case hearing must include:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the disqualification, to the extent the license holder otherwise has a hearing right on the disqualification under this chapter; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the licensing sanction or denial of a license.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) As provided for under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.08#stat.245A.08.2a">245A.08, subdivision 2a</a>, if the denial of a license or licensing sanction is based on a determination of maltreatment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a> or chapter 260E, or a disqualification for serious or recurring maltreatment which was not set aside, the scope of the contested case hearing must include:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the maltreatment determination, if the maltreatment is not conclusive under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.29">245C.29</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the disqualification, if the disqualification is not conclusive under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.29">245C.29</a>; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the licensing sanction or denial of a license. In such cases, a fair hearing must not be conducted under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>. If the disqualification was based on a determination of substantiated serious or recurring maltreatment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a> or chapter 260E, the appeal must be submitted under section 245A.07, subdivision 3, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.33">260E.33</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557#stat.626.557.9d">626.557, subdivision 9d</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) Except for family child care and child foster care, reconsideration of a maltreatment determination under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.33">260E.33</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a>, subdivision 9d, and reconsideration of a disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>, must not be conducted when:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) a denial of a license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.05">245A.05</a>, or a licensing sanction under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.07">245A.07</a>, is based on a determination that the license holder is responsible for maltreatment or the disqualification of a license holder based on serious or recurring maltreatment;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the denial of a license or licensing sanction is issued at the same time as the maltreatment determination or disqualification; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the license holder appeals the maltreatment determination, disqualification, and denial of a license or licensing sanction. In such cases a fair hearing under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045 </a>must not be conducted under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.27">245C.27</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.33">260E.33</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a>, subdivision 9d. Under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.08#stat.245A.08.2a">245A.08, subdivision 2a</a>, the scope of the consolidated contested case hearing must include the maltreatment determination, disqualification, and denial of a license or licensing sanction.</p>
          <p class="in">Notwithstanding clauses (1) to (3), if the license holder appeals the maltreatment determination or disqualification, but does not appeal the denial of a license or a licensing sanction, reconsideration of the maltreatment determination shall be conducted under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.33">260E.33</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557#stat.626.557.9d">626.557, subdivision 9d</a>, and reconsideration of the disqualification shall be conducted under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>. In such cases, a fair hearing shall also be conducted as provided under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.27">245C.27</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260E.33">260E.33</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557#stat.626.557.9d">626.557, subdivision 9d</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.28.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Individual other than license holder.</span></h2>
          <p>If the basis for the commissioner's denial of a license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.05">245A.05</a> or a licensing sanction under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.07">245A.07</a> is a maltreatment determination or disqualification that was not set aside under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>, and the disqualified subject is an individual other than the license holder and upon whom a background study must be conducted under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03</a>, the hearing of all parties may be consolidated into a single contested case hearing upon consent of all parties and the administrative law judge.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.28.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Employees of public employer.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A disqualified individual who is an employee of an employer, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/179A.03#stat.179A.03.15">179A.03, subdivision 15</a>, may request a contested case hearing under chapter 14, and specifically Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/1400.8505">1400.8505</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/1400.8612">1400.8612</a>, following a reconsideration decision under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.23">245C.23</a>, unless the disqualification is deemed conclusive under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.29">245C.29</a>. The request for a contested case hearing must be made in writing and must be postmarked and sent within 30 calendar days after the employee receives notice of the reconsideration decision. If the individual was disqualified based on a conviction or admission to any crimes listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15</a>, the scope of the contested case hearing shall be limited solely to whether the individual poses a risk of harm pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) When an individual is disqualified based on a maltreatment determination, the scope of the contested case hearing under paragraph (a), must include the maltreatment determination and the disqualification. In such cases, a fair hearing must not be conducted under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) Rules adopted under this chapter may not preclude an employee in a contested case hearing for a disqualification from submitting evidence concerning information gathered under this chapter.</p>
          <p>(d) When an individual has been disqualified from multiple licensed programs, if at least one of the disqualifications entitles the person to a contested case hearing under this subdivision, the scope of the contested case hearing shall include all disqualifications from licensed programs.</p>
          <p>(e) In determining whether the disqualification should be set aside, the administrative law judge shall consider all of the characteristics that cause the individual to be disqualified, as well as all the factors set forth in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>, in order to determine whether the individual has met the burden of demonstrating that the individual does not pose a risk of harm. The administrative law judge's recommendation and the commissioner's order to set aside a disqualification that is the subject of the hearing constitutes a determination that the individual does not pose a risk of harm and that the individual may provide direct contact services in the individual program specified in the set aside.</p>
          <p>(f) An individual may not request a contested case hearing under this section if a contested case hearing has previously been held regarding the individual's disqualification on the same basis.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.28.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Final agency order.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner's final order under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.08#stat.245A.08.5">245A.08, subdivision 5</a>, is conclusive on the issue of maltreatment and disqualification, including for purposes of subsequent background studies. The contested case hearing under this section is the only administrative appeal of the final agency determination, specifically, including a challenge to the accuracy and completeness of data under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.04">13.04</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.28.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.28.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.71.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 71</a>-73; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/4/#laws.1.42.0">1Sp2005 c 4 art 1 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/112/#laws.0.47.0">2007 c 112 s 47</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/329/#laws.2.3.0">2010 c 329 art 2 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/228/#laws.5.8.0">2014 c 228 art 5 s 8</a>,9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.8.59.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 8 s 59</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.29">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.29 CONCLUSIVE DETERMINATIONS OR DISPOSITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.29.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Conclusive maltreatment determination or disposition.</span></h2>
          <p>Unless otherwise specified in statute, a maltreatment determination or disposition under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557 </a>or chapter 260E is conclusive, if:</p>
          <p>(1) the commissioner has issued a final order in an appeal of that determination or disposition under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.08#stat.245A.08.5">245A.08, subdivision 5</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) the individual did not request reconsideration of the maltreatment determination or disposition under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a> or chapter 260E; or</p>
          <p>(3) the individual did not request a hearing of the maltreatment determination or disposition under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.29.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Conclusive disqualification determination.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A disqualification is conclusive for purposes of current and future background studies if:</p>
          <p>(1) the commissioner has issued a final order in an appeal of the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.08#stat.245A.08.5">245A.08, subdivision 5</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.28#stat.245C.28.3">245C.28, subdivision 3</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>, or a court has issued a final decision;</p>
          <p>(2) the individual did not request reconsideration of the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.21">245C.21</a> on the basis that the information relied upon to disqualify the individual was incorrect; or</p>
          <p>(3) the individual did not timely request a hearing on the disqualification under this chapter, chapter 14, or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a> after previously being given the right to do so.</p>
          <p>(b) When a licensing action under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.05">245A.05</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.06">245A.06</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.07">245A.07</a> is based on the disqualification of an individual in connection with a license to provide family child care, foster care for children in the provider's own home, or foster care services for adults in the provider's own home, that disqualification shall be conclusive for purposes of the licensing action if a request for reconsideration was not submitted within 30 calendar days of the individual's receipt of the notice of disqualification.</p>
          <p>(c) If a disqualification is conclusive under this section, the individual has a right to request reconsideration on the risk of harm under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.21">245C.21</a> unless the commissioner is barred from setting aside the disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.24">245C.24</a>. The commissioner's decision regarding the risk of harm shall be the final agency decision and is not subject to a hearing under this chapter, chapter 14, or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/256.045">256.045</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.29.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.29.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/288/#laws.1.74.0">2004 c 288 art 1 s 74</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/228/#laws.5.10.0">2014 c 228 art 5 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.8.60.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 8 s 60</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.30">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.30 VARIANCE FOR A DISQUALIFIED INDIVIDUAL.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.30.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">License holder and license-exempt child care center certification holder variance.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except for any disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15#stat.245C.15.1">245C.15, subdivision 1</a>, when the commissioner has not set aside a background study subject's disqualification, and there are conditions under which the disqualified individual may provide direct contact services or have access to people receiving services that minimize the risk of harm to people receiving services, the commissioner may grant a time-limited variance to a license holder or license-exempt child care center certification holder.</p>
          <p>(b) The variance shall state the reason for the disqualification, the services that may be provided by the disqualified individual, and the conditions with which the license holder, license-exempt child care center certification holder, or applicant must comply for the variance to remain in effect.</p>
          <p>(c) Except for programs licensed to provide family child care, foster care for children in the provider's own home, or foster care or day care services for adults in the provider's own home, the variance must be requested by the license holder or license-exempt child care center certification holder.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.30.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.47.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 47</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.30.1b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.<span class="headnote">Child foster care variances.</span></h2>
          <p>For an individual seeking a child foster care license who is a relative of the child, the commissioner shall consider the importance of maintaining the child's relationship with relatives as an additional significant factor in determining whether the individual should be granted a variance.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.30.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Disclosure of reason for disqualification.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may not grant a variance for a disqualified individual unless the applicant, license-exempt child care center certification holder, or license holder requests the variance and the disqualified individual provides written consent for the commissioner to disclose to the applicant, license-exempt child care center certification holder, or license holder the reason for the disqualification.</p>
          <p>(b) This subdivision does not apply to programs licensed to provide family child care for children, foster care for children in the provider's own home, or foster care or day care services for adults in the provider's own home.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.30.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Consequences for failing to comply with conditions of variance.</span></h2>
          <p>When a license holder or license-exempt child care center certification holder permits a disqualified individual to provide any services for which the subject is disqualified without complying with the conditions of the variance, the commissioner may terminate the variance effective immediately and subject the license holder to a licensing action under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.06">245A.06 </a>and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.07">245A.07</a> or a license-exempt child care center certification holder to an action under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142C.06">142C.06</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142C.07">142C.07</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.30.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Termination of a variance.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may terminate a variance for a disqualified individual at any time for cause.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.30.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Final decision.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner's decision to grant or deny a variance is final and not subject to appeal under the provisions of chapter 14.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.30.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.30.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/4/#laws.1.43.0">1Sp2005 c 4 art 1 s 43</a>,44; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/6/#laws.16.42.0">1Sp2017 c 6 art 16 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/9/#laws.2.79.0">1Sp2019 c 9 art 2 s 79</a>-81; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.65.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 65</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.44.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 44</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/80/#laws.3.3.0">2024 c 80 art 3 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.62.28.0">2024 c 127 art 62 s 28</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.245C.301"><b>245C.301</b> MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.17.63.0">2023 c 70 art 17 s 63</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.31">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.31 INDIVIDUAL REGULATED BY A HEALTH-RELATED LICENSING BOARD; DISQUALIFICATION BASED ON MALTREATMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.31.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Board determines disciplinary or corrective action.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall notify a health-related licensing board as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.01">214.01</a>, subdivision 2, if the commissioner determines that an individual who is licensed by the health-related licensing board and who is included on the board's roster list provided in accordance with subdivision 3a is responsible for substantiated maltreatment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a> or chapter 260E, in accordance with subdivision 2. Except as provided in paragraph (b), the health-related licensing board shall make a determination as to whether to impose disciplinary or corrective action under chapter 214, rather than the commissioner making the decision regarding disqualification.</p>
          <p>(b) The prohibition on disqualification in paragraph (a) does not apply to a background study of an individual regulated by a health-related licensing board if the individual's study is related to child foster care, adult foster care, or family child care licensure.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.31.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Commissioner's notice to board.</span></h2>
          <p> The commissioner shall notify a health-related licensing board:</p>
          <p>(1) of a record showing that the individual licensed by the board was determined to have been responsible for substantiated maltreatment;</p>
          <p>(2) upon the commissioner's completion of an investigation that determined an individual licensed by the board was responsible for substantiated maltreatment; or</p>
          <p>(3) upon receipt from another agency of a finding of substantiated maltreatment for which an individual licensed by the board was responsible.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.31.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Commissioner's or local agency's referral to board.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) When the commissioner or a local agency has reason to believe that the direct contact services provided by an individual may fall within the jurisdiction of a health-related licensing board, the commissioner or local agency shall refer the matter to the board as provided in this section.</p>
          <p>(b) If, upon review of the information provided by the commissioner, a health-related licensing board informs the commissioner that the board does not have jurisdiction to take disciplinary or corrective action, the commissioner shall make the appropriate disqualification decision regarding the individual as otherwise provided in this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.31.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Agreements with health-related licensing boards.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner and each health-related licensing board shall enter into an agreement in order for each board to provide the commissioner with a daily roster list of individuals who have a license issued by the board in active status. The list must include for each licensed individual: the individual's name, aliases, date of birth, and license number; the date the license was issued; status of the license; and the last four digits of the individual's Social Security number.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.31.3b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3b.<span class="headnote">Maltreatment study; fees.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The administrative service unit for the health-related licensing boards shall apportion between the health-related licensing boards that are required to submit a daily roster list in accordance with subdivision 3a an amount to be paid through an additional fee collected by each board in accordance with paragraph (b). The amount apportioned to each health-related licensing board must equal the board's share of the annual appropriation from the state government special revenue fund to the commissioner of human services to conduct the maltreatment studies on licensees who are listed on the daily roster lists and to comply with the notification requirement under subdivision 2. Each board's apportioned share must be based on the number of licensees that each health-related licensing board licenses as a percentage of the total number of licensees licensed collectively by all health-related licensing boards.</p>
          <p>(b) Each health-related licensing board may collect an additional fee from a licensee at the time the initial license fee is collected to compensate for the amount apportioned to each board by the administrative services unit. If an additional fee is collected by the health-related licensing board under this paragraph, the fee must be deposited in the state government special revenue fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.31.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Facility monitoring.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner has the authority to monitor the facility's compliance with any requirements that the health-related licensing board places on regulated individuals practicing in a facility either during the period pending a final decision on a disciplinary or corrective action or as a result of a disciplinary or corrective action. The commissioner has the authority to order the immediate removal of a regulated individual from direct contact or access when a board issues an order of temporary suspension based on a determination that the regulated individual poses an immediate risk of harm to persons receiving services in a licensed facility.</p>
          <p>(b) A facility that allows a regulated individual to provide direct contact services while not complying with the requirements imposed by the health-related licensing board is subject to action by the commissioner as specified under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.06">245A.06</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245A.07">245A.07</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner shall notify a health-related licensing board immediately upon receipt of knowledge of a facility's or individual's noncompliance with requirements the board placed on a facility or upon an individual regulated by the board.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.31.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.31.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.8.61.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 8 s 61</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.1.63.0">2022 c 98 art 1 s 63</a>-66; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.17.32.0">2023 c 70 art 17 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.66.13.0">2024 c 127 art 66 s 13</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.32">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.32 SYSTEMS AND RECORDS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.32.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Establishment.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may establish systems and records to fulfill the requirements of this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.32.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">NETStudy 2.0 system.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall design, develop, and test the NETStudy 2.0 system and implement it no later than September 1, 2015.</p>
          <p>(b) The NETStudy 2.0 system developed and implemented by the commissioner shall incorporate and meet all applicable data security standards and policies required by the Federal Bureau of Investigation (FBI), Department of Public Safety, Bureau of Criminal Apprehension, and the Department of Information Technology Services. The system shall meet all required standards for encryption of data at the database level as well as encryption of data that travels electronically among agencies initiating background studies, the commissioner's authorized fingerprint collection vendor or vendors, the commissioner, the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension, and in cases involving national criminal record checks, the FBI.</p>
          <p>(c) The data system developed and implemented by the commissioner shall incorporate a system of data security that allows the commissioner to control access to the data field level by the commissioner's employees. The commissioner shall establish that employees have access to the minimum amount of private data on any individual as is necessary to perform their duties under this chapter.</p>
          <p>(d) The commissioner shall oversee regular quality and compliance audits of the authorized fingerprint collection vendor or vendors.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.32.1b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.<span class="headnote">Civil remedies.</span></h2>
          <p>When accessing private data on individuals through NETStudy 2.0, entities that are authorized to initiate background studies and the commissioner's authorized fingerprint collection vendors shall be subject to all responsibilities and civil remedies applicable to a responsible authority or government entity as specified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.08">13.08</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.32.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Use.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner may also use these systems and records to obtain and provide criminal history data from the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension, criminal history data held by the commissioner, and data about substantiated maltreatment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.557">626.557</a> or chapter 260E, for other purposes, provided that:</p>
          <p>(1) the background study is specifically authorized in statute; or</p>
          <p>(2) the request is made with the informed consent of the subject of the study as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.05#stat.13.05.4">13.05, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) An individual making a request under paragraph (a), clause (2), must agree in writing not to disclose the data to any other individual without the consent of the subject of the data.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner may use these systems to share background study documentation electronically with entities and individuals who are the subject of a background study.</p>
          <p>(d) The commissioner may recover the cost of obtaining and providing background study data by charging the individual or entity requesting the study a fee as described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.10">245C.10</a>. The fees collected under this paragraph are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of conducting background studies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.32.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">National records search.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) When specifically required by statute, the commissioner shall also obtain criminal history data from the National Criminal Records Repository.</p>
          <p>(b) To obtain criminal history data from the National Criminal Records Repository, the commissioner shall require classifiable fingerprints of the data subject and must submit these fingerprint requests through the Bureau of Criminal Apprehension.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner may require the background study subject to submit fingerprint images electronically. The commissioner may not require electronic fingerprint images until the electronic recording and transfer system is available for noncriminal justice purposes and the necessary equipment is in use in the law enforcement agency in the background study subject's local community.</p>
          <p>(d) The commissioner may recover the cost of obtaining and providing criminal history data from the National Criminal Records Repository by charging the individual or entity requesting the study a fee of no more than $30 per study. The fees collected under this subdivision are appropriated to the commissioner for the purpose of obtaining criminal history data from the National Criminal Records Repository.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.32.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/12/#laws.2.4.0">2003 c 12 art 2 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/15/#laws.1.32.0">2003 c 15 art 1 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/4/#laws.5.9.0">1Sp2005 c 4 art 5 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/86/#laws.2.1.0">2013 c 86 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/250/#laws.0.19.0">2014 c 250 s 19</a>,20; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/2/#laws.1.13.0">1Sp2020 c 2 art 1 s 13</a>; art 8 s 62; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/0/31/#laws.2.16.0">2021 c 31 art 2 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/7/#laws.2.66.0">1Sp2021 c 7 art 2 s 66</a>,67; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.7.45.0">2023 c 70 art 7 s 45</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.33">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.33 ADOPTION AND TRANSFER OF PERMANENT LEGAL AND PHYSICAL CUSTODY BACKGROUND STUDY REQUIREMENTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.33.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Background study requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Before placement of a child for purposes of adoption, the commissioner shall conduct a background study on individuals listed in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/259.41#stat.259.41.3">259.41, subdivision 3</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.611">260C.611</a>, for county agencies and private agencies licensed to place children for adoption. When a prospective adoptive parent is seeking to adopt a child who is currently placed in the prospective adoptive parent's home and is under the guardianship of the commissioner according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.325#stat.260C.325.1">260C.325, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), and the prospective adoptive parent holds a child foster care license, a new background study is not required when:</p>
          <p>(1) a background study was completed on persons required to be studied under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03</a> in connection with the application for child foster care licensure after July 1, 2007;</p>
          <p>(2) the background study included a review of the information in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08">245C.08</a>, subdivisions 1, 3, and 4; and</p>
          <p>(3) as a result of the background study, the individual was either not disqualified or, if disqualified, the disqualification was set aside under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>, or a variance was issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.30">245C.30</a>.</p>
