          <p>(b) Before the kinship placement agreement is signed for the purpose of transferring permanent legal and physical custody to a relative under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.503">260C.503</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/260C.515">260C.515</a>, the commissioner shall conduct a background study on each person age 13 or older living in the home. When a prospective relative custodian has a child foster care license, a new background study is not required when:</p>
          <p>(1) a background study was completed on persons required to be studied under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.03">245C.03</a> in connection with the application for child foster care licensure after July 1, 2007;</p>
          <p>(2) the background study included a review of the information in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08">245C.08</a>, subdivisions 1, 3, and 4; and</p>
          <p>(3) as a result of the background study, the individual was either not disqualified or, if disqualified, the disqualification was set aside under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.22">245C.22</a>, or a variance was issued under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.30">245C.30</a>. The commissioner and the county agency shall expedite any request for a set-aside or variance for a background study required under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142A.60">142A.60</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142A.612">142A.612</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.33.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Information and data provided to county or private agency.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The subject of the background study shall provide the information specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05">245C.05</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.33.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Information and data provided to commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The county or private agency shall forward the data collected under subdivision 2 to the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.33.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Information commissioner reviews.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The commissioner shall review the following information regarding the background study subject:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the information under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08">245C.08, subdivisions 1</a>, 3, and 4;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) information from the child abuse and neglect registry for any state in which the subject has resided for the past five years; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) information from national crime information databases, when required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08">245C.08</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The commissioner shall provide any information collected under this subdivision to the county or private agency that initiated the background study. The commissioner shall also provide the agency with a notice whether the information collected shows that the subject of the background study has a conviction listed in United States Code, title 42, section 671(a)(20)(A).</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.33.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.3.22.0">2007 c 147 art 3 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/269/#laws.2.1.0">2010 c 269 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.25.5.0">2014 c 312 art 25 s 5</a>,6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.17.33.0">2023 c 70 art 17 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/80/#laws.1.96.0">2024 c 80 art 1 s 96</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/115/#laws.16.34.0">2024 c 115 art 16 s 34</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.245C.34">
        <h1 class="shn">245C.34 ADOPTION AND CHILD FOSTER CARE BACKGROUND STUDIES; TRIBAL ORGANIZATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.34.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Background studies may be conducted by commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Tribal organizations may contract with the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.12">245C.12</a> to obtain background study data on individuals under tribal jurisdiction related to adoptions.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Tribal organizations may contract with the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.12">245C.12</a> to obtain background study data on individuals under tribal jurisdiction related to child foster care.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Background studies initiated by tribal organizations under paragraphs (a) and (b) must be conducted as provided in subdivisions 2 and 3.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.34.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Information and data provided to tribal organization.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The background study subject must provide the information specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.05">245C.05</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.34.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Information and data provided to commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The tribal organization shall forward the data collected under subdivision 2 to the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.245C.34.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Information commissioner reviews.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The commissioner shall review the following information regarding the background study subject:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the information under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08">245C.08, subdivisions 1</a>, 3, and 4;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) information from the child abuse and neglect registry for any state in which the subject has resided for the past five years; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) information from national crime information databases, when required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.08">245C.08</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The commissioner shall provide any information collected under this subdivision to the tribal organization that initiated the background study. The commissioner shall indicate if the information collected shows that the subject of the background study has a conviction listed in United States Code, title 42, section 671(a)(20)(A).</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.245C.34.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/147/#laws.3.23.0">2007 c 147 art 3 s 23</a>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
